This is a task-selected reference, not an always-loaded rail. Root and local
AGENTS.md govern agent workflows. Domain contracts below remain binding for their

# mailserver/internal — Go Gateway Packages (DOX child)

Parent: `mailserver/AGENTS.md` → `AGENTS.md` (root).

## Purpose

The packages under `mailserver/internal/` form the stateless Go gateway: a thin protocol translator that accepts SMTP, IMAP, JMAP, CalDAV, CardDAV, LDAP, and HTTP connections, enforces auth and policy, and delegates all business logic to PL/pgSQL reducers in PostgreSQL. This directory is the full HTTP/TCP service surface of the mailserver binary (`mailserver/cmd/mailserverd/`).

## Ownership

This doc owns: per-package contracts, the complete protocol and feature inventory for every package here, and the HTTP route surface registered in `main.go`.

Does NOT own: reducer/schema logic (`mailserver/sql/AGENTS.md`), webmail frontend (`mailserver/webmail/AGENTS.md`), shared cross-cutting packages (`shared/AGENTS.md`), or the auth server (`authserver/AGENTS.md`).

Auth proxying is a thin pass-through: `/auth/*` plus an exact root-level OIDC Provider/OAuth allowlist (discovery, keys, authorize + callback, token + introspection, userinfo, end-session, and revocation) is forwarded to `authserver` via `internal/authproxy`. Do not replace exact `/oauth/token` and `/oauth/introspect` with a broad `/oauth/` proxy. Unknown `/oauth/*` and `/authorize/*` children terminate in explicit gateway 404 sinks rather than reaching authserver or the SPA. The auth server owns JWT issuance, TOTP, WebAuthn, SAML, OIDC, and the threat pipeline; this package just mounts the proxy and enforces JWT Bearer on downstream routes.

## Local Contracts

### Architecture invariant

All business logic lives in PL/pgSQL reducers. No Go package in this directory makes multi-step data decisions; each handler calls one or a small number of reducer functions and translates the result to protocol wire format. State token bumps, `pg_notify` pushes, and data changes are atomic inside the reducer transaction.

### Credential-reset and push-revocation seams

- `admin.UsersHandler.ResetPassword` may do an early privacy lookup and password-policy/hash work, but the committing call is exactly one `admin_reset_password` reducer invocation. That reducer acquires the V172 authority-topology fence before the person/target locks, requires a live persisted assignment whose same row owns `users.password_reset` plus current domain/OU/set reach (so a stale JWT cannot survive assignment/JIT revocation), refuses an in-scope Guest only after that no-oracle decision, and owns password mutation plus complete all-session revocation in one transaction. BZ060/BZ061 remain indistinguishable HTTP 404s; an in-scope Guest remains a 409.
- `auth.Chain.AuthenticatePushToken` is the single WS/SSE token-acceptance seam. It accepts only unscoped access tokens with `iat`; missing `iat` fails closed because runtime all-session revocation is ordered against that timestamp.
- `ws.Handler` checks `SessionDenylist` before WS/SSE success, registers the established transport in the process-wide `threat.ProtocolSessionRegistry` before subscriptions, and checks the denylist again. The registry closes matching established WS/SSE transports on all/JTI/family NOTIFY or reconcile events and closes a late registration that raced the event.
- Connectivity-aware sync E3 derives a five-minute, one-blob `mailserver-mail-attachment-1` credential only from a normal native access session at `POST /api/mail/attachment-transfer-token`. Its HS256 key is HKDF-domain-separated from a gateway-only `JWT_MAIL_ATTACHMENT_SECRET` (default: `JWT_SECRET` for compatibility); the dedicated verifier accepts only the current derived key plus optional `JWT_MAIL_ATTACHMENT_PREVIOUS_SECRET` during a bounded rolling rotation, while neither attachment master can verify normal auth tokens. The bearer deliberately omits `cnf` like the existing native main-process bridge: its five-minute lifetime, signed device identity, live session/device/policy checks, exact route, and one-blob binding are containment, not proof-of-possession. `GET /mail-attachment/{sha256}` is its only scoped surface; every request rechecks the origin JTI/family revocation rows, exact live device, effective-policy generation, feature flags, and current blob ownership before `http.ServeContent` handles identity-coded Range/If-Range bytes. Feature or winning-mode OFF is hidden as 404; other policy-generation drift is 409; ownership loss is 404. Tokens never enter URLs or checkpoints. `internal/auth/{jwt,chain}.go`, `internal/jmap/{attachment_transfer,content_serve}.go`.

### Key files

| File | Role |
|---|---|
| `mailserver/cmd/mailserverd/main.go` | Entry point — startup order + all route registrations |
| `mailserver/internal/config/config.go` | Env/YAML-driven `Config` struct; `LoadWithKoanf` for hot reload |
| `mailserver/internal/middleware/auth.go` | `RequireAuth` (authn-only + DomainScope — the SOLE auth gate), `RequirePermAudited` / `RequireAnyPermAudited` (granular perm enforcement, audit-logged), `RequireApp` (app-entitlement gate). `RequireAdmin`/`RequireRole`/`RequireSuperAdmin`/`RequireAuditor`/`RequireSplitPermAudited` were removed in the RBAC cut-over. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/handler.go` | JMAP envelope + dispatch + shared infra ONLY: wire types (`BatchRequest`/`MethodCall`/`Response`/`BatchResult`/`Error`/`ProblemDetails`), the `JMAPHandler` struct/ctor, and **the sole `ExecuteBatch` execution envelope**. `ExecuteBatch` uniformly owns non-empty `using`, max 32 calls, max 2,000,000 argument bytes per call, ordered ResultReference resolution/continuation, `createdIds`, and registry-derived typed invocation metadata; HTTP and WebSocket only decode and encode. The c4 method registry remains the ONE `map[string]methodEntry{fn, capability, app, gate}` table that drives capability, entitlement, and **#13 shared-mailbox METHOD GATE** policy (`personal` fail-closed | `read` | `mutate` | `submit` | `collaborate`). `resolveInvocation` snapshots that row into typed `invocationMetadata`; `dispatchInvocation` carries it in an `invocationContext` field, never a private context key, and `parseAccountID` defaults absent metadata to `personal`. Every one of the 111 rows is pinned by `registry_test.go` × `testdata/methods.golden` and exercised through the envelope by `TestExecuteBatchCarriesAllRegistryInvocationMetadata`. `parseAccountID` still admits a DELEGATED accountId only through `identity.ValidateAccountID` plus uncached, `dbscope.RunUserScoped` delegate grants; grant-less → `accountNotFound`, insufficient level → `forbidden`, lifecycle suspension → `forbidden`, and the admitted id flows unchanged into `CallScoped(accountID)`. Reducers/RLS remain business authority. `Session`, state-token helpers, and `dispatchSet`/`mergeSetResults` remain in this file; per-family method bodies remain siblings. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/parse.go` | The c4 position-aware argument codec: `methodError(typ, desc)` (the one constructor every method-level error pair routes through — 103 former inline `json.Marshal(Error{…})`+return pairs), `parseID(id) (int64, ok)` (per-record id; ids are positive BIGSERIALs), `parseArg(id, property)` (scalar id → `invalidArguments` naming the property), `parseIDList(ids) (valid, bad)` (all-malformed → non-nil EMPTY slice so reducers get `'{}'`, never the NULL fetch-all/FOREACH-error sentinel; absent stays nil → get-all), and the response folders `addNotFound(result, bad)` / `addSetErrors(result, key, bad)` (malformed ids land per-record in `notFound` / `notDestroyed`/`notRecovered`/`notPurged` as `{"type":"notFound"}` — byte-identical passthrough when `bad` is empty). Kills the 15 silent-drop ParseInt loops; `calendarEventFreeBusy`/`queryFreeSlots` instead REJECT a malformed `userIds[i]` (never silent partial availability). Tests: `parse_test.go`. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/statecodec.go` | The ONE owner of the JMAP state-token WIRE representation (c1). `encodeStateToken`/`decodeStateToken` (per-domain base-10 counter — byte-identical to the reducers' `counter::TEXT`), `encodeSessionState`/`decodeSessionState` (the 8-part dash-joined RFC 8620 §2 composite), `stateUnavailable` (error-path sentinel: error paths must NEVER emit `"0"`, a valid fresh-mailbox token), and `sinceStateError` (the `*/changes` sinceState gate: an undecodable token → `cannotCalculateChanges` instead of a reducer 22P02 → serverFail). Reducers still own the counters and render their own oldState/newState (logic-where-the-data-is); Go-side producers (`GetSessionState`, `scanStateTokens`, `satelliteStateForMailbox`, `identityState`, the constant Blob/Reminder states) all route through it, and `internal/ws` pushes the produced tokens verbatim. Golden-pinned against Postgres in `statecodec_test.go` — the offline SPA persists sinceState, so an encode change needs a client migration plan. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/mail.go` | JMAP Mail family handlers (RFC 8621): `Mailbox/*`, `Email/*`, `EmailSubmission/*`, `Thread/*`, `SearchSnippet/get`. Also the snooze-extension helpers (`splitSnoozeUpdates`/`parseSnoozeUntil`/`mergeSnoozeResults`, `VendorCapability`) and the recover/purge `emailBatchOp` used only by the mail surface. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/mail_smime.go` | Phase-D S/MIME **signing** pass for `EmailSubmission/set` (the sign-only half; the combined sign+encrypt orchestration lives in `mail_encrypt.go`): `signSubmissions` (per-creation resolve-cert-by-From-match → fetch draft blob → sign → store signed blob → record the swap key), `signOneSubmission` (draft load R1-scoped incl. `has_attachments`, cert resolution, S3 fetch/sign/store, `classifyDraftLoadErr` fail-closed on transient errors; **W0/#4 assemble-then-sign:** a draft WITH attachments is signed over its ASSEMBLED multipart/mixed wire via `buildSMIMEWire` — never the body-only draft, and an attachment-load failure fails CLOSED), `loadSignCandidates` (R1 cert load + `pgp_sym_decrypt`), `fetchBlob`, `rawFromRef` (**W5 raw_ref convention seam, shared with the encrypt path**: a draft's `raw_ref` is EITHER the inline RFC 5322 text — reducer-built drafts — OR a MinIO key; resolution dispatches on `blob.IsInlineMessage`, never on attachment presence. Pre-fix, every no-attachment inline draft was GetObject'ed and sign/encrypt failed closed. Oracle: `TestSMIMEOracleInlineRawRefSign`, the 10th `smime_attach_oracle_test.go` cell), plus pure helpers `stripSignSMIME`/`mergeNotCreated`/`smimeNotCreatedDescription` (the latter now also maps the three `smime_encrypt_*` reasons). The reducer swap seam is `p_processed_raw_ref` (renamed from `p_signed_raw_ref`, V027) + `p_is_encrypted` (V027). The outbound worker no longer signs OR encrypts (V011/D3 + SP1 Phase 3). |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/mail_smime_wire.go` | **`buildSMIMEWire` — the ONE owner of the outbound submission byte-pipeline order** (W0/#4 fix): assemble (multipart/mixed when attachment parts exist) → sign (inner, over the assembled ENTITY so the inner `multipart/mixed` Content-Type rides INSIDE the signed part) → encrypt (outer envelope). `signOneSubmission` and `poolEncryptOps.SignAndEncrypt` route their byte handling through it, so the assemble-then-sign ordering cannot diverge per caller again (the old shape signed/enveloped the body-only draft and the tail assembler skipped already-processed creations — signed/encrypted sends silently shipped WITHOUT their attachments). Verified by the openssl crypto-oracle matrix `smime_attach_oracle_test.go`: {sign, encrypt, sign+encrypt} × {regular attachment, inline-cid, none} = 9 cells, attachment bytes asserted BYTE-IDENTICAL in the `openssl smime -verify`/`-decrypt` output, plus a signed-wire-never-smaller-than-the-attachment regression. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/mail_attach.go` + `attachment_mime.go` | Outbound attachment wire assembly for `EmailSubmission/set`: `assembleAttachmentSubmission` (draft+parts load → `assembleMixedMIME` → content-addressed store → swap key for the plain, non-S/MIME path), `loadDraftAttachmentParts` (message_attachments rows + MinIO blob bytes — SHARED with the S/MIME paths), and the pure assembler `assembleMixedParts(rawDraft, parts) (top, entity, err)` — the draft's headers minus content headers vs. the multipart/mixed entity; full wire = `top + "MIME-Version: 1.0\r\n" + entity` (`assembleMixedMIME`). **Contract (inline-cid #2 rides this):** every part — regular or `ContentID`/inline — is carried in the entity, and the entity is what gets signed/encrypted, so parts survive ALL S/MIME modes; part filenames are MIME-injection-escaped via `mime.FormatMediaType`. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/identity.go` | JMAP `Identity/*` handlers (get/changes/set) — a distinct RFC surface, kept separate from mail. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/calendar.go` | JMAP Calendar family core: `Calendar/*` and `CalendarEvent/*` (get/query/changes/set/freeBusy/queryFreeSlots/rsvp), plus the iMIP invite serialization + resource-booking side-effect helpers used only by the calendar create path. iMIP invite and COUNTER enqueue pass recipient slices directly to PostgreSQL's `outbound_queue.rcpt_addrs text[]`; JSON-encoding that slice produces an invalid PostgreSQL array literal and silently strands the best-effort mail leg. A3 parity handlers live in `jmap/calendar_a3.go` (sharing, shared reads, occurrence edit, delegate list, resource list, send update). |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/contacts.go` | JMAP Contacts family: `AddressBook/*`, `ContactCard/*` (get/query/changes/set/galSearch), directory browse (`directoryTree`/`directoryMembers`/`directorySearch`). `ContactGroup/*` lives in `jmap/contact_group.go`. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/tasks.go` | JMAP `Task/*` handlers (get/query/changes/set) + the `TaskComment/*` activity-feed extension (get/set). Personal-view split (Phase 1, V112): the unfiltered listing excludes list-owned tasks. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/task_lists.go` | Shared/team task lists (Phase 1, plan `2026-07-08-shared-team-task-lists.md`): `TaskList/get\|set`, `TaskListMember/set`, `TaskList/tasks\|taskSet`, `TaskAssigned/get\|setStatus`. **User-scoped** (`claims.UserID`), NOT `parseAccountID`/`CallScoped` — the reducers take `p_user_id` and enforce list membership in every statement (R1), called on `h.workerPool` (the cross-mailbox BYPASS posture `identity.go`/`calendar_a3.go` use). Gated by `feature.shared_task_lists` (seeded OFF, `shared/toggle`), surfaced as `sharedLists` in the session's `urn:ietf:params:jmap:tasks` capability blob so the webmail needs no extra round trip. `TaskList/set`/`TaskListMember/set` hand-roll their create/update/destroy fan-out in Go (one reducer call per record — `task_list_create`/`_update`/`_delete` are single-object, not the vectorized `jmap_<x>_set_*` shape `dispatchSet` expects); `TaskList/taskSet` is a single reducer call (`task_list_task_set` does its own per-key JSONB loop in SQL, mirroring `jmap_task_set_*`). |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/mailtips.go` | The three non-JMAP HTTP endpoints on `JMAPHandler` wired directly in `main.go`: `HandleReadReceipt` (POST `/auth/me/read-receipt`, RFC 3798 MDN), `HandleMailTips` (GET `/auth/me/mailtips`), and `HandleDlpCheck` (POST `/auth/me/dlp-check` — compose-time DLP dry-run of `dlp_evaluate_draft`; `!feature.dlp` short-circuits to `{violated:false}` with no DB call; a reducer failure fails OPEN to no-tip, logged — the advisory tip must never 500 the compose screen; send-time enforcement, not this handler, is the hard gate). |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/resultref.go` | The RFC 8620 §3.7 ResultReference evaluator. `resolveReferences(args, priorResponses)` rewrites each `#argName` by finding the first prior response with that call id + matching name and applying the JSON Pointer (`*` map/flatten included). Malformed/missing/errored/name-mismatched/non-resolving references → `invalidResultReference`; `foo`+`#foo` → `invalidArguments`. It is pure and package-private: only `ExecuteBatch` invokes it, so transports cannot recreate the loop. Pure tables live in `resultref_test.go`; envelope continuation/order lives in `execution_test.go` and the HTTP/WS corpus. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/caller.go` | The `reducerCaller` seam: `Call(ctx, fn, args…)` runs `SELECT fn($1,…)` and scans the single JSON column. `poolCaller` wraps the pool (production); `fakeCaller` (test) records `(fn,args)` + replays a canned `json.RawMessage` or a synthetic `*pgconn.PgError{Code:"BZ0xx"}`, making the gateway's translate→reduce→error-map middle unit-testable with no Postgres. |
| `mailserver/internal/smtp/inbound.go` | MTA on port 25: feature-toggle-gated pipeline stages |
| `mailserver/internal/smtp/submission.go` | MSA on port 587: submission auth + threat interceptor |
| `mailserver/internal/smtp/outbound.go` | Outbound worker: DKIM signing, DANE, MTA-STS. **S/MIME signing AND encryption are both re-homed to the `EmailSubmission/set` handler** (Phase D + SP1 Phase 3); the worker relays the final (signed and/or encrypted) blob VERBATIM — it no longer signs or encrypts (a source-level machine guard `smtp/smime_encrypt_gate_test.go` forbids `encryptSMIME`/`smime.Encrypt`/`smime_encrypt` in this file). **W5 raw_ref convention:** `loadRawForDelivery` dispatches on `blob.IsInlineMessage` — an `outbound_queue.raw_ref` that IS the inline RFC 5322 text (reducer-built draft sends: `jmap_email.sql` create → `send.sql` enqueues it verbatim) is delivered directly; only key-shaped refs are GetObject'ed (pre-fix EVERY inline-draft external send deferred until it bounced). An empty raw_ref fails the row cleanly (the old combined guard nil-deref'ed and panicked the delivery goroutine). |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/mail_encrypt.go` | **SP1 Phase 3 encrypt-at-submission.** `processSubmissions` = the combined sign+encrypt pass `EmailSubmission/set` now calls (sign-only creations still go through `signSubmissions`). For each `encryptSMIME` creation it resolves the sender's encrypt-to-self cert + every recipient cert (collect-all-misses) + (when `journaling_required_for_domain`) the journal cert, builds the dedup'd set (`buildEncryptCertSet`), signs-inner/encrypts-outer + stores the ciphertext, and feeds `processedRefs`+`isEncrypted`. **W0/#4:** `poolEncryptOps.SignAndEncrypt` loads `has_attachments` and routes through `buildSMIMEWire` (`mail_smime_wire.go`) so a draft WITH attachments is enveloped over its ASSEMBLED multipart/mixed wire (sign-inner over the assembled entity when requested) — never the body-only draft; attachment-load failure fails CLOSED. **FAIL-CLOSED:** any missing recipient/sender/journal cert (or encrypt error, or nil infra) → per-creation `notCreated` (`smime_encrypt_no_cert`/`_no_sender_cert`/`_no_journal_cert`) + DROP, never plaintext. **SP1 Phase 4** threads the offending recipient addresses out of `encryptOneSubmission` into the no-cert `notCreated` entry's `addresses` field (`encryptNotCreated(reason, addresses)`) so the compose UX names EVERY missing recipient (D-F2); the sender/journal markers carry no list. `smimeEncryptOps` is the DB/S3 seam (pool-backed `poolEncryptOps`; stubbed in tests). `stripSMIMEFlags` removes both flags before the reducer. |
| `mailserver/internal/jmap/mail_decrypt.go` | **SP1 Phase 5 decrypt-on-read** (the read side of S/MIME encryption — webmail/JMAP only). `decryptEmailResult` runs AFTER `jmap_email_get`: for each `cryptoType=='smime-encrypted'` email it loads the reader's keystore keys (`loadDecryptKeys`, R1-scoped, `pgp_sym_decrypt(private_key_enc::bytea, …)`), fetches the `raw_ref` EnvelopedData blob, `smime.DecryptMulti`-tries ALL keys, and injects **transient** `bodyText`/`preview` + a `decryptState` (`decrypted`/`no_key`/`wrong_key`/`error`). **Plaintext is NEVER persisted; a failed decrypt is a calm read-state, never a 5xx.** Each success is audited (`email_decrypt_read`). Cost-bounded per user (`decryptAllowed`, `middleware.NewRateLimiter(120, time.Minute)`) — a denied op serves the encrypted read-state, never plaintext. `parseDecryptCandidate` (pure) skips bad-PEM/non-RSA rows. |
| `mailserver/internal/imap/imap.go` | IMAP4rev2 bridge server. **SP1 P5 (C3): IMAP serves encrypted blobs VERBATIM — never server-decrypts** (the IMAP client decrypts with its own key). `handleFetch` streams `raw_ref` as-is; SEARCH `body_text ILIKE` can't match encrypted mail (its `body_text` is NULL). Locked by `imap_ciphertext_test.go`: a source scan (`TestIMAP_NoDecryptImported`) forbids any `smime.Decrypt`/`DecryptMulti`/`DecryptKey`/`DecodeEnvelopedDER` reference in the package, and a DB-gated test proves SEARCH excludes the encrypted body while the retained subject still matches. |
| `mailserver/internal/cron/tasks.go` | All scheduled cron tasks (drain, scheduled-actions dispatcher + reminder scan, retention, purge, partitions) |
| `mailserver/internal/scim/server.go` | SCIM 2.0 route registration; exported production mounting and package-local inventory tests both replay the same private `registerRoutes(router)` authority |
| `mailserver/internal/mdm/engine.go` | MDM check-in engine — a thin caller of the `mdm_device_checkin` reducer (compliance eval + grace + wipe FSM + UPSERT run atomically in one txn); `RequestWipe`/`ListDevices`/`ResolvePolicy` stay in Go |
| `mailserver/internal/admin/admin_mam.go` | MAM admin list projects the stable numeric `device_registry.id` used by MAM policy/event actions, then LEFT JOINs `mdm_device_state` on `(device_fingerprint, user_id)`; nullable MDM rows fall back to registry timestamps/unknown display fields. Route middleware remains the sole `mam.read`/`mam.write` authorization boundary. |

### Startup order (mandatory, enforced in `main.go`)

1. Config load
2. DB pool open (PgBouncer, `DB_DSN`)
3. S3/SeaweedFS client init
4. Recovery: `startup.EnsureMonthlyPartitions` runs on the owner pool; then `startup.RunDatabaseRecovery` invokes the cross-tenant `startup_recovery()` reducer on the typed worker/BYPASSRLS pool before any listener or worker starts and queues the bounded admin recovery report on that same pool; then `startup.AbortStaleMultiparts` handles object-store residue. Recovery failure refuses startup rather than accepting work with stranded `sending` rows. `sql/tests/startup_recovery.pg` pins the reducer repair and the real Ubuntu D3 lane pins the process wiring.
5. `startup.InitWORM` + `startup.BootstrapSecrets`
6. LISTEN/NOTIFY dispatcher (`db/listen.go` compatibility alias over `shared/pgnotify`, direct `DB_DIRECT_DSN`)
7. Auth chain construction
8. TCP listeners (SMTP :25, submission :587, IMAP :993/143)
9. Background workers: `eapjobs.NewWorker(workerPool, logger).Run(ctx)` (EAP bulk-apply) + `bulkroles.NewWorker(workerPool, logger).Run(ctx)` (bulk-role-assignment) + `notifyjobs.NewWorker(workerPool, logger).Run(ctx)` (NOTIFY-decouple drain, V088 — inert while `feature.notify_decouple` is OFF) + one configured `pushjobs.NewWorker(workerPool, pushSet, logger)` (mail push + unconditional chat push + Guest G1 digest drains; `SetGuestDigestConfig` receives the same worker pool's S3/sender settings before `Run`) + `drive.NewWorker(workerPool, drive.MinioStore{Client: minioClient}, cfg.S3Bucket, logger).Run(ctx)` (Drive P1 maintenance: chunk GC sweep + `tmp/upload/` reaper, V114/spec §3.5 — runs unconditionally like `pushjobs`; `feature.drive` only gates the route layer) + `drive.NewPreviewWorker(workerPool, store, bucket, cmd, logger).Run(ctx)` (Drive P3 Task 14: office→PDF renditions via the `DRIVE_PREVIEW_CMD` sidecar; inert — no claim, no work — when the env var is unset) + `cron.RunUserRiskCron(ctx, workerPool, featureToggles, logger)` (15-min user-risk scorer, gated `feature.user_risk`, DEFAULT OFF) — all on `workerPool` (BYPASSRLS); all exit on ctx cancel
10. HTTP/2 server

### DB connection model

Two connections always: PgBouncer pool for queries; a single direct `pgx` connection for `LISTEN` (PgBouncer transaction mode breaks async notification).

**Native-RLS pool split (W1b/W4, INERT until W6).** A second `workerPool` (`setjoin_worker`, BYPASSRLS) carries the **cross-tenant** paths that the future `messages`/`message_tags` FORCE'd RLS must not police: inbound SMTP **delivery** (`smtp.NewInboundServer(workerPool, …)` + TLS variant — `deliver()` is cross-tenant by nature; this also covers the SP1 **S/MIME signer-cert harvest** UPSERT into `recipient_smime_certs`, whose `owner_user_id` is resolved per-recipient inside `deliver()`, not from a tenant GUC — an intentional cross-tenant carve-out on `c.srv.pool`, satisfying R2 since the owner comes from the resolved receiving mailbox, never `req.*`), SMTP **submission** (`smtp.NewSubmissionServer(workerPool, …)` — `send()` fans out local-recipient `deliver()` into other mailboxes), JMAP **`EmailSubmission/set`** (routed through `JMAPHandler.workerCaller` — a `poolCaller{workerPool}` seam, plain `Call`, NO tenant GUC), the **welcome email** (`UsersHandler.workerPool` → `notify.SendWelcomeEmail`), the **outbound drain** (`smtp.NewOutboundWorker(workerPool, …)`), the **admin delivery-trace per-recipient read** (`admin.NewTraceHandler(pool, workerPool, …)` — the Phase-2b cross-mailbox `delivery_attempts` drill-down; its `message_trace`/`outbound_queue` reads stay on the app `pool`), the **spam trainer** (`spam.NewPGModelStore(workerPool, minioClient, S3Bucket)` — background per-user batch worker; reads raw message blobs straight from object storage by `raw_ref`, like the outbound worker — it must NOT round-trip the JWT-gated `/jmap/download` HTTP endpoint), and the cron worker (DSN bounce / health-check `send()`/`deliver()`). The same worker credential is load-bearing for R4 committed-mail publication: SMTP wrappers, JMAP submission, approval replay and scheduled fire call the worker-only publication reducer; `setjoin_app` has no EXECUTE grant and cannot forge a historical message fact. SMTP submission scans the nullable `send()` result, so accepted-and-held mail returns SMTP 250 but emits neither durable nor legacy `mail.sent`; dispatch remains after commit only. `workerPool` **aliases** the app pool while `DB_WORKER_DSN` is unset, so this is INERT today. `EmailSubmission/get` + `/cancel` stay on the tenant-scoped `CallScoped` seam (sender-scoped, not cross-tenant). **CalDAV/CardDAV are NOT routed onto `workerPool`** — see the caldav/carddav note below.

**Native-RLS coverage (W7, INERT until the DSN switch to `setjoin_app`).** Three tenant-scope seams set the transaction-scoped GUC `app.current_mailbox_id` so a wrapped `messages`/`message_tags` statement is scoped to the authenticated mailbox once RLS is FORCE'd (W6/V004): jmap `poolCaller.CallScoped` (`jmap/caller.go`, reducer calls), imap `connState.withScope` (`imap/imap.go`, per-IMAP-command), and the shared **`shared/dbscope.RunScoped(ctx, pool, mailboxID, fn)`** (`Begin → set_config → fn(tx) → Commit`, moved from `mailserver/internal/dbscope` by arch #A8) for the raw-pool packages with no caller seam of their own (used by `user/quarantine.go` Release/Reject after an owner read resolves the per-message mailbox). The guard **`scripts/check-rls-coverage.sh`** enumerates EVERY app-pool `messages`/`message_tags` SQL literal in this tree (non-test) and FAILS any not classified in `scripts/rls-coverage-allowlist.txt` as `WRAPPED` (inside a CallScoped/withScope/RunScoped tx) · `BYPASS` (handler constructed on `workerPool`) · `AUDITED-XTENANT` (intentionally cross-tenant on the bypass pool). `check-rls-coverage-selftest.sh` proves the guard fires + is precise; both run selftest-first in `test.sh`/`lint.sh`. **MULTI-MAILBOX reads** (scoped by `mb.user_id` across a user's mailboxes — `blob/handler.go`, `jmap/blob.go` Blob/*, `user/{raw_email,newsletter}.go`, `user/quarantine.go` List, recall) cannot fit one mailbox GUC, so they run on `workerPool` (the admin-case treatment) — `jmap.JMAPHandler` gained a raw `workerPool` field (distinct from the reducer-only `workerCaller`) for its Blob/* ownership reads. The DSN-switch deploy gate's precondition is **a green coverage guard**.

### Package index

Route inventory contract: Drive, Chat, Meet, and SCIM production construction
and their package-local evidence tests call the same private
`registerRoutes(router)` authority. The shared `testutil.RouteRecorder` validates
real `ServeMux` patterns/conflicts and atomically emits one owner artifact; no
source regex or test-only route list is authoritative.

| Package | Key role |
|---|---|
| `continuity` | U5 dedicated Ed25519 continuity key loader/service. Issues and verifies compact account/installation/timeline/state/boot witnesses and exact raw-MIME/blob recovery receipts whose `received`/`sent-terminal` eligibility is inside the signature. Missing key keeps OFF-gated methods unavailable; it never falls back to JWT or release trust. The operator-configured private-key path must be non-empty, clean, and absolute before any read. |
| `admin` Exchange assessment slice | `admin_exchange_assessment.go` is the thin seven-permission V1A REST translator over reducer authority. Bundle uploads are 64 MiB bounded, digest-bound, encrypted under per-artifact random DEKs and installation AAD, and use unique object keys plus exact replay lookup to prevent random-ciphertext overwrite races. `exchange_deletion_worker.go` drains already-authorized requests even while the feature is OFF, removes active-store objects, signs deletion receipts through the rotating continuity key, and records only the honest `backup_expiry_pending` disposition. Signed publication receipts are server-created and encrypted; callers never provide a trusted receipt artifact ID. |
| `mailrecovery` | U7 thin HTTP/quarantine translator. Strict/capped JSON and range input, current user/delegate checks, exact request-to-signed eligibility equality, ClamAV fail-closed scan, content-addressed exact-byte assembly, and idempotent chunk cleanup wrap reducer authority. The reducer additionally serializes cumulative device/mailbox/incident/installation upload admission; it cannot ingest a message without the verified incident/artifact token. Admin approve/close use two compile-time fixed reducer queries; reducer names are never interpolated into SQL. Fresh semantic-AAL2 acceptance delegates to `shared/jwtutil.VerifyFreshAAL2` with the explicit shared `CurrentFreshAAL2Policy()`; only the incident-specific digest remains local. |
| `acme` | ACME DNS-01 certificate automation via `go-acme/lego`; multi-provider DNS |
| `ad` | Active Directory sync engine: LDAPS paged searches, `uSNChanged` delta cursor, conflict resolution. Legacy sync runs write the current `identity_sync_log` columns (`users_*`, `fields_skipped`, `duration_ms`, nullable `error`) rather than the retired pre-collapse names. **Address writes are Go-canonical at the seam** (AD/LDAP-writer-canonical + validation-boundary waves): the create path routes through `identity.ProvisionAccount` and the UPDATE path through `ldaputil.SyncAttrs` — a raw `u.Email`/`u.UPN` fails to compile, so stored addresses match the canonicalizing matcher by construction. `ProvisionAccountParams.Email` is now `identity.ValidatedAddress`; AD is a trusted directory source, so the create path lifts its canonical address via the validation-FREE `identity.CanonicalizeAddress(u.Email).AssumeValidated()` (NOT `ValidateAndCanonicalizeEmail` — a directory `mail` value may not pass RFC-5321), staying validation-free exactly as before. `ad_upn` stays `CanonicalAddress`. `krb5_principal` keeps the RAW UPN (case-sensitive Kerberos realm). |
| `admin` | Admin REST API handlers (~70 files): users, domains, mailboxes, rules, DLP, quarantine, eDiscovery, audit, trace, system, deploy, identity, migration, spam, TLS, updates, webhooks, departments, S/MIME, MDM, MAM, branding, alerts, roles, conditional access, **offline governance** (`admin_offline.go` — `OfflineHandler` serving `GET /admin/offline/policies`, `GET/PUT /admin/offline/policies/{id}`, `DELETE /admin/offline/policies/{id}`, `GET /admin/offline/devices/pending`, `POST /admin/offline/devices/{id}/approve`, `POST /admin/offline/devices/{id}/deny`, `POST /admin/offline/devices/{id}/revoke`, **`POST /admin/offline/devices/{id}/wipe`** (offline #2b — `WipeDevice`; gated `mam.write`; calls `offline_device_revoke` to set `offline_approval='revoked'` then broadcasts a `capsChanged` WS event so the device receives its revocation immediately)); policy CRUD gated on `mail.offline_policy`; device-queue reads gated on `mam.read`, writes on `mam.write`), **access reviews** (`access_reviews.go` — `AccessReviewsHandler`: `NewAccessReviewsHandler(pool, logger)`, `List`/`Get`/`Start`/`Decide`/`DecideBulk`/`Close` — six thin translators over the four `access_review_*` reducers; all 6 routes are `roles.manage`-gated and domain-scope-confined per-campaign; BZ060→404 (not found: campaign or item), BZ061→403 (out of scope), BZ062→409 (campaign closed), BZ063→409 (SSO-revoke blocked); empty-name validation is Go-side (HTTP 400), no BZ code), **等保三级 SoD** (`sod.go` — `SodMode`/`DeriveSodClass`; `sod_handler.go` — the `GET`/`PUT /admin/dengbao/sod-mode` enablement gate: precheck + flip in ONE tx, refuses ON on any `admin_sod_precheck` violation, audited). `admin_roles.go` carries the Go SoD mutual-exclusion pre-check (principal-locked, ON-only — UX layer over the authoritative DB `trg_sod_exclusion` trigger) + `ValidateRoleClass` on role create/update. **OU/set scope:** `AssignMember` accepts optional `scopeType`/`scopeId` fields on the request; validates `scopeType ∈ {nil,"ou","set"}` (group/user → 400 "group/user scope is retired; use a scope-set"; ou+nil scopeId → 400; nil+non-nil scopeId → 400). For `scopeType="set"` the handler also `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` locks the `admin_scope_sets` row (Rule A atomic check — the scope-set cannot change under the grant write). Before INSERT calls `admin_scope_within(actor, scopeType, scopeId, nil)` — any object-confined actor granting a broader-than-self scope gets 403. Persists `scope_type`/`scope_id` to `admin_role_assignments` (V039/V041 columns). The role-members response also returns `setName` (joined from `admin_scope_sets` on `scope_type='set'`) so the UI can display the set's name even if the set is later renamed. `BulkAssign` accepts the same optional `scopeType`/`scopeId` (V040/V041): validates `∈ {nil,"ou","set"}` + paired non-nil constraint; persists `scope_type/scope_id` to `bulk_role_jobs`. The reducer (`bulk_role_job_apply`) passes the job's real scope to `admin_scope_within` for the job-level Rule A check and stamps it onto every per-target `admin_role_assignments` INSERT/UPSERT. Revoke carries no scope. **Scope-sets CRUD** (`admin_scope_sets.go` — `ScopeSetsHandler`, `NewScopeSetsHandler(pool, logger)`): `ListScopeSets` (`GET /admin/scope-sets`), `GetScopeSet` (`GET /admin/scope-sets/{id}` — returns `{set, members}`), `CreateScopeSet` (`POST /admin/scope-sets` — 409 on duplicate name), `UpdateScopeSet` (`PUT /admin/scope-sets/{id}`), `DeleteScopeSet` (`DELETE /admin/scope-sets/{id}` — 409 if any grant references the set, counts live grants in the error message), `AddMember` (`POST /admin/scope-sets/{id}/members` — per-type validation: `ou` via `admin_scope_within`, `user` via `admin_can_reach`, `group` must be non-dynamic AND eligible (`scim_external_id IS NOT NULL OR usable_as_admin_scope`) AND all static members reachable by actor; idempotent on existing members), `RemoveMember` (`DELETE /admin/scope-sets/{id}/members/{memberType}/{memberId}`). All mutations audited at tier core. `ListDistributionGroups` (`GET /admin/distribution-groups`): returns all distribution groups with eligibility flags (`isDynamic`, `scimSynced`, `usableAsAdminScope`, `memberCount`) for the scope-set member picker UI. All scope-sets routes are gated by `requireScopeSetsManage` (a `RequirePermAudited(permissions.PermScopeSetsManage, …)` middleware — `scope_sets.manage`, Super-only seed; delegation lands with spec §11). **Reach-coverage guard** (`scripts/check-reach-coverage.sh` + `scripts/reach-coverage-allowlist.txt`): scans every non-test `.go` file in the admin package for functions that hold a caller-supplied target `userId`/`memberId` and verifies each either calls a reach-check reducer (`admin_can_reach`, `admin_can_manage`, `admin_protection_check`, `admin_scope_within`) or is classified in the allowlist (`WRAPPED`/`SUPER-ONLY`/`DOMAIN-ONLY`/`READ-ONLY`). Three previously-BLOCKED handlers are now **WRAPPED**: `admin_spam.go` `ResetModel`/`DisableModel`/`EnableModel` (add `admin_can_reach` before `user_spam_model` mutations), `admin_departments.go` `AssignUser` (add `admin_can_reach` before `users.department_id` UPDATE), `admin_ad.go` `SetFieldPolicy` (add `admin_can_reach` before `field_policy` INSERT/DELETE). Guard runs selftest-first in `scripts/ci-local.sh` (alongside `check-rls-coverage`, `check-openapi-coverage`, `check-openapi-visibility`). **P3 bulk role:** `admin_roles.go` also exposes `RolesHandler.BulkAssign` (`POST /admin/roles/{id}/members:bulk`, `roles.manage`-gated) and `BulkRevoke` (`DELETE /admin/roles/{id}/members:bulk`), each validating Rule A + cap (≤1000 userIds) + past-`expiresAt` then inserting a `bulk_role_jobs` row and returning `202 {jobId}`. `admin/bulk_role_jobs.go` exposes `BulkRoleJobsHandler`: `JobDetail` (`GET /admin/jobs/bulk_role/{id}`, `queue.read`-gated) returns the full job row incl. `results`; `RetryJob` (`POST /admin/jobs/bulk_role/{id}/retry`, `queue.write`-gated) re-queues an `error` job keeping `processed`/`results` (resumes, never restarts). `admin_jobs.go` `knownQueues` map gains `"bulk_role": true`. `consoles.go` defines the console permission matrix (ConsoleID, ConsoleScopes, ConsolesForPerms, ConsoleHasAccess, RequireAnyPerm) over the 30 RBAC permissions (`mail.offline_policy` is system-console-scoped; the CA `access.policy_*` perms are auth-console-scoped). **`config_store.go`** is the `system_config` write seam shared by the AD/LDAP/TLS SetConfig handlers: `ConfigStore{GetByPrefix/GetKey/SetKey/BatchSet}` over `ConfigValue{Value json.RawMessage, Encrypted bool}` is the single writer that persists the authoritative **`encrypted` column** per-key (secret = the `_enc` suffix convention via `configValuesFromRaw`), so the config-backup export/import contract round-trips the secret marker (the old `batchUpsertSystemConfig` omitted the column — retained only for the non-secret alerts/antiphish writers). A `SideEffectRegistry{prefix→hook(ctx,key,old,new)}` fires after each write; `main.go` registers `ad_sync_`/`ldap_sync_` → a background directory `SyncNow` (own context, async — the HTTP write never blocks). TLS `GetConfig` redacts secret `tls_*` rows via `redactConfigValue` (was returning every value raw). `admin_mam.go` is unaffected — it operates on `device_policy` tables, not `system_config`. **`admin_users.go` `Create` + `ImportCSV`** mint the provisioning address via `identity.ValidateAndCanonicalizeEmail` (the validation-boundary wave): it both validates AND canonicalizes in one step, producing the `identity.ValidatedAddress` `ProvisionAccount.Email` now demands — so the separate `validate.ValidateEmail` call was removed from `Create`, and `ImportCSV` gained per-row email validation (malformed rows rejected, partial-success preserved). `admin_mailboxes.go` `Create` is NOT a `ProvisionAccount` caller (raw secondary-mailbox INSERT) and keeps its own `validate.ValidateEmail`. **Admin-protection guard** (`admin_protection.go` + the 5 handler call-sites): `mapProtectionErr(pool, logger, w, r, err, actorID, targetID) bool` maps the three BZ06x protection codes — BZ064→409 Conflict (last Super Admin), BZ065/BZ066→403 Forbidden (outranked / self-act) — emits a `'admin_protection.denied'` audit row on the shared pool in autocommit (survives the mutation tx rollback), and writes the HTTP problem. The 5 handler call-sites each open a transaction, call **`SELECT admin_can_reach($actor,$target)`** (the OU-scope enforcement primitive — TRUE iff the actor's domain + OU reach covers the target) before the mutating statement; an unreachable target returns 403. Then `SELECT admin_protection_check($actor,$target,$op,$roleGroupId)` before the mutating statement; roll back on any error (the guard and the mutation are atomic). **OU-axis Rule B:** `admin_users.go` — `Update` (suspend, op=`suspend`), `Delete` (op=`delete`), `ResetPassword` (op=`password_reset`); `admin_roles.go` — `AssignMember` (op=`assign_role`, passes role id), `RemoveMember` (op=`remove_role`, passes role id). All five call `admin_can_reach` first. `GET /admin/users` exposes per-row `manageable bool` + `is_self bool` flags (a lateral WITH query keyed on the actor's `claims.UserID` + the `admin_effective_authority` broadest-reach computation **plus an `admin_can_reach` clause on the per-user lateral so an OU-confined actor cannot see unconfined targets as manageable**); the role-members endpoint (`RoleMembersPanel`) exposes a per-member `manageable bool` flag computed the same way. **`audit_policy.go`** (`AuditPolicyHandler`): `GET /admin/audit/policy` (`audit.read`-gated via `requireLogsPerm`) returns the singleton `audit_policy` row as `{level, reasonRequiredAreas}`; `PUT /admin/audit/policy` (`audit.export`-gated + `RequireReason`) validates `level` ∈ `{lean,full}`, reads the old row for the before/after audit diff, updates the singleton (actor from claims, never from body), and writes a core `audit_policy_change` audit row via `audit.Write`. A policy change always requires a non-empty `reason` in the JSON body (beyond the `RequireReason` middleware — belt-and-suspenders). **JIT activation** (`admin_jit.go` — `JITHandler{pool, logger, toggles}`, `NewJITHandler(pool, logger, toggles)`): `Activate` (`POST /admin/me/roles/{id}:activate`, `requireAuth` self-service; body `{durationMinutes, justification?}` → reducer `admin_role_activate($actor,$role,$dur,$just)` → `{activatedUntil, tokenRefreshRequired?}`; **BZ104**→400 JIT off or no eligible assignment; **BZ105**→400 justification required; D6 cold-start: token `IsAdmin=false` ⇒ response includes `tokenRefreshRequired:true` so webmail refreshes session before re-deriving consoles); `Deactivate` (`POST /admin/me/roles/{id}:deactivate`, `requireAuth` self-service; nulls `activated_until`, audited); `ListMyRoles` (`GET /admin/me/roles`, `requireAuth` self-service; returns `[{roleGroupId, roleName, eligible, activated, activatedUntil}]` for the calling user); `GetActivationPolicy` (`GET /admin/roles/{id}/activation-policy`, `requireRolesManage`-gated); `PutActivationPolicy` (`PUT /admin/roles/{id}/activation-policy`, `requireRolesManage`-gated; sets per-role `max_duration_minutes`, `require_justification`, `auto_deactivate_minutes`, `allowed_activation_hours`); `GetJITDefaults` (`GET /admin/system/jit-defaults`, `requireRolesManage`-gated); `PutJITDefaults` (`PUT /admin/system/jit-defaults`, `requireRolesManage`-gated). **`eligible` grant flag**: `AssignMember` (`POST /admin/roles/{id}/members`) + `BulkAssign` accept optional `eligible bool` (default false); an eligible grant is dormant — the admin cannot exercise authority until they call `Activate`. `BulkAssign` propagates `eligible` through `bulk_role_jobs.eligible` (V043). **Coverage guard** (`scripts/check-jit-coverage.sh`): scans every non-test Go file in the admin package for `admin_role_assignments` queries with an `expires_at`-only filter (missing the `activated_until`/`admin_assignment_active` guard) and verifies each either calls `admin_assignment_active` or is in the allowlist. **CA-3b** (`admin_terms_of_use.go`): `ToUHandler` — Terms-of-Use doc CRUD (`GET/POST /admin/terms-of-use`, `PUT/DELETE /admin/terms-of-use/{id}`); `RiskHandler` — user-risk dashboard (`GET /admin/user-risk` list with remediated rows filtered out, `POST /admin/user-risk/{id}/reset`). Both gated `access.policy_read`/`access.policy_write` (the CA-policy perms), writes audited. **Sharing console** (`admin_sharing.go` — `SharingHandler{pool, logger}`, `NewSharingHandler(workerPool.Pool, logger)` — W7 cross-tenant read surface, BYPASSRLS-inert until DB_WORKER_DSN; spec 2026-06-27 §7): `ListDefaults` `GET /admin/sharing/defaults` (labels resolved; D7 domain filter for org/group/user rows; OU rows have no domain axis and are always listed), `CreateDefault` `POST /admin/sharing/defaults` (upsert via `sharing_default_set`; `sharing.manage` + `requireReason`; reducer re-enforces the ladder + domain scope BZ116 and validates BZ117), `DeleteDefault` `DELETE /admin/sharing/defaults/{id}` (`sharing_default_clear`, BZ114→404), `UserOversight` `GET /admin/sharing/users/{id}` (per-user delegates + grantees + applicable defaults; domain scope folded into the resolving SELECT — out-of-scope user is an indistinguishable 404), `Audit` `GET /admin/sharing/audit` (the four sharing `audit_write` actions). Reads gated `sharing.read` (or `sharing.manage`); tests `admin_sharing_test.go`. **`admin_approvals.go`** (`ApprovalsHandler` — approval-policy CRUD console, Task 13/spec §11: `ListPolicies`/`CreatePolicy`/`UpdatePolicy`/`DeletePolicy`/`ReorderPolicies` `/admin/approval-policies*`, `ListAudit` `GET /admin/approval-requests/audit`; `approvals.manage`/`approvals.view`-gated): `validConsumerTypes` — the Go-side mirror of the `approval_policies`/`approval_requests` CHECK constraint that every write/list path checks before touching the DB — gained `hr.leave`/`hr.expense` (V110); without it the admin console's new Leave/Expense tabs 400 "invalid consumer_type" on every call despite the DB CHECK happily allowing the rows. **`admin_chat_compliance.go`** (`ChatComplianceHandler{pool, workerPool, flags, logger}`, `NewChatComplianceHandler` — Chat A4a Tasks 11a/11b, `chat.compliance_view`/`chat.compliance_manage`-gated via `requireViewClaims`/`requireManageClaims`; `gate` 404s the whole handler when `feature.chat_compliance` is OFF): `ListPolicies`/`CreatePolicy`/`UpdatePolicy` (the N4/FIX-3 re-arm — `moreDestructive` bool computed from the old-vs-new row, `effective_at = CASE WHEN moreDestructive THEN GREATEST(effective_at, now()+7d) ELSE effective_at END` in the same UPDATE)/`DeletePolicy` (FIX-3 exposure guard — `exposedChannelCount` compares the retention resolution over the surviving vs current policy set; refuses `409 {code:"exposure", exposedChannels}` without `?confirm=DESTROY`) at `/admin/chat-retention-policies[/{id}]`; `ListChannels` (`/admin/chat-channels?q=&userId=` — the participant-projecting list, a DM/group-DM row renders "Alice ↔ Bob", never a blank name; since A4b Task 8 the hold column, the Overview holds tile, and the purge preview all project `chat_hold_active(c.id) AS legal_hold` — the two-arm predicate, never the raw boolean; `scripts/check-chat-hold-truth.sh` fails any raw `legal_hold` read outside the arm's own plumbing), `SetHold` (`/admin/chat-channels/{id}/hold` — the boolean-arm per-channel hold; the A4a `SetCustodianHold`/`POST /admin/chat-holds/custodian` snapshot-loop route was DELETED in A4b Task 5, superseded by `ChatEDiscoveryHandler.SetCaseHold` below), `SetClassification` (`/admin/chat-channels/{id}/classification` — regular→gxp only), `SetRetention` (`/admin/chat-channels/{id}/retention` — the per-channel override, immediate, no grace); `PreviewPurge` (`/admin/chat-purge/preview`, dry-run count, `nextChatPurgeRunAt` computes the next `02:50` slot for the Overview tile); `Overview` (`/admin/chat-compliance/overview` — holds/gxp-count/override-count/last purge run totals+`drained`+timestamp/next-run+preview, one workerPool round-trip); `GetAudit`/`writeAuditCSV` (`/admin/chat-channels/{id}/audit[?format=csv]` — recent acts + a keepable CSV evidence export) and `VerifyAudit`/`chainSummary` (`/admin/chat-channels/{id}/audit/verify` → `verify_chat_audit_chain` + the notary cross-check, the three-outcome verdict: PASS / CHAIN BROKEN incl. TRUNCATION DETECTED / RECORD ALTERED). All routes registered in `main.go` behind `requireChatComplianceView`/`requireChatComplianceManage` (`middleware.RequireAnyPermAudited`/`RequirePermAudited`, the approvals-console shape). **`admin_chat_ediscovery.go` + `admin_chat_ediscovery_export.go`** (`ChatEDiscoveryHandler{pool, workerPool, flags, logger, minio, bucket}`, `NewChatEDiscoveryHandler` — Chat A4b Tasks 5/6/7, split from `admin_chat_compliance.go` one-handler-per-authority-plane, the mail `admin_ediscovery.go`/`admin_ediscovery_cases.go` precedent; same per-request toggle `gate`): **holds** (`chat.compliance_manage`/`_view`) — `SetCaseHold` `POST /admin/chat-ediscovery/holds` (custodian scope carries the `admin_can_reach` OU-scope check, the 6267355c fix replicated; calls `chat_case_hold_set`), `ReleaseCaseHold` `POST /admin/chat-ediscovery/holds/{id}/release`, `ListCaseHolds` `GET /admin/chat-ediscovery/holds`, `ListCases` `GET /admin/chat-ediscovery/cases` (exists because mail's own cases route is `requireMailPerm`-gated — a chat-compliance officer holding only Access-console perms cannot reach it); **reader/export** (`chat.ediscovery` via `requireChatEDiscovery` — the D-A SoD split, NOT satisfied by `chat.compliance_manage`) — `Search` `POST /admin/chat-ediscovery/search`, `ReadMessages` `GET /admin/chat-ediscovery/channels/{id}/messages` (record-floor data on the envelope), `Export` `GET /admin/chat-ediscovery/export` (streamed ZIP inside ONE REPEATABLE READ tx: `messages.jsonl` canonical rows, attachment bytes by `content_sha256` through the Drive chunk manifest on workerPool — never the member content route, `manifest.json` LAST with snapshot-consistent counts + `messages.jsonl` sha256 + chain heads + the verbatim L8 `disclaimers` block). All V127 tables are FORCE-RLS deny-all → every read runs on workerPool with the W6/W7 carve-out comments; routes ledgered in `openapi-pending.txt`; full contract `internal/chat/AGENTS.md` "Oversight (A4b)". **`admin_meet_recordings.go`** (`MeetRecordingsHandler`, Meet B2 Task 8 — `GET /admin/meet/recordings?state=&meetingId=`): the org-wide recordings registry read, gated on the EXISTING `drive.view` permission (plan delta 3 — no new permission minted; recordings are Drive-governed content and `drive.view` already means "read Drive governance administratively"). Runs on `workerPool.Pool` (BYPASSRLS carve-out — `meeting_recordings` is FORCE-RLS attendee-scoped, and an invoker-rights query would only ever show the admin their OWN attended meetings, defeating the point of an eDiscovery-adjacent registry). Deliberately carries NO `feature.meet_recording` toggle gate (D10: a toggle gates new intent — starting a recording — never the visibility of already-committed state; the registry row exists from `requested` onward). AUDITED read (`meet_recordings_list`, tier core) — browsing the org's recording registry is itself surveillance-relevant metadata. **Webmail surface (whole-pipeline review UX #11):** consumed as-is (no Go change) by `mailserver/webmail/src/routes/admin/(access)/meet-recordings/+page.svelte` — a minimal read-only table, same `drive.view` gate; see `mailserver/webmail/AGENTS.md`'s Access console table (the consolidated review moved the drive.view surfaces to the Access console — `drive.view` rides `ConsoleScopes[ConsoleAccess]` for visibility, never `ConsoleSystem`, whose set also derives `requireSystemPerm`). |
| `approval` | User-facing approval-engine handlers. `Handler{pool, workerPool, logger}` (`NewHandler(pool, pgxutil.WorkerPool, logger)`) serves two `requireAuth` routes — `ListMyApprovals` (`GET /me/approvals`, the caller's pending-to-decide + own requests) and `Decide` (`POST /me/approvals/{id}/decide`, routes by `consumer_type`). **Pool split (native-RLS W4 class):** visibility (list + the `consumer_type` lookup) and the `rbac_approval_decide` call run inside `dbscope.RunUserScoped(claims.UserID)` → FORCE RLS on `approval_requests` (stranger probe → opaque 404); the two MAIL consumers' decide (`mail_external_approval_decide`, `group_moderation_approval_decide`) then runs on the **worker pool (BYPASSRLS), plain** — their reducers replay a cross-tenant leg (send() fan-out into recipients' mailboxes / group release deliver() into members' mailboxes / deny-NDR into the sender's mailbox) that a tenant GUC pinned to the approver fails closed under FORCE. Safe because `approval_decide` self-authorizes `p_actor` in SQL (non-approver → BZ107/403; SoD → BZ106/409; not-pending/already-decided → BZ108/409 — re-checked atomically, so the tx split is TOCTOU-free; policy misconfig → BZ109/422). Pinned by `routing_test.go`. `EmailPageHandler{pool, workerPool, hmacKey, logger}` (`NewEmailPageHandler(pool, workerPool, []byte(cfg.JWTSecret), logger)`) serves the **prefetch-safe** approve-by-email surface (unauthenticated, HMAC-token-gated): `ServeGET` (`GET /approvals/email/{token}`) renders the confirmation page and NEVER mutates (so scanner/prefetch can't auto-decide); `ServePOST` (`POST /approvals/email/{token}`) decides as the token's bound approver with the same pool split as `Decide`. Token = HMAC-SHA256 over `(request_id, approver_user_id, decision, exp)`, single-use via decide idempotency. **Approve-by-email notification fan-out:** `HandleApprovalCreatedNotify(ctx, pool, notifyPayload, secret, baseURL, logger)` is the handler for the `approval_created` pg_notify channel (subscribed once in `main.go`, fired by `approval_request_create` for every consumer) — it skips `rbac.jit_activation` (console-only, spec D7) and calls `SendApprovalNotification` for the two mail consumers; this is the ONE Go call site that reaches the `mail.external_send` hold, which has no request id otherwise (`send()` PERFORMs `approval_request_create` and RETURNs NULL). `SendApprovalNotification` resolves `approver_kind='role_group'` to every active `admin_role_assignments` member (not just `'user'`) and emails each — replacing a prior group-moderation-only direct call at the SMTP inbound seam (`internal/smtp/inbound.go` no longer has `SetApprovalNotify`/`approvalBaseURL`). **Domain scoping (V093 `approval_requests.domain_id`):** populated by `approval_request_create` from the requester's `users.domain_id` (or an explicit `p_domain_id` override — `group_moderation_hold` passes the target group's mailbox domain, since the external sender has no requester row); `admin/admin_approvals.go`'s `ListAudit` (`GET /admin/approval-requests/audit`) filters `WHERE domain_id = $N` for a domain-scoped admin (`middleware.DomainScopeFromContext`), mirroring `admin_users.go`. **HR leave/expense (V110, 4th+5th consumers — `retired-docs:superpowers/plans/archive/2026-07-08-hr-approval-templates.md`):** `SubmitHR` (`POST /me/approvals/hr`, `hr.go`) — toggle-gated 404 (`approval.hr_leave`/`approval.hr_expense`, D11; `Handler.SetFeatureFlags(*toggle.FeatureFlags)`, nil = no gating, same setter idiom as `admin_roles.go`/`jmap/handler.go` — avoided an 11-call-site `NewHandler` signature change) — calls `hr_request_create`; 400 on BZ124 (form)/BZ126 (missing receipt), 422 when no policy matched. `Decide`'s switch routes `hr.leave`/`hr.expense` to `hr_approval_decide` on the **tenant tx** (no cross-tenant mail leg, same lane as `rbac.jit_activation`; pinned by `routing_test.go`). `Cancel` (`POST /me/approvals/{id}/cancel`, engine-level, all five consumers) calls the new `approval_request_cancel` reducer — closes the spec §10.1 "cancel while pending" gap (the UI's Cancel button pre-existed and silently 400ed on every consumer until this shipped); BZ125 opaque 404. `approvalItem`/`scanApprovalItem` now include `payload` in the wire response **only** for `hr.leave`/`hr.expense` (their payload is the small user-facing form; every other consumer's payload — e.g. `mail.external_send`'s full `send()` replay args — stays withheld, wire-identical to before). Tests: `handler_test.go`, `email_page_test.go`, `routing_test.go`, `notify_test.go`, `hr_test.go`, `cancel_test.go`. |
| `audit` | Thin Go wrapper over the `audit_write` SQL choke point. `Write(ctx, q Execer, e Entry) error` — calls `audit_write` on the passed `Execer` (pgx.Tx or *pgxpool.Pool); row commits/rolls back with the caller's tx. `WriteAutonomous(ctx, pool, e Entry) error` — acquires its own pool connection + `context.Background()`, commits independently, SURVIVES a rolled-back caller tx (the `admin_protection.denied` invariant). `TierForPerm(perm) string` / `TierForAction(action) string` → `"core"`/`"routine"`; both reuse `shared/permissions.SensitivePermissions` + `ClassOf` — no parallel sensitivity list. `Execer` is the minimal `Exec(ctx,sql,args…) (pgconn.CommandTag, error)` interface satisfied by both `pgx.Tx` and `*pgxpool.Pool`. DB-backed tests (`audit_test.go`) prove the survive-rollback invariant against the real DB. **`coverage_guard_test.go`** enforces two structural invariants at test-time: (1) **Single INSERT path** — scans all `.go` and `.sql` files under `mailserver/internal` and `mailserver/sql/reducers`; fails if any file other than `audit_write.sql` contains `INSERT INTO admin_audit_log` (bypassing the choke point risks skipping tier validation, level gate, and the v2 chain trigger); (2) **Sensitive→core** — every perm in `SensitivePermissions` and every perm whose SoD class is `"security"` or `"audit"` must resolve to `"core"` via `TierForPerm` (a newly-added sensitive perm silently resolving to `"routine"` would be dropped at lean level). Non-vacuous sub-test plants a rogue INSERT and asserts the scanner fires. |
| `assist` | Loads AI assist provider config from `system_config` keys (`assist.provider_url`, `assist.model`, `assist.api_key`); exposes `LoadConfig(ctx, configReader) (shared/assist.Config, error)` and `ErrNotConfigured` sentinel; `configReader` interface keeps it DB-free in tests; production `NewPoolConfigReader(pool)` calls `get_system_config` via the pool. Also exposes `Handler` (B4.3): `POST /assist/complete` and `POST /assist/stream` (reserved/501). `ToggleAssistGate` wraps `*toggle.FeatureFlags` for the `feature.assist` gate. Note: `get_system_config` returns raw JSONB — the `encrypted` column is informational only; no pgp_sym_decrypt is called. Store `assist.api_key` as a plain JSON string. |
| `auth` | JWT/bcrypt/Kerberos/Basic-auth/**certificate** method implementations; priority-chain resolver. `certAuthMethod` (CA-3b, priority 5, `auth_method=certificate`) authenticates a request's mutual-TLS client cert BEFORE the Bearer token: `verifyClientCert` always checks chain + validity against the configured CA roots, and revocation when `crl_paths` is set in the method `config_json` (loaded by `loadCRLFiles`) — **fails closed** (no cert / bad chain / revoked → reject). **Guest Accounts G0 satellite belt** (`chain.go`): `Chain.SetGuestPool(pool)` wires the worker/BYPASSRLS pool the belt reads (nil unless the caller opts in — a bare unit-test `Chain` skips it, claim-only); `applyGuestBelt(ctx, claims)`, called from BOTH `AuthenticateRequest` and the push-seam `AuthenticatePushToken` (the WS/SSE paths validate their JWT directly and bypass the REST chain), is UNCONDITIONAL — a `guest_account_status()` hit forces `claims.AccountType = jwtutil.AccountTypeGuest` regardless of what the token itself claims, refusing via `ErrGuestInactive` on a disabled/expired satellite row or a guest-claiming token with no satellite row at all (fail-closed on the claim/row mismatch); a query error denies via `ErrGuestLookupUnavailable` (mapped to 503 by `guestgate.Middleware`, never silently let through). `bcrypt.go`'s `CheckPassword` is the ONE shared choke point for IMAP/SMTP-submission/DAV/LDAP Basic-auth: after the normal bcrypt verify it calls `is_guest_account()` and refuses outright — guests never had a Basic-auth login path to begin with, so this is a denial, not a re-derivation of the JWT belt above. |
| `authproxy` | Reverse-proxy `/auth/*` and the root-level OIDC Provider/OAuth protocol endpoints to authserver without path rewriting; preserves trusted forwarding and correlation headers |
| `backup` | Blob backup engine. A `backup_runs` NOTIFY drives `processRun`: CAS-claim (single-flight; the worker startup-drains stranded `pending` rows), snapshot live blobs (fatal on a partial-read cursor error), idempotent copy (HeadObject **size**-skip + post-write size check), emit the manifest set (incr/config/index + the `backup-manifest.json` commit-point latest pointer written last), async best-effort cross-region `replica` mirror, then **real GFS tiered retention** (`ApplyRetention`→`runsToPrune`/`parseGFS` prunes old runs' index objects — never blobs). The GFS counts are **tier depths, not a flat total**: a run is retained iff it is the newest run within any of the most-recent `daily` distinct calendar-days, `weekly` distinct ISO-weeks, or `monthly` distinct calendar-months (classified from its manifest timestamp `20060102T150405Z`); pruned only when in NO retained bucket, so an old monthly DR snapshot survives behind many newer runs. (An unparseable timestamp is retained conservatively. The predecessor's flat-sum `keep=daily+weekly+monthly` + `runs[keep:]` was a data-loss lie — it kept newest-N-total and silently deleted old monthlies.) `restore.Engine` (sibling pkg) is the read side. See `docs/design/archive/storage-ha-backup-restore.md` + `docs/design/mail/archive/mailctl-restore-lock-replication.md` |
| `blob` | Content-addressed blob HTTP handler (GET/PUT `/blobs/{sha256}`); bridges to SeaweedFS S3. **PUT is hash-validated + write-safe:** the SHA path param must match `^[0-9a-f]{64}$` (else 400), and the body is streamed to a RANDOM `tmp/upload/<rand>` key while hashing, verified `computed==sha`, then promoted to the canonical key via `CopyObject` (dedup no-op if it exists) — it NEVER `PutObject`/`RemoveObject`s the client-supplied canonical key, so a mismatched body can't destroy another tenant's cross-tenant-deduped blob (mirrors the safe `jmap/blob.go` Upload path). The storage calls go through a package-private `objectStore` interface (`*minio.Client` satisfies it) purely as the test seam for that invariant. Also home of **`IsInlineMessage(ref)` (`inline.go`) — THE raw_ref discriminator** (W5): inline RFC 5322 message text (contains a line break — reducer-built drafts) vs object key/sentinel (single-line). Every consumer that can legally receive both routes through it (`jmap.rawFromRef`, `smtp.loadRawForDelivery`). **`StartCleanup`'s 5-minute ticker also runs `processOrphanedBlobs`** — the drain for `orphaned_message_blobs` (every message-purge path — IMAP EXPUNGE/CLOSE, JMAP Email/purge, `purge_deleted_messages`, the S/MIME re-sign swap — queues an unshared `messages.raw_ref` there; nothing read the table before this, so the SeaweedFS objects it named were never reclaimed). Skips `IsInlineMessage` rows (never a real object — just drops the junk row) and, for real keys, re-checks `NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM messages WHERE raw_ref=$1)` on `h.workerPool` at drain time before calling `RemoveObject` (double-delete safety: some producers content-address `raw_ref`, so a new message can legitimately recreate a key an older purge queued). No cron/SQL equivalent exists or should — a PL/pgSQL function cannot call the SeaweedFS/MinIO HTTP API. |
| `booking` | Self-service booking pages (feature #6; `feature.booking_pages`, default OFF). `Handler{pool, flags, logger}` (`NewHandler`) serves the authed owner CRUD surface (`GET/PUT/DELETE /api/booking/pages`, `requireAuth`-wrapped) — resolves `identity.ResolvePrimaryMailbox`, then runs `booking_page_list/upsert/delete` inside mailbox-scoped `dbscope.RunScoped` transactions; the JSON upsert argument is explicitly cast to `jsonb`. The constrained-`setjoin_app` live test is load-bearing: an unscoped call is RLS-denied while all three real handler operations succeed. `PublicHandler{workerPool, flags, minio, sendInvite, logger}` (`NewPublicHandler`) is setjoin's **first deliberate unauthenticated public app surface**: `GET /api/public/booking/{slug}` (meta) · `GET .../slots` (busy-only `{start,end}` geometry, never event/attendee detail) · `POST .../book` (honeypot `website` field, mandatory double-opt-in email verification before any calendar event exists) · `POST .../confirm` (creates the event via `jmap_calendar_event_set_create`, sends the iTIP invite via the injected `jmap.EnqueueInvite`) · `POST .../cancel`. Mounted OUTSIDE `requireAuth` in `main.go` (like `/.well-known/*`) behind two dedicated `middleware.HybridRateLimiter`s (read/write) and the global `middleware.SecurityHeaders` CSP; toggle-off and unknown-slug are byte-identical 404s (enumeration parity). Runs on the **worker pool (BYPASSRLS)** — it has no per-request tenant identity to scope a GUC to, the same carve-out class as inbound delivery/journaling/admin. `Sweeper` (`sweeper.go`, `NewSweeper(workerPool, logger)`) ticks `booking_expire_due()` every 10 minutes (a standalone own-ticker worker — the approval-engine's expiry sweep this once mirrored, `approvaljobs`, is now a `cron.AllTasks()` row, arch review #A5). See `docs/adr/DES-007-public-booking-surface.md`. |
| `bulkroles` | **P3 bulk-role-assignment worker.** `NewWorker(workerPool, logger).Run(ctx)` — started once at boot on the `workerPool` (BYPASSRLS, cross-tenant; admin data). Startup: reclaims `running`→`queued` for jobs orphaned by a hard crash. Tick loop: `SELECT … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` claim → `status='running'` → `CALL bulk_role_job_apply(id)` (one-tx: claim + apply + finalize) → `status='done'` (or leaves `error` the reducer set) — rolling deploys never double-process (row is locked for the whole job). Exits cleanly on ctx cancel. Tests: `worker_test.go` (claim+apply+finalize in one tx → `done`; startup reclaim; SKIP LOCKED non-double-claim). Near-copy of `eapjobs.Worker` (same durable-job pattern). |
| `caldav` | CalDAV bridge (RFC 4791); free/busy batch; iTIP/iMIP invite dispatch; CalDAV WebDAV layer. Calls reducers through the shared `reducercall.Caller`; reducer errors map to the correct WebDAV status via `reducererr` (507/403/404/409), not a blanket 500. **RFC 6578 `sync-collection` (A4 wave 3):** `handleSyncCollection` serves the incremental collection sync — parses the DAV:sync-collection REPORT, `CallScoped`s `caldav_changes('CalendarEvent', calID, token)` on the calendar's owner mailbox, renders a 207 with one `<response>` per changed event (200 + getetag) / removed event (404) + a trailing `<DAV:sync-token>`; an invalid/pruned/future token renders the RFC 6578 `DAV:valid-sync-token` precondition (403 via `webdav.WriteValidSyncTokenError`). Token wire format `webdav.SyncTokenPrefix + <entity_state seq>`; advertised in `supported-report-set`. |
| `calendarsync` | **Webcal/ICS calendar-subscribe sync worker (WAVE-5 #10; V092 `calendar_subscriptions`).** `NewWorker(workerPool, logger).Run(ctx)` — jittered ~15-min ticker (started once in `main.go`) on `workerPool` (BYPASSRLS, cross-tenant due-poll); `claimDue` claims-by-write (`UPDATE…RETURNING`, `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`) so concurrent replicas never double-fetch one subscription within a refresh window. `fetch` follows redirects manually (up to 5 hops; `ssrf.ValidateURL` re-checked on every REDIRECT TARGET — the stored base URL is trusted from subscribe-time, a TOCTOU gap a feed's later redirect could otherwise open) over `ssrf.SafeHTTPClient` (the dial-time guard, authoritative against DNS rebinding, on every hop incl. 0); conditional GET (If-None-Match/If-Modified-Since) short-circuits an unchanged feed to a 304/no-op; `io.LimitReader` bounds the body to 5 MiB. Parses with the shared `migration.SplitAndParseICS`/`EventsToJSON` (same mapping `jmap/calendar_import.go` uses). `applyRefresh` calls the diff-by-uid `calendar_subscription_refresh` reducer + the status/etag bookkeeping UPDATE in ONE tx. `RefreshSubscriptionNow` is the exported bypass-the-tick entry point the REST subscribe handler (`internal/user/calendar_subscriptions.go`) calls, detached from the request, right after a new subscription row is created — so the user doesn't wait a full tick for their first sync. Tests: `worker_test.go` (fake-server matrix: 200/304/redirect-to-private-ip rejected/oversize/partial-parse/too-many-redirects; DB-backed claim+apply+diff test gated on `DB_DSN`). |
| `carddav` | CardDAV bridge (RFC 6352); vCard import/export; WebDAV base. Calls reducers through the shared `reducercall.Caller`; reducer errors map to the correct WebDAV status via `reducererr`, not a blanket 500. **RFC 6578 `sync-collection` (A4 wave 3):** `handleSyncCollection` mirrors the CalDAV path with datatype `'ContactCard'` over addressbooks — the removed-member/changed-member hrefs are `/{ab}/{vcard_uid}.vcf` (the contact's `vcard_uid` is its href token, carried in the journal `resource_uid`). |
| `chat` | **setjoin Chat A1** (channels, DMs, threaded messages; `feature.chat`/`toggle.FeatureChat`, DEFAULT OFF; V119; `retired-docs:superpowers/plans/archive/2026-07-11-chat-A1-core-messaging.md`). `Handler{pool, flags, logger, mux}` (`NewHandler`) — same single-gate + internal-mux shape as `drive`: `ServeHTTP` is the ONE gate every route passes through, per-request `flags.IsEnabled(toggle.FeatureChat)` (never cached in the constructor) then auth, dispatch delegated to the internal mux. `errors.go`'s `bzSlug` + `writeChatProblem` is the chat BZ160–BZ170→RFC7807 map — a thin `reducererr.Classify`/`Detail` consumer (no per-code status override needed, unlike drive's BZ148/BZ149 — every chat code's `reducererr.codeClass` entry already lands on the wire status Task 8's locked table wants: BZ160→400, BZ161/163/164/166/167→403, BZ162/165/168→422, BZ169/BZ170→404 anti-oracle). **Tracer routes (Task 9, `channels.go`+`messages.go`):** create/dm/conversations, send/list/since messages — the two proven bets (row-lock-serialized `change_seq` cursor, `chat:{uid}` self-scoped push) `cmd/mailserverd/main.go` mounts behind auth at `/chat/`. **Breadth routes (Task 10):** `channels.go` adds discovery/lifecycle/membership — `GET /chat/channels/browse` (public non-archived only, F1/grill-Q1), `POST /chat/channels/{id}/join` (BZ170 anti-oracle — private/archived/nonexistent all read identically), `PATCH /chat/channels/{id}` (rename/topic/archive, owner/admin), `POST /chat/channels/{id}/members` + `DELETE .../members/{userId}` (owner/admin, self-leave always allowed). `messages.go` adds `PATCH /chat/messages/{id}` (edit, author-only), `DELETE /chat/messages/{id}` (delete, author/owner/admin), `POST /chat/messages/{id}/reactions` (toggle, BZ163 non-member vs BZ169 message-gone). `presence.go` adds `POST /chat/channels/{id}/read` {seq}, `POST /chat/channels/{id}/typing`, and `GET /chat/presence?userIds=` — the last backed by `PresenceRegistry` (`Presence`, package-level singleton), a process-local in-memory per-user connect-COUNT (not a bool — multi-tab/device safe) with no DB and no push; `ws/handler.go`'s WS Upgrade and `ws/eventsource.go`'s SSE handler both `Connect`/`Disconnect` (deferred) it around their existing `chat:{uid}` subscribe block, so presence tracks exactly the same connections that already receive `ChatEvent`/`chat` push frames. Shared thin-handler helpers (`decodeChatBody`, `chatIDBody[T]`, `execChat`, `queryChatJSON` in `handler.go`) collapse the repeated claims→path-id→decode-body→reducer→map-error shape; `.golangci.yml` excludes `internal/chat/` from `dupl` for the same by-construction-FP reason as `drive`/`jmap`/`admin`. Roster (Task 11) not yet built. **Chat A2 (attachments on Drive, V120, BZ172–175 — full detail in `chat/AGENTS.md`):** `channels.go`'s `ensureSpace` (`POST /chat/channels/{id}/space` → `chat_space_ensure` → `{spaceId}`) provisions the channel's chat-bound Drive space lazily; `messages.go`'s send route passes an `attachments[]` body field straight through as `chat_message_send`'s `p_attachments jsonb` (Go never validates the array — the reducer owns every decision, atomically inside the send transaction); the conversations envelope gains top-level `attachmentsEnabled` (`feature.chat && feature.drive`) and a per-conversation nullable `spaceId`. Governance (derived membership, no ad-hoc grants, attachment-node immutability) lives entirely in `drive_member_role` v2 + the V120 RLS/reducer guards, documented in THIS row's own P1–P6 history below and in `chat/AGENTS.md`; ADR `docs/adr/DES-009-chat-bound-drive-space-governance.md`. **Chat A3 QoL (V122, BZ176–179 — full detail in `chat/AGENTS.md`):** scheduled-send (`scheduled.go`, a `chat_send` `ScheduledAction` kind), pin/save (`chat_pin.sql`/`chat_save.sql`), read receipts (`chat_readers.sql`, DES-010 watermark projection, no new write path), translation (`translate.go`, thin `/assist/complete` reuse), voice (the `chat_message_send` `msg_type`/`payload` seam), `notif_level`/broadcast-permission client-decision surfaces, polls (`poll.go`), CJK (`pg_trgm`) search + jump-to-message (`search.go`, `p_around_seq`), My-Threads (`threads.go`), org-default channels (`chat_org_defaults.sql` + a `provision_account` hook), and SSRF-hardened link unfurl with images (`unfurl.go`, `shared/ssrf.SafeHTTPClient`, its own `chat.unfurl` toggle). **W4 native-RLS Go leg (completes V134):** every user-request-path reducer/table call in this package now runs GUC-scoped — `execChat`/`queryChatJSON` (`handler.go`) and every direct multi-statement handler wrap `dbscope.RunUserScoped(ctx, h.pool, claims.UserID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {...})` before touching the DB, so `app.current_user_id` is set transaction-locally and the V134/V119 write policies admit the call under the flipped `setjoin_app`/NOBYPASSRLS runtime. Exempt (documented in code, allowlisted by name in the guard): `unfurl.go`'s `processUnfurl`/`applyUnfurl`/`fetchAndStoreImage`/`unfurlImage` (DEFINER reducers over non-user-keyed global tables — `chat_link_previews`/`blobs` — no caller identity is ever threaded through `triggerUnfurl`'s detached goroutine); `unfurl.go`'s `Worker` runs on `workerPool` (BYPASSRLS) like every other maintenance worker in this repo. Guarded by `scripts/check-rls-scope-wrap.sh`; live-proven (not just shape-checked) by `rls_scope_live_test.go`'s `TestRLSScopeWrapChatMessageSend`, which runs the real `Handler` against a pool connected AS the constrained `setjoin_app` role. |
| `config` | Env + YAML `Config` via koanf; common fields use shared `AddBaseDefaults`/`DecodeBase`, including YAML worker/owner DSNs; hot-reload on SIGHUP/HTTP-POST |
| `directorysync` | Protocol-neutral normalized directory-sync engine shared by AD and LDAP. It owns lease/recovery, connector-neutral payload projection, manager relation joins, bounded two-pass paging, and terminal cursor advancement. Protocol adapters retain transport, cursor encoding/folding, immutable-subject checks, and relation-key normalization; no LDAP type crosses this package boundary. |
| `identitysource` | Identity I2 connector supervisor and generation-fenced durable inbox/cursor pipeline shared by AD, LDAP, and SCIM. `cmd/mailserverd` starts it behind the OFF-by-default `feature.identity_model_v2` master gate, reloads active AD/LDAP generations from `config_changed`, and polls the catalog every 30 seconds to repair lost notifications. It cancels and joins replaced workers, competes through per-source DB leases across processes, stages bounded pages, never advances a cursor past blocked/non-terminal entries, and exits every worker on context cancellation. AD/LDAP lease holders resume the database's exact unfinished batch before any new remote read; restart recovery reuses its batch ID and pending inbox rows rather than discovering under a fresh UUID. |
| `identitytransition` | Identity I5 unconditional post-commit effect worker. Drains exact auth-session, LiveKit participant, and subscriber-notice intents even when identity admission is later disabled; completion explicitly excludes the just-updated effect from the data-modifying CTE snapshot before deciding whether siblings remain, preventing an otherwise permanent `effects_pending` state. Exits on context cancellation. |
| `identityops` | Identity I6/I7 unconditional bounded-job worker. Claims V167/V168 jobs through lease-fenced reducers, executes restart-safe 500-person legacy-backfill batches, atomically records one result per person with progress/terminal state, and creates sanitized 25 MiB/24-hour diagnostic ZIPs containing only `manifest.json` plus bounded JSONL evidence. `attempt_count` is claim/recovery telemetry and may exceed 20; `failure_count` alone owns the 20-failure terminal budget. A full final backfill page performs one bounded existence probe after its last ID before declaring completion. It continues already accepted work when identity flags are later disabled and exits on context cancellation. |
| `contacts` | A4: contact-import/export HTTP handler (`ImportHandler`, mounted `/contacts/import/csv` + `/preview`, `/contacts/import/vcf`, `/contacts/export/csv`, `/contacts/export/vcf`). CSV import does per-field column mapping (skip/overwrite strategy); the vCard import path (`/import/vcf`) is self-describing — no mapping/preview, mirrors the CSV handler otherwise. All four gate on `ownedMailboxForBook` (the addressbooks→mailboxes `user_id` ownership join, R1/R3 — the IDOR guard), which returns the owning mailbox id; on `imported>0` the import handlers bump the contact state token via `SELECT notify_contact_state($1,'contact_created',NULL)` so raw-INSERT imports (which bypass the `jmap_contact` reducer) still advance `addressbooks.jmap_state`/`pg_notify` for `Contact/changes` + other sessions. The two export handlers share `exportAddressBook` (gate + stream; a `render func` picks the serializer) — CSV via `migration.ExportContactsForAddressBook`, vCard via `carddav.ExportVCards` (reuses `contactToVCard`, the same serializer CardDAV GET uses, so the .vcf download stays byte-compatible with the CardDAV path). CSV/vCard parse lives in `internal/migration` (`csv_mapping.go`, `csv_import.go`, `vcf_import.go`). R3 in-statement (CONTACTS-002): the gate-verified mailbox id is threaded into the import via `migration.ImportCSVOwned`/`ImportVCFOwned`, whose shared `contactStore` re-checks the (addressbook, mailbox) pair in every statement's own WHERE (exists/delete/INSERT..SELECT), so a book re-parented or deleted mid-import cannot receive writes. Contact-import entry points after r2: the connector migration phase resolves the target address book UNDER the job's target mailbox (a `addressbooks.mailbox_id`-scoped default-book query) and imports through `ImportVCFOwned` with that mailbox as the owner scope — a resolution failure is an explicit phase error, and passing a bare mailbox id into a book-id parameter is forbidden (it let a numeric collision insert cross-tenant through the scope-0 wrapper); the unscoped `migration.ImportCSV` was deleted (zero callers after r2) and the one remaining unscoped wrapper `migration.ImportVCF` is trusted-internal only — callers must pass ids resolved server-side under the owning mailbox/user (the upload-phase VCF import), never request-owned ids |
| `cron` | In-process cron worker replacing pg_cron. `Worker` is the single scheduler authority: an injected clock/ticker drives atomic due+reserve, one in-flight execution per task, per-task timeout/panic/error accounting, cancellation admission-stop, and a bounded 30s join with loud `cron_join_timeout`. Internals receive only an Exec-only runner; `New` still requires typed `pgxutil.WorkerPool`, preserving the BYPASSRLS composition-root boundary, while reducers remain the sole business authority. The 29-row `AllTasks()` registry validates names/schedules/SQL/duplicates before startup.
