Also `RunUserRiskCron` (CA-3b, `compute_user_risk.go`) — a 15-min ticker (started as a goroutine in `main.go`) that scores every user with `auth_attempts` activity in the last 24h via the `compute_user_risk` reducer on the worker (BYPASSRLS) pool; no-op when `feature.user_risk` is off (DEFAULT OFF). |
| `db` | Mailserver-local compatibility alias for the shared `pgnotify.Listener`; existing callers keep the `db.Listener` name while the cross-binary implementation and reconnect tests live in `shared/pgnotify`. `Subscribe` is request-scoped and removes a handler when its initial LISTEN fails; `SubscribeDurable` retains process-lifetime security/config handlers for replay on the next successful reconnect. |
| ~~`dbscope`~~ | **Moved to `shared/dbscope` (arch #A8)** so authserver (a separate Go module) can consume the same GUC-scoping seam — `terms.go` replaced a hand-rolled `Begin`/`set_config`/`Commit` copy with it. Every mailserver caller (drive/chat/meet/admin/user/jmap) now imports `setjoin.com/shared/dbscope`; contract unchanged (`RunScoped`/`RunUserScoped`). Full contract: `shared/AGENTS.md`'s `dbscope` row. |
| `delivery` | Single per-recipient outbound-delivery read contract (`read.go`, `recipient.go`) shared by `user/trace.go` + `admin/admin_trace.go`: one DISTINCT-ON delivery-row query so the user trace and admin trace project identical delivery state. |
| `deploy` | SeaweedFS systemd unit generation; HA planner; scale-up/scale-down wizard; PgBouncer config |

| `drive` | **setjoin Drive P1** (spaces, sync, versioning; `feature.drive`/`toggle.FeatureDrive`, DEFAULT OFF; V114). `Handler{pool, workerPool, store, bucket, jwt, flags, logger}` (`NewHandler`) registers every route PREFIX-LESS on its own internal mux (`GET /drive/spaces`, …) — `main.go` mounts it at `mux.Handle("/drive/", d.requireAuth(d.driveH))` under the `/api` StripPrefix, so by the time `ServeHTTP` runs the path has already lost `/api`. `ServeHTTP` is the ONE gate every route passes through: per-request `flags.IsEnabled(toggle.FeatureDrive)` (G5 — never cached in the constructor) then auth. Route DISPATCH lives in `handler.go` (`NewHandler`'s route table + `ServeHTTP`'s G5/auth gate + the shared `writeJSON`/`writeJSONList` helpers only — arch-hardening C8 split the ~670-line god-file into per-domain handlers); the handlers themselves: `spaces.go` — spaces list/create/patch (field-driven PATCH — `classification` → `drive_space_set_classification`, `exportPolicy`/`retentionDays`/`wormMode`/`vaultQuotaMb` → `drive_space_set_policy`, `quotaBytes` → `drive_space_set_quota`; `?admin=1` switches to a `drive.view`-gated cross-tenant metadata read on `workerPool`); `members.go` — members list/grant/revoke; `tree.go` — tree + changes (JMAP-`/changes`-shaped; a `cannotCalculateChanges` body maps to 409); `nodes.go` — node create/patch(rename+move)/trash/restore/destroy. `memberscope.go`'s `driveMemberScopeSQL(alias, spaceExpr, userPlaceholder)` (C8) is the single R1 membership-scope predicate text every same-shape JOIN/EXISTS call site splices in (chunks.go's differently-shaped subquery is deliberately left hand-written, not shoehorned through it); `problem.go`'s `renderGateBlocked(w, r, classification)` (C8) is the one `X-Setjoin-Render` secure-open gate check `content.go`'s `getContent` and `preview.go`'s `getPreview` both call, replacing two copies. `chunks.go` (Task 19): `HEAD/PUT /drive/chunks/{sha256}` — the I-4 anti-oracle existence probe (200 only with upload-claim or version-manifest proof-of-possession, else 404, never leaking a cross-tenant dedup hit) and the tmp→verify→promote pipeline (the `blob/handler.go` pattern: stream to `tmp/upload/<rand>` while hashing, verify, `CopyObject` to `drive/<scope>/<sha>`, never `PutObject` the canonical key directly). `content.go` (Task 20): `POST/GET /drive/files/{id}/versions` (thin translation to `drive_file_put_version` — the reducer owns every decision) and `GET /drive/versions/{id}/content` (RFC 7233 single-Range streaming via `streamManifest`/`chunkRange`; secure/gxp classifications 403 unless `X-Setjoin-Render: 1` is set — the P3 in-browser viewer's signal, never a plain download link). `native_token.go` (Task 17b, batch-3 item 6): `POST /drive/native-token` mints a `scp:"drive"` mirror JWT (12h TTL, no `cnf` — unbound, skips the DPoP branch) for native (Electron) clients holding no server-origin cookie; `chain.AuthenticateRequest` rejects a non-empty `Scp` outside its named path prefix (the ONE choke point for the normal REST chain) — but `ws/handler.go` and `ws/eventsource.go` validate their JWT directly (bypassing that choke point) and independently re-guard the same invariant, machine-checked by `scripts/check-scope-containment.sh` (a manual "single choke point" review missed this once). `problem.go` owns the §7 BZ→status map (`bzSlug`) — the ONE place a drive-raised BZ code becomes an RFC 7807 `type`/`code`; `writeDriveProblem` is `reducererr.Classify` + exactly one override (BZ148 → 413, not the generic 507 every other quota code gets). `objectStore` is the package's storage seam, narrowed from blob's identical interface: `GetObject` returns `io.ReadCloser` (not the concrete `*minio.Object` blob's version returns), because the Range-streaming test double can't fabricate a real one — `MinioStore` adapts `*minio.Client` to it. `worker.go` (Task 21): `drive.NewWorker(workerPool, store, bucket, logger).Run(ctx)` — chunk GC sweep (`drive_maintenance_sweep` → `drive_chunk_gc_queue` drain, zero-ref chunks removed from storage then the DB row), the `tmp/upload/` orphan reaper (G8). Runs unconditionally (like `pushjobs`) — the toggle only gates the route layer. **P2 addition** (the desktop FolderMirror's one extra server-side read): `nodes_meta.go`: `GET /drive/nodes?ids=1,2,3` (≤500, RFC7807 422 BZ141 over the cap) — a batch `(name, parentId, kind, deletedAt, headVersionId, contentSha256, sizeBytes, contentType)` read for the sync client's delta-apply/full-resync passes (walking `tree?parent=` per changed node would be O(depth) requests; this is one). RLS is NOT the filter here (it is inert pre-DSN-flip) — the query carries an explicit `EXISTS (… drive_space_members …)` membership predicate under `dbscope.RunUserScoped`; an invisible/unknown id is silently absent from the response, never a 403/404 (BZ139 posture). **Known gap:** the P2 plan's Task 1 (`changes_wire_test.go`, a Go-side pin of the `GET /drive/changes` wire contract — anchor call, opaque-token paging, the 409 `cannot-calculate-changes` problem type — the sync client hard-depends on) was never written; only Task 2 (`nodes_meta.go`, above) landed. The TS client still consumes the route correctly today (`protocol.ts`'s tests pin the CLIENT side of the same contract), but a server-side drift in `/drive/changes`'s shape would currently surface downstream in the client rather than at the origin. **P3 addition** (ARCH-017, the secure-vault byte plane + in-app viewer's server-side surface): `preview.go` — `GET /drive/versions/{id}/preview` (Task 14), the office→PDF rendition read; same `X-Setjoin-Render` secure gate as `content.go`'s `getContent` (a rendition is still a byte-exact view of secure content — never a way around the render-only gate); lazily flips a `preview_status='none'` version to `'pending'` on first hit via `drive_preview_request`; a non-`'ready'` status 404s with an RFC 7807 `detail` naming the live status (`"...status=<pending|unsupported|failed>)"`, which the webmail `SecureViewer` parses instead of trusting its own snapshotted prop); `auditPreview` reuses `content.go`'s `auditDedup` (G3, a `'preview'`-tagged row alongside `'view'`). `PreviewWorker` (`worker.go`) ticks on `workerPool`, converts pending office versions via the `DRIVE_PREVIEW_CMD` sidecar (headless LibreOffice; `{in}`/`{outdir}` placeholders, 2-min timeout, server-side plaintext temp is fine — this is the server, not the vault), chunks the rendered PDF into the version's scope through the SAME storage-put path `content.go`'s promote uses (so P5's WORM/retention hook covers renditions for free), then calls `drive_preview_attach`; unset `DRIVE_PREVIEW_CMD` ⇒ inert (metadata-card fallback client-side, no claim, one debug log). `native_token.go` (above) is shared P1/P2/P3 infrastructure — P3's `VaultStore`/`SecureViewer` (webmail) consume the same bearer P2's `mirrorBridge` mints, via `nativeToken.ts`'s cache, never a second auth path. **P4 addition (versioning/locks, V115):** `branches.go` (`POST /drive/files/{id}/branches`, `PATCH /drive/branches/{id}` field-driven rename/set-status, `POST /drive/branches/{id}/resolve` {verb,newName?}; BZ150→409, BZ149→423 via `problem.go`'s map), `locks.go` (`PUT/DELETE /drive/files/{id}/lock` — heartbeat = repeat PUT, `?break=1` for manager/folder-admin; `GET /drive/spaces/{id}/locks` live list), `folderadmins.go` (per-node `GET/PUT/DELETE /drive/nodes/{id}/folder-admins` subtree-admin grant/revoke + the delegated member-grant variants routed off `…/members?folder=`), `activity.go` (`GET /drive/activity?space&node&beforeId` keyset feed off the per-space audit chain — the journal has no actor, so the audit rows are the substrate; the feed renders verb-only lines because `drive_activity_feed.sql` selects `notes`, always NULL in the P4 write reducers — rich per-event copy is a P5/P6 follow-up), `watchnotify.go` (the §5.6 Go push layer: on a lock/branch mutation, fan the event to SPACE MEMBERS — not just watchers — on `pg_notify('drive-watch:<uid>', …)`, per-uid in-process dedupe; `ws/handler.go`+`ws/eventsource.go` forward it as a distinct `{"@type":"DriveWatch"}` frame, NOT folded into a JMAP StateChange). **V115 DEFINER inventory** (the cross-policy actors, worker-owned + PUBLIC-revoked per the P1 LR-12 posture — everything else invoker-rights): `drive_subtree_admin` (folder-admin authority helper, used by policies + break/resolve authority), `drive_file_unlock` (manager/folder-admin break reaches another holder's row), `drive_lock_list` (a `drive.view` admin non-member reads a space's locks), the folder-member grant/revoke pair, and the notify fan-out (`drive_event_recipients` — `drive_watches` is own-rows-only under RLS, so the fan-out is DEFINER or it mails nobody). **Bug fixed with P4** (`content.go`): `postVersion` never decoded `branchId` from the body nor passed the reducer's 10th `p_branch_id` arg, so every branch save silently landed on main — now threaded end to end (client side: `api.ts`/`upload.ts`/DriveApp save-as-branch). `nodes_meta.go`'s batch read gained `branchCount` (the tree LATERAL reused) for the mirror's sibling materialization. **P5 addition (compliance-worm, V116):** `export.go` (`POST /drive/export` mints a 256-bit token, sends only its sha256 down → `drive_export_request`; `GET /drive/export/{token}` hashes → `drive_export_consume` single-use burn → streams; the client hits the consume URL via `window.location.assign` ONLY — an `<a href>` would let a prefetcher burn the grant), `worm.go` (`applyWormLocks` — the gxp put-version Object-Lock hook; the `worm_applied_until` mark-bookkeeping is a `workerPool` Exec because `drive_chunks` has no user UPDATE policy under R2; `SetRetention` nil-safe post-construction wiring like `SetJWTProvider`; the G1 posture — a lock-apply failure returns 2xx `wormPending:true`, never fails the save; the verify-chain route `GET /drive/spaces/{id}/audit/verify` → `verify_drive_audit_chain`, `drive.view`-gated). `worker.go` gained `retryPendingWormLocks` (re-applies pending gxp locks each tick) + a GC `errors.Is(err, storage.ErrObjectLocked)` gcSkip arm (forward-compatible — minio-go/SeaweedFS doesn't surface that sentinel today, ponytail-flagged). The two approval consumers ride the EXISTING approval engine: `internal/approval/handler.go`'s Decide switch gains `case "drive.export"`/`"drive.share"` (tenant tx → `drive_export_approval_decide`/`drive_share_approval_decide`, the `hr.leave` recipe), `email_page.go` covers them, `admin_approvals.go`'s `validConsumerTypes` lists both (editable in the shipped approvals console, zero new UI). `listSpacesAdmin` (the `?admin=1` read) merges per-space `drive_worm_pending()` counts into each row's `wormPending` (G1 admin visibility — degrades to 0 on error, never fails the list). **V116 DEFINER inventory** (worker-owned + PUBLIC-revoked, the LR-12 posture — the decide wrappers run as a NON-member approver, so without the footer the grant INSERT / audit write / anti-oracle read all fail under FORCE-RLS): `drive_export_approval_decide`, `drive_share_approval_decide`, `drive_worm_pending`. BZ146 (grant invalid/used/expired) registered in `reducererr` + `problem.go` (`bzSlug["BZ146"]="invalid-grant"`, 409). **G9 substrate fix** (`storage/posix_driver.go`, a separate pre-P5 commit): POSIX WORM lengthen-only retention extend + immutable-file dance + xattr-namespace fallback, with a per-key `PutObjectRetention` mutex (the lost-update race — the shorter lock could win in storage while the DB's GREATEST mark recorded the longer). **P6 addition (qol-audit, V117 + reducers):** `trash.go` (`GET /drive/spaces/{id}/trash?tier=2` admin tier-2 bin over `drive_trash_admin_list`; `POST /drive/nodes/{id}/purge-restore` → 204 over `drive_node_purge_restore` — restore-to-original with a `-2` suffix on a live-path collision; tier≠2 400s naming the existing `tree?deleted=1` tier-1 route rather than reimplementing it), `search.go` (`GET /drive/search?q=&space=&max=` name ILIKE over `drive_search_nodes`), `audit.go` (`GET /drive/audit?space=&actor=&action=&from=&to=&q=&max=&beforeId=` → `{items,hasMore}` keyset over `drive_audit_query`; `&format=csv` streams a `text/csv` download looping `beforeId` until `hasMore` false — `encoding/csv`, no dep; **`csvCell` defuses formula injection** — a leading `= + - @ \t \r` gets a `'` prefix, applied to every user-controlled column, pinned by `TestAuditQuery_CSVExport_FormulaInjectionGuard`; alarm CRUD `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /drive/audit-alarms[/{id}]` body `{spaceId?,actionPattern,threshold?,windowMinutes?,enabled?}`, `actionPattern` = exact §9.3 action or `prefix.*`; `GET /drive/audit-actions` — arch-hardening C2 — reads `drive_audit_actions()`, the single-source 37-action catalog reducer both this dropdown and `drive_access_audit`'s own-action check consume, replacing the deleted Go `driveAuditActionCatalog`/`validActionPattern` copy; no admin gate needed, it's a `STABLE` SQL constant), `asof.go` (`GET /drive/spaces/{id}/asof?at=<RFC3339>` time-point metadata view over `drive_space_as_of`, documented ceiling — a node purged **after** T is invisible to a normal caller; `GET /drive/watches` → `[{nodeId,nodeName,kind,ops}]`, the read side P4 never shipped; the `drive-alarm` push channel auth in `jmap/handler.go`+`ws/handler.go`+`ws/eventsource.go`). **App-level authz is PRIMARY here (RLS inert pre-flip, batch-3 blocker #1):** `requireDriveManage` (`audit.go`) runs `SELECT drive_has_admin_perm($1,'drive.manage')` before `listAlarms` — the one alarm route with no reducer of its own, a plain SELECT. Arch-hardening C2 moved create/update/delete onto `drive_alarm_create`/`_update`/`_delete`, which own the `drive.manage` gate (BZ140) and the action-pattern validation against `drive_audit_actions()` (BZ141) themselves — `audit.go` is now thin HTTP<->reducer translation for those three, no separate Go-side check. The watches query carries an explicit `WHERE w.user_id = $1` bound to `claims.UserID`, never bare RLS. `JMAPHandler.HasDriveAdminPerm` is a new exported method so `ws.Handler` (no direct pool) can gate the `drive-alarm` channel at connect time, fail-closed on any error incl. nil pool (keeps the `jmap.NewJMAPHandler(nil,…)` ws unit tests safe); channel membership is checked at connect, not per-message (a mid-session grant/revoke needs a reconnect — same as every fixed-list WS channel). **V117 reducers:** two-level trash columns (`purged_at`/`purged_by`) + `drive_audit_alarms`; `drive_node_destroy` flipped to a tier-2 **purge** (soft) and the true leaf-first hard-delete relocated to a worker-only `drive_node_destroy_hard(bigint) RETURNS boolean` (returns false, never raises, on gxp-in-retention); `drive_node_purge_restore`/`drive_trash_admin_list` (drive.manage/drive.view gated, SECURITY DEFINER — I-16); `drive_search_nodes`/`drive_audit_query`/`drive_space_as_of` (invoker-rights, own R1 checks; audit-query's `drive.view` arm gated by `drive_has_admin_perm`, keyset-paginated, **raw per-access rows NEVER collapsed** — §9.3 locked, any session/timeline grouping is UI-only); `drive_maintenance_sweep` layered with the two-level cadence (tier-1 `deleted_at`>30d → tier-2 `purged_at`; tier-2 `purged_at`>30d → `drive_node_destroy_hard` bottom-up; gxp refuses until retention lapses) + `drive_audit_alarms` evaluation (chained `alarm.fire` audit + `drive-alarm` notify, once per rule per window) — sweep result grew `trashAged`/`trashDestroyed`/`alarmsFired`. **`drive_node_purge_mark`** is a private DEFINER helper (local to `drive_node_destroy.sql`) — a FORCE-RLS UPDATE that sets `purged_at` makes the resulting row SELECT-invisible under `drive_nodes_read`'s USING clause, so the purge write routes through a DEFINER choke to avoid a post-flip 42501. **Chat A2 addition (V120, `docs/adr/DES-009-chat-bound-drive-space-governance.md` — full contract detail in `chat/AGENTS.md`, this entry covers only the Drive-side surface):** `drive_spaces.chat_channel_id` (nullable UNIQUE FK, `ON DELETE SET NULL`) marks a space as chat-bound. **`drive_member_role(space,user)` v2** (`mailserver/sql/dbschema/V120__chat_drive_binding.sql`) gains a second arm — a chat-bound space with no matching row in arm 1 (`drive_space_members`) derives a role from `chat_channel_members` (owner/admin → `manager`, member → `editor`); COALESCE keeps arm 1 first, but V120's RLS + reducer belt guarantee arm 1 can never hold a row for a chat-bound space (below), so derivation is authoritative in practice — behavior for a NON-chat space is byte-identical (arm 2 joins on `chat_channel_id IS NULL`, never matches). `memberscope.go`'s `driveMemberScopeSQL` (the single Go R1 gate) and the new `driveMemberScopeUnionSQL` (a two-arm UNION ALL FROM-fragment for set-returning list queries — `spaces.go`'s `listSpaces`, `nodes_meta.go`'s batch read — where a per-row `SECURITY DEFINER` call would be the wrong shape) both delegate to the same function/logic, so Go and SQL cannot drift on who counts as a member; `scripts/check-drive-memberscope.sh` guards against a hand-rolled `drive_space_members ...` membership join creeping back in anywhere. **Governance is enforced, not narrated (DES-009 D2/D3/D4):** the `drive_space_members_write` self-seed RLS arm and the `drive_spaces_read` owner-read arm are both guarded to `chat_channel_id IS NULL` — a chat space's `owner_user_id` (still `NOT NULL`, set to the channel creator) grants NOTHING; `drive_member_grant` raises **BZ175** on any ad-hoc grant/revoke targeting a chat-bound space (the RLS WITH CHECK is the primary block, this is the reducer-level belt); `drive_node_trash`/`drive_node_move`/`drive_node_rename`/`drive_file_put_version` all raise **BZ175** when the target node is already referenced by `chat_message_attachments` — a chat attachment is immutable via the Drive app, mutable only through the chat message itself (soft-delete). **Chat-bound spaces are hidden from the `/drive` surface entirely** (Grill D1) — both `listSpaces` and `listSpacesAdmin` (`spaces.go`) filter `chat_channel_id IS NULL`; files are reached only via chat + the per-file content route. **`chatAttachmentLiveSQL`** (`memberscope.go`) is the content-route guard closing Grill D3: a version whose ONLY `chat_message_attachments` rows are all for soft-deleted messages 404s for a regular member (bytes+rows retained for A4 eDiscovery, read directly by the BYPASSRLS worker pool, which never routes through this predicate). **The orphan-chunk reaper** (`drive_maintenance_sweep.sql`, Chat A2 Task 6): the pre-existing GC only enqueued a chunk for deletion on an `UPDATE OF ref_count` trigger fire, so a chunk registered but never actually referenced by a `put_version` call (an abandoned chat composer, or any abandoned Drive upload) leaked forever, unmetered — this was a genuine pre-existing Drive bug, not chat-specific; the sweep now also enqueues any `ref_count=0` chunk whose only `drive_upload_claims` row is past its 7-day TTL and prunes the expired claim itself. **M5 mailctl-owner caveat (open, pre-native-RLS-flip gap — see TODOS.md):** V120 is the first schema migration to `CREATE OR REPLACE` a `setjoin_worker`-owned function (`drive_member_role`); `mailctl schema apply` connects via `resolveSchemaDSN()` (`mailctl/schema.go`), which resolves `DB_DIRECT_DSN` → `DB_DSN` only — it has **no awareness of `DB_OWNER_DSN`** at all. In this dev/test environment the connecting role (`mail`) happens to be a Postgres superuser, so the `CREATE OR REPLACE` silently succeeds regardless of ownership — masking that in a real role-separated deployment (a non-superuser app-pool credential, `setjoin_worker`/`setjoin_owner` as distinct roles, the native-RLS-flip target state) the SAME `mailctl schema apply` would fail with a permission-denied replacing a function it does not own. Confirmed present, not yet fixed — `resolveSchemaDSN` needs a `DB_OWNER_DSN` fallback (or schema-apply needs to run as `setjoin_owner`) BEFORE the native-RLS flip; ledgered in `TODOS.md`. **Chat A4a addition (V126, purge-driven staging):** `chat_purge_expired` (`mailserver/sql/reducers/chat_purge_expired.sql`) is the one caller that lifts the chat-attachment never-trash posture on purpose — a chat-referenced node is otherwise permanently live (BZ175 blocks trash/move/rename/re-version through the Drive app itself), so before reclaiming an expired message's attachment bytes the purge reducer directly `UPDATE`s the node's `deleted_at`/`purged_at` (the V117 tier-2 columns) — legal because the BZ175 guard is a reducer-level check, not a trigger, so a direct staging UPDATE from the purge reducer bypasses nothing it shouldn't. Only once staged does `drive_node_destroy_hard` (tier-2 sweeper, `purged_at IS NOT NULL` precondition) actually reclaim chunks/refcount/quota on the worker's next maintenance tick — the purge reducer itself never destroys bytes directly. Full contract: `mailserver/internal/chat/AGENTS.md`'s "Records lifecycle (A4a)" section. **Meet B2 addition (V129, plan delta 5 as corrected at scoring review):** `drive_spaces.meeting_id` (nullable UNIQUE FK, `ON DELETE SET NULL`) marks a space as meeting-bound — the exact V120 §A chat-bound-space shape generalized to a second binding kind, one nullable FK, zero membership mirroring; `meeting_space_ensure` (a `chat_space_ensure` clone, worker-owned `SECURITY DEFINER`, lazy per-channel-equivalent per-meeting provisioning) deliberately carries **NO `upload_max_bytes`** — cloning `chat_space_ensure`'s 100 MiB `export_policy` cap would fail every real recording at BZ148. **`drive_member_role` v3** gains a third arm deriving `manager` (host/cohost, no `joined_at` required — their participant row is reducer-created and the B2 ingest attributes to the meeting owner regardless) / `viewer` (an attended member, `joined_at IS NOT NULL`) from `meeting_participants`, banned excluded, no `left_at` filter (attendance is a historical fact, the `meeting_attendee_role` posture). **`driveMemberScopeUnionSQL` gains the same arm 3** (`memberscope.go`) — corrected at scoring review (eng E-F4): the union has TWO real consumers, `spaces.go`'s `listSpaces` AND `nodes_meta.go`'s batch metadata read, not one; without arm 3 there an attendance-derived member could stream a recording's bytes (the single-gate predicate, `driveMemberScopeSQL` → `drive_member_role`, picks it up for free) but never resolve its node metadata. `listSpaces`/`listSpacesAdmin` gain `AND s.meeting_id IS NULL` beside the existing `chat_channel_id IS NULL` filter — meeting-bound spaces stay hidden from `/drive` entirely, reached only via the Meet recordings surface + the per-file content route, the chat-bound-space precedent. **`PromoteChunk`/`ChunkPromoter`** (`chunks.go`) are now EXPORTED (were package-private) — the B2 ingest worker (`internal/meet`) calls the exact same tmp→verify→promote pipeline a user chunk upload uses, so a recording's bytes get the same content-addressing/dedup/anti-oracle guarantees as any other Drive write, no parallel path. The DES-009 governance guards (owner-grants-nothing, no ad-hoc `drive_space_members` rows, immutable-via-Drive-app for a registry-referenced node) extend verbatim to the `meeting_id IS NOT NULL` case in `drive_member_grant`/`drive_node_{trash,move,rename}`/`drive_file_put_version` — one guard clause added per site, no new predicate shape. Full contract: `mailserver/internal/meet/AGENTS.md`'s "Recording (B2)" section. |
| `eapjobs` | EAP bulk-apply worker (`worker.go`): drains `eap_apply_jobs` by calling `email_address_policy_apply_bulk` per row on `workerPool` (BYPASSRLS) with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`; a ticker started in `main.go`, exits on ctx cancel. |
| `federation` | **Pillar C0 cross-org federation SPIKE** (`feature.federation`/`toggle.FeatureFederation`, DEFAULT OFF; V138; branch `pillar-c0-federation-spike`, **NOT merged** — a throwaway de-risking spike, findings `retired-docs:superpowers/research/2026-07-16-C0-federation-findings.md`, program index `retired-docs:superpowers/plans/archive/2026-07-16-pillar-C-program-index.md`). Bespoke setjoin↔setjoin s2s: `wire.go` (`Frame{Kind,StanzaID,Payload}`, `Hello`, length-prefixed JSON), `stream.go` (`Dial`/`Listen`/`AcceptStream`/`PeerStream`, XEP-0178 anchor auth — **asymmetric**: Dial validates the server leaf in `VerifyPeerCertificate` against the per-peer anchor, Listen validates the client leaf post-hello against the `federation_allowlist.peer_anchor` row selected by `hello.FromDomain` since client certs carry no SNI), `discover.go` (`ResolvePeer` over an injectable `Resolver`, `_setjoin-im._tcp.<domain>` SRV), `sender.go`+`receiver.go` (DES-011 dumb transport: ONE `Sender` per peer owns the in-order outbox drain + the single read loop; **NOT** pushjobs — a dedicated NOTIFY-woken drain via the `DB_DIRECT_DSN` `db.Listener`, halt-on-failure, reconnect resumes from the durable ack cursor). Authority is all in reducers (`federation_enqueue` — `federation_peers.last_seq` row-lock rail, NOT bigserial; `federation_apply_inbound` — exactly-once on the `(peer,seq)` PK, dup-still-acks, BZ202 gap-raise; `federation_ack`; `federation_peer_authorize` — allow-list default-off/mutual-opt-in, BZ203; `federation_ingress_check` — advisory rate/spam; `federation_room_fanout` — hub-per-room, deterministic lock order). All V138 tables (`federation_peers`/`outbox`/`inbox`/`inbox_cursor`/`allowlist`/`ingress_counters`/`rooms`/`room_members`) are FORCE-RLS **deny-all** (no membership axis — the ARCH-018 matrix disposition, not a scope helper). Live-proven on linux-ci-host (real Linux/PG18): ordered exactly-once across receiver/sender SIGKILL + iptables RST (p50=41ms), sovereign residency, wrong-anchor reject, allow-list refuse. `testharness/` (the `fedharness` daemon, SRV stub, per-domain-CA gen) stays as the C1 live-gate harness, untouched — a SECOND, production-shaped consumer of the same transport now also exists (below), not a replacement. **Pillar C1 (DM federation, `retired-docs:superpowers/plans/archive/2026-07-17-pillar-C1-dm-federation.md`, V139) Task 8 — admin allow-list/anchor console:** `internal/admin/admin_federation.go` (`GET/POST/DELETE /admin/federation/peers[/{domain}]`, `federation.manage`-gated, NOT toggle-gated — the console must be usable to prep an allow-list before `feature.federation` ever flips ON) + reducers `federation_peer_set.sql`/`federation_peer_delete.sql` (BZ207 admin-argument backstop, BZ208 disable-before-delete). Real x509 parse (reuses `smime.Fingerprint`) + self-domain refusal (against `config.Config.FederationLocalDomain`, Task 5's formalized field — a reducer can't see process config, so this stays Go-side) live in the handler; the reducer's own PEM-shape check is a shallow defense-in-depth backstop. Every write audits an `admin_audit_log` act carrying an anchor **fingerprint**, never the PEM, and emits `app_notify('federation_allowlist_changed', …)` for the Task 5 Service reconcile to pick up. Delete requires the peer already disabled (DES-011: disabling is what makes the Service drop the live stream first) and reaps orphaned unacked `federation_outbox` rows for the deleted peer in the same transaction. Webmail: `src/routes/admin/(access)/federation/+page.svelte`, a one-click enable/disable toggle posts a blank `anchorPem` so the backend carries the existing anchor forward (the anchor is never echoed back by `GET`/`List`, so a full re-paste isn't needed just to flip a peer off). **Task 5 — production `Service` (`service.go`), wired into `cmd/mailserverd/main.go`:** promotes `testharness/cmd/fedharness`'s `reconnect.go` dial-loop + `senderRegistry` and `anchor.go`'s allow-list-backed `AnchorLookup` into a long-lived component started unconditionally alongside `meet.NewWorker`/`drive.NewWorker` — TWO independent gates, do not conflate: a STATIC gate in `main.go` (constructed+started only when `FEDERATION_LOCAL_DOMAIN`+`_CERT`+`_KEY` — `config.Config`, Task 5's formalized fields — are all set, the `meetCfg.Configured()`/LIVEKIT_* precedent; `NewService` itself returns nil on an empty `LocalDomain`, so a half-built Service can never exist), and a DYNAMIC gate inside `Run`'s `reconcileOnce` (the `meet.Worker.tick` pattern — re-reads `feature.federation` + the enabled `federation_allowlist` set every 20s tick AND on a `federation_allowlist_changed` NOTIFY wake, converging the live per-peer dial-loop/stream set to match: a peer that drops out of the enabled set gets its dial context actively cancelled — which tears down its stream via `wireAndRun`'s own `sender.Run(ctx)` returning on `ctx.Done` — and an ACCEPT-side stream for a peer we don't dial is force-closed directly via a `streamRegistry`, DES-011 eviction-not-TTL both ways; a toggle flip to OFF drops everything unconditionally). `federation_outbox:<peer>` NOTIFY wake is subscribed per-peer dynamically (not the harness's static `--peers` CSV) as peers enter/leave the enabled set. Unit-tested without a DB via injected seams (`AllowlistLister`, `Resolver`) — `service_test.go` proves the static-inert case, the OFF-drops-existing case, and the dial-vs-accept split; the real 2-instance dial/accept round trip is Task 9's live gate. **Task 9 — deploy + live gate (RAN 2026-07-17, linux-ci-host, real Linux/PG18) — this is now the PRODUCTION posture, no longer a spike:** `Config.Resolver` (nil ⇒ `NetResolver{}`/real DNS — main.go unchanged) is the second adapter on the discover.go Resolver seam, injected by `testharness/cmd/fedservice` — a thin main that runs the PRODUCTION `federation.Service` (not fedharness's C0 dial loop) against a real DB + leaf cert, built solely for this gate. Two fedservice instances (fed_alpha/fed_bravo, dm-scoped allow-list rows via `federation_peer_set`, loopback SRV stub) proved live: compose→cross→delivered-tick (ack-as-receipt), exactly-once across BOTH sender- and receiver-SIGKILL (cursor resume, zero dups), poison consumed+acked+dropped without wedging, live admin disable → ~1s NOTIFY-driven stream eviction → dial refused → re-enable recovers, mixed-charset spam quarantined (flagged, still DELIVERED), BZ204/BZ206 verbatim, fingerprint-only audit acts, and the compliance leg — `chat_hold_active`=t on the federated channel, `chat_ediscovery_search` AND the real Export ZIP attribute every federated message to `federated_sender_addr` with `authorId` null. **The gate found+fixed a live product bug (committed separately):** accept-side `Listen` authorized streams at scope `'room'` (C0-harness inheritance); `federation_peer_authorize` refuses that against a `scope='dm'` row, so every dm-scoped peer — the exact rows the admin console creates for DM federation — could never connect. Stream authorize is now floor-`'dm'`; room-CONTENT enforcement for dm-scoped peers is C2's per-payload job at fanout/apply. Deploy: install.sh gained an optional `FEDERATION_ENABLED` leg (org CA + SAN leaf in ONE step, law 3; `FEDERATION_*` rendered into config.env; ca.crt is the only artifact handed to peers) — discovery is SRV-ONLY, the air-gap per-peer endpoint override does NOT exist (documented honestly; deferral seam in TODOS.md "Federation C1"). **Standing gates before `feature.federation` flips ON: 记录留存 legal sign-off (C0 GO-PENDING-LEGAL) + a real-DNS two-box install smoke** — plus the rest of the deferral registry (webmail live-browser badge proof, BZ205 live proof, room-scope C2 requirement) in TODOS.md "Federation C1". **Pillar C2 (rooms + federated typing, `retired-docs:superpowers/plans/archive/2026-07-17-pillar-C2-rooms-presence-federation.md`, V140, branch `pillar-c2-rooms-federation`) Task 1 — schema:** promotes `federation_rooms`/`federation_room_members` from C0 scaffolding to a real roster (`invited_at`/`parked_at`/`parked_reason='backpressure'`/`left_at`/`left_reason IN ('removed','revoked')`/`coverage_epochs` gap history), binds `chat_channels.federated_room_id`/`federated_room_removed_at` (hub: `federated_peer_domain NULL`; spoke mirror: `federated_peer_domain` = the bound hub, the V139 column reused), widens `chat_messages.msg_type` to include `'system'` (the local federation gap-marker, forge-guarded at `chat_message_send`'s BZ168), and fixes THE stanza-dedup cross-org collision (`chat_messages_fed_stanza_uk` re-scoped to `(channel_id, federated_origin_domain, federated_stanza_id)` — `federation_apply_chat` updated in the same commit to stamp+arbiter on the 3-column key). **Task 2 — room lifecycle reducers:** `federation_room_create` (hub-side bind, R1 owner/admin proof BZ163/BZ209, refuses non-channel/already-bound/archived/DM-shaped/org-default (Q14 arm 1, BZ209) and gxp (Q7 arm 1, BZ210) channels, mints `roomId` as `gen_random_uuid()::text || '@' || lower(btrim(p_local_domain))`); `federation_room_enqueue_member` (the ONE per-member fan-out discipline home, shared by every control-op AND, in Task 3, message fan-out: parked-skip, per-send `federation_peer_authorize(…,'room')` → REVOKE `left_reason='revoked'` + a best-effort `application/x-setjoin-advisory` `room_revoked` frame on BZ203 — never a park, the ratified Q9 split — depth-over-cap → PARK `'backpressure'`, and an edge-triggered pre-cap `federation.room_member_depth_warn` audit act at `federation.outbox_warn_pct`% (DEFAULT 80) of `federation.outbox_cap`); `federation_room_invite`/`federation_room_remove` (BZ211 membership-authority family, Q3 two-key gate — the fed admin's allow-list scope decides who's invitable, the hub owner/admin decides membership; THE LOCK ORDER LAW's sorted-`FOR UPDATE`-snapshot-before-upsert shape, via a `WITH … FOR UPDATE` CTE since `FOR UPDATE` can't combine with an aggregate in one query; invite IS re-admit, closing an open coverage epoch and carrying `gapFrom`/`gapTo`; remove appends an open `'removed'` epoch so a kicked-then-re-admitted gap still reaches the manifest); `federation_room_has_active_members` (the DEFINER Q13 archive-refusal probe — `federation_room_members` is FORCE-RLS deny-all, so chat_channel_update's invoker-rights caller can't read it directly). Q13 (archiving a room-bound channel refused, both shapes, BZ209) lives in `chat_channel.sql`'s `chat_channel_update`; Q7 arm 2 (gxp-classifying a room-bound channel refused, BZ210) in `chat_channel_set_classification.sql`; Q14 arm 2 (org-defaulting a room-bound channel refused, BZ209) in `chat_org_defaults.sql`. Full pgTAP: `federation_room_lifecycle.pg` (91 assertions incl. the Q12 scope-downgrade-mid-control-op-fanout revoke case, the warn-fires-once-then-doesn't-refire case, the remove→re-invite gap-marker chain, an archived-hub-channel re-invite refusal (BZ211, Task 3 review-fold), and the warn's lower-bound-conjunct mutation-proofing). BZ209/BZ210/BZ211 registered in `shared/reducererr`. **Task 3 — the outbound room arm + the promoted `federation_room_fanout` (BUILT):** `chat_message_send` (`chat_message.sql`) gains a three-way enqueue split on the same choke as C1's DM arm — DM (`v_fed_domain` set, `v_room_id` NULL, unchanged) / SPOKE (both set: one user-initiated `federation_enqueue` of `{op:'message',roomId,fromAddr,body,sentAt,clientMsgId}` — NO `originDomain`, the hub stamps it, Q2) / HUB (`v_room_id` set, `v_fed_domain` NULL: the local land already happened, then `federation_room_fanout` relays the SAME payload to the roster). New refusals BZ212 (Q11 content scope — attachment or non-`'text'` msg_type in a federated room) and BZ213 (kicked/closed-mirror send refusal — `federated_room_removed_at` set), both registered in `shared/reducererr` (this task's sole site). `federation_room_fanout` promoted from the C0 4-arg scaffolding to the 5-arg production shape (`p_origin_domain text DEFAULT NULL`, `DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS` the 4-arg overload first): resolves the room, stamps `originDomain` (NULL ⇒ the room's own `hub_domain` — the hub's own send; set ⇒ a relay, Task 4), takes the sorted roster `FOR UPDATE` snapshot (a standalone statement, the Task 2 CTE-split shape — THE LOCK ORDER LAW: all roster locks before any `federation_peers` lock) with `member_domain <> v_origin` as the no-re-relay/origin-skip exclusion baked into the same statement, then loops `federation_room_enqueue_member` per member — all park/revoke/warn/enqueue discipline stays in that ONE Task 2 helper, now proven under genuine concurrency (`fanout_concurrency_test.go`, DB-gated: N-iteration invite/remove-vs-message-fanout race, zero `40P01`; a park-racing-re-invite race settles into a coherent final roster state, never left, never torn). Q10 (no delivered ticks in rooms) is mechanical — `federation_ack`'s receipt leg only ever matches `content_type = 'application/x-setjoin-chat'`, so a room-content ack can never set `delivered_at`; pinned by a `federation_receipt.pg` case, commented in `federation_ack.sql` as deliberate. Two review-fold fixes landed in this task: `federation_room_invite` now refuses re-invite into an archived hub channel (BZ211 — closes a silent Q13-close-flow re-open path), and `federation_room_enqueue_member`'s park/revoke UPDATEs are `IF FOUND`-guarded before their audit act + notify (a remove racing the same member no longer produces a spurious act naming an already-removed member). Full pgTAP: `federation_room_outbound.pg` (35 assertions — folds the two surviving C0 spike assertions, atomic fan-out + independent per-peer seqs, against the promoted fan-out with real scope-`'room'` allow-list fixtures; the spike file `federation_room.pg` is DELETED, superseded). **Task 4 — inbound `federation_apply_room` (the security crux, message op) + receiver dispatch:** `federation_apply_room(p_peer_domain, p_peer_seq, p_stanza_id, p_payload, p_spam_quarantined, p_spam_reason) → text` (DEFINER, worker-owned, `federation_apply_chat`'s sibling): exactly-once bookkeeping first (verbatim from `federation_apply_chat` — dup/cursor-lock/BZ202-gap/envelope-only inbox row), then `op` dispatch (`'message'` here, others poison-stub until Task 5), then per-payload `'room'`-scope authorize (BZ203 → poison, never a raise), then the HUB/SPOKE trust split that is **ADR DES-012's compensating control**: HUB side re-derives `fromAddr`'s domain against the stream peer (C1's law, unchanged — a member can only claim to be itself) and OVERWRITES any payload `originDomain`; SPOKE side requires the payload's `originDomain` to be `roster ∪ {hub}` (the bound hub's own `federation_room_members` mirror, non-left) — never trusts a relayed origin unconditionally. Lands under the 3-column dedup arbiter (`channel_id, origin, clientMsgId`); the HUB side relays in the SAME txn via `federation_room_fanout` with the origin-skip (no re-relay to the sender). `receiver.go` dispatches both `application/x-setjoin-room` and `application/x-setjoin-advisory` content types into this one op-dispatch table (`roomContentType`/`advisoryContentType` constants). **Task 5 — control-op applier arms (spoke-side only):** replaces the Task 4 op stub with `op:'invite'` (the ONLY spoke mirror-creation path — hub-binding poison guards run BEFORE any write: the `@`-suffix structural check + an existing local room's `hub_domain` mismatch both poison, closing a cross-hub roomId-hijack BLOCKER a review found; creates the mirror `visibility='private'` — never V119's `'public'` default, which DES-008 would make self-joinable before the contact owner acts; clears `federated_room_removed_at` on re-admit; inserts the local `msg_type='system'` gap-marker row from `gapFrom`/`gapTo` when the invite closes an open coverage epoch — this insert surfaced a same-day bug, fixed in `V141__federation_room_gap_marker.sql`: the pre-existing V139 `chat_messages_author_or_federated_ck` CHECK predated V140's `'system'` widening and rejected a row with neither a local author nor a remote sender), `op:'roster'` (replaces the roster mirror from the payload, never DELETEs — history stays visible), `op:'remove'` (sets `federated_room_removed_at`, the kicked-mirror read-only law — BZ213 at send, straggler poison at apply), `op:'room_revoked'` (the Q9 advisory — same `remove` machinery, but the ONE deliberate skip of the step-3 `'room'`-scope authorize, since the frame's entire purpose is informing a peer whose scope was JUST withdrawn). **Task 6 — federated typing** (`sender.go`/`receiver.go`/`service.go`/`federation_typing_notify.sql`/`chat/presence.go`): a promoted `KindEphemeral` wire class — no seq, no outbox row, no ack, silently dropped on any failure (never a poison RAISE — there is no bookkeeping to protect). `federation_typing_notify` is READ-ONLY + NOTIFY, zero table writes (the `notify_chat_state` NOTIFY-decouple carve-out aside — see `chat/AGENTS.md`). Outbound: `presence.go`'s `typingSignal` debounces (~1s floor, per-channel, bounded map) BEFORE any DB read or goroutine spawn — a noisy client must never create local DB load even though the wire frame would be dropped anyway; fires only after `chat_typing_signal` itself succeeded. `Service.SendTyping` fans DM/spoke-room (one send) or hub-room (roster fan-out, no per-member identity in the payload) through a per-peer in-memory token bucket (~5 tokens, refill 1/s) — no `federation_ingress_counters` involvement, ephemeral is deliberately outside that ledger. Typing payloads/addresses are never logged on any path. **Task 7 — Go surface + webmail** (`internal/chat/federation_rooms.go`, `federation_room_roster.sql`): routes `POST /chat/channels/{id}/federation` (bind, 404 while OFF or local-domain unconfigured) `/federation/invite` `/federation/remove` `GET /federation` (roster read, any member) on the chat mux; `chat_read.sql`'s envelope gains `federatedRoomId`/`federatedRoomRemoved`/`isFederatedRoomHub`. Webmail: a persistent "Federated · N orgs" badge (hub and spoke alike — the N counts sibling member orgs only, never the hub itself); a roster sheet (active/amber-depth-warn/parked/revoked/left chips, owner/admin invite+remove+re-admit-via-re-invite); the Q1 divergence-disclosure text at invite time AND on edit/delete confirmation (not just the export manifest); the kicked/scope-revoked banner (one string covers both — both arrive as the same `federation_removed` state); the composer attach affordance derives from `!federatedRoomId` client-side (honesty — BZ212 remains the enforcement); the archive affordance mirrors the BZ209 Q13 refusal (still enforced server-side); the `msg_type='system'` gap marker renders as a centered i18n'd system row. **Task 8 — compliance + hygiene**: the eDiscovery export manifest (`admin_chat_ediscovery_export.go`) gains per-member coverage facts (`joinedAt`/`removedAt`/`removedReason`/`knownMissingIntervals`, sourced from `coverage_epochs`) and disclosure text branched on `isHub` (a spoke-worded sentence on a hub export would undersell the record) plus the transport-residue and typing-exclusion disclaimer lines whenever any federated room or DM is present; a cursor-regression guard in all three appliers (`IF p_peer_seq <= v_last THEN RETURN 'dup'`, closing the hole the reaper would otherwise open — a reaped seq's replay must still dup, never move the cursor backward); `federation_reap(p_keep)` (DEFINER, `c_batch=5000`/tick, reaps `federation_inbox`/`federation_outbox` past a 7-day window — envelope-only, nothing compliance-bearing) on an hourly `ReapWorker` ticker wired unconditionally in `main.go` (D11 — residue reaps even with the toggle OFF or the s2s Service never constructed); `federation_outbox_redact_purged` (origin-qualified `{origin,key}` pairs, never origin-blind — the same cross-org collision V140's dedup fix targets) called from `chat_purge_expired`'s destroy leg so the outbox — transport residue, never an archive copy — honors a lawful purge instead of outliving it; the receiver funnel (any content_type outside `{chat,room,advisory}` consumed+acked+redacted, never verbatim-landed, never dropped-without-consuming). **Task 9 — the live 3-org gate (RAN 2026-07-18, linux-ci-host, real Linux/PG18) — production posture, evidence in `retired-docs:superpowers/research/2026-07-18-C2-task9-live-gate.md`:** three `fedservice` instances (alpha=hub, bravo, charlie; bravo↔charlie deliberately never allow-listed, proving hub-per-room over mesh) proved live, with literal command/output: real room bootstrap via the actual reducers, mirror+roster materialization on both spokes, message relay both directions with honest attribution, THE DEDUP COLLISION proof (bravo's and charlie's colliding local ids both land as distinct rows everywhere via the V140 3-column arbiter), hub SIGKILL → queue-not-lose → instant gap-free reconnect, scope-downgrade → visible revocation (BZ213 + the advisory + the read-only banner + the re-invite gap marker), park-at-cap with the pre-cap warn firing strictly before park, kick + straggler poison + zero leakage, bilateral `federation_peer_delete` full-reap → re-add clean restart, federated typing crossing the hub relay + flood-throttling (DB-write-count 0) + zero `delivered_at` on any of 50 room messages across all three orgs, and the compliance leg (search attribution, hold, export ZIP + checksums) — plus the automated `room_test.go` oracle (`TestRoomHarness_BootstrapAndMessageRelay`/`TestRoomHarness_HubOfflineConvergence`, `FED_HARNESS`-gated) covering the OS-process-control-free subset. One real architectural finding, NOT fixed in this pass (a feature-shaped follow-up, not a bug): spoke-side eDiscovery export `KnownMissingIntervals` is structurally always empty (the `roster`/`invite` wire payload never carries `coverage_epochs` to a spoke) — see TODOS.md "Federation C2". **Standing gates before `feature.federation` flips ON anywhere: 记录留存 legal sign-off (now covering C2's room-residency posture, ADR DES-012) + a real-DNS two-box install smoke** — neither run from any sandboxed/CI session to date. |
| `appmarket` | Closed managed-Collabora test-delivery runner. It reads one strict root-controlled manifest, claims reducer-authorized operations through `DB_WORKER_DSN`, pulls/verifies the pinned multi-arch digest, stages the fixed Docker container/network/config-volume profile, probes discovery, records immutable revision evidence, and stops at `connected_inactive`. Install failure after staging transitions through rollback and deletes only the manifest's fixed app-owned resources. It never activates Office routing, accepts raw commands, or receives Drive/object-store credentials. `setjoin-updater --office-app-only` runs it without release-store or migration-owner privileges. Live gate: `SETJOIN_LIVE_COLLABORA=1 go test -v ./internal/appmarket -run '^TestLiveDockerCollaboraInstall$'`. |
| `office` | Provider-neutral WOPI edge for the default-OFF Office foundation (V220/V224). `Handler` exposes CheckFileInfo/GetFile/PutFile and opaque lock operations only after `feature.drive` + `feature.office_editing`; every callback hashes its launch token, calls `office_wopi_file_query`, rechecks the current Drive capability/fence/provider entitlement, validates Collabora proof-key signatures from a bounded cached HTTPS discovery document, and streams bytes through Drive's existing staged-chunk/version/branch authority. `LiveProviderProber` performs bounded SSRF-safe discovery/editor/proof qualification; the configure/qualify/activate reducers keep validation and routing separate. It owns protocol limits and translation only; `office_*` reducers own sessions, locks, receipts, conflicts, audit and revocation. Providers receive no DB/object-store credentials. |
| `deploysources` | Concrete read-only D1/D3/D4 projection for the Collabora test vertical: resolves `global`/`china`/`isolated`/`custom` repository posture with `NETWORK_MODE` compatibility and isolated fail-closed behavior; projects URL-reputation/AI configuration from presence/host facts only; and inspects fixed Ubuntu APT/Docker paths without following symlinks or writing host state. It has no fetch/apply/provider interface. `mailserver/deploysources` is the narrow public alias used by `mailctl sources`. |
| `deploysources/hostcontract` | **TestOnly** fixture model of the D4 enrollment/apply/recovery contract (wave-1 unit A4): in-process memory host, plan/apply/resume/rollback receipts and journal, refusal taxonomy and recovery guidance. Production `Plan`/`Apply`/enrollment stay unimplemented and owner-gated; the only production host surface remains `deploysources.InspectUbuntu` (read-only). Production code must not import this package; see the "D4 isolated-fixture contract" section of the managed-host-package-sources plan. |
| `geoip` | DB-IP Lite auto-downloader and geo-lookup for per-IP country codes |
| `guestgate` | **Guest Accounts G0–G4** (spec P11b): the single-source guest route ALLOWLIST + its enforcing middleware. `allowlist.go` — `allowRules` (BETWEEN THE `guest-allowlist-begin`/`-end` MARKERS, the one place a guest route decision is encoded; `scripts/check-guest-doors.sh` reads the same markers) is method+kind(prefix/root-prefix/exact/regex)+pattern tuples: `/chat/` (the guest app; doors are BZ215-denied in the reducers, the route surface itself is open), the live-rail exception (`GET /jmap/ws` + `GET /jmap/eventsource` — chat's push/reconnect), the REAL session routes (`GET /.well-known/jmap`, `GET /auth/me` — there is no `/jmap/session`/`/auth/session`), `POST /auth/refresh` + `POST /auth/logout` (the authserver is the real refresh authority; this gate is only the belt), and three GENERIC Drive routes (`HEAD`/`PUT /drive/chunks/`, `GET /drive/nodes`, `GET /drive/versions/{id}/content`) whose guest containment is the GRANT DOOR, not the route — `drive_member_grant`/`drive_folder_member_grant` refuse guest grantees (BZ215), so a guest's only reachable space membership is the LIVE chat-derivation (`drive_member_role` arm 2), revoked the instant channel membership goes; `POST /drive/native-token` stays denied outright in G0. **G1 pre-auth exception:** exact `GET`+`POST /auth/guest-invite/accept` and the bare-root-only, GET-only `root-prefix` `/guest-invite/` are reachable before the guest has a session; `/api/guest-invite/*`, API suffixes, and wrong methods remain denied, while the authserver's `enumIPGuard` rate-limits the real API routes at origin. `root-prefix` matches the original path before the normal `/api` normalization used by every existing rule kind. **G3 Meet extension:** the allowlist enumerates the current method/path shapes for meeting list/create/detail/join/control/knock/recording/captions/breakouts, the secret-bound public poll GET, and exact `/rtc` plus `/rtc/` (never a blanket future-route prefix). Reducer scoping is the authority: exact slug possession permits prejoin/knock only, while numeric reads and token minting require the guest's live meeting scope. **G4 Drive split:** exact regex `GET /drive/versions/{id}/preview` is added; raw content remains separately reducer-gated by `drive_content_download_require` before version lookup, while native-token and all broader Drive routes stay absent. `GuestAllowed(method, path)` trims an `/api` prefix before matching ordinary rules, so they accept both the `/api`-mounted and bare-root forms; `root-prefix` is the deliberate exception. `middleware.go`'s `Middleware(ch *auth.Chain, next http.Handler, gates ...featureGate)` wraps BOTH gateway dispatch sites (the `/api` mount and the bare-root pinned branch, via `main.go`'s `buildRootDispatch` — closing the E-B1 hole where `POST /jmap` at root bypassed the gate — with the legacy non-GET 308 alias behind the SAME wrap, C-7, so a guest can't use route-existence-via-308 as an oracle the allowlist denial is supposed to hide) and only ever NARROWS: a request carrying no JWT credential (no Bearer header, no `access_token` cookie) skips `ch.AuthenticateRequest` entirely — cannot carry a guest claim, and re-authenticating would double every CalDAV/CardDAV Basic-auth check (2x bcrypt/threat-score/audit, a Task-6 follow-up fix); an authenticated non-guest or unauthenticated request passes through unchanged; a guest claim outside the allowlist gets `http.NotFound` — the SAME emitter the real missing-route path uses, so a blocked guest request is byte-identical to a genuinely nonexistent one (P11b). The production gateway passes its live feature flags as the optional gate: while `feature.guest_accounts` is OFF, every authenticated guest runtime request gets that same 404 before allowlist dispatch, while the unauthenticated invite-accept and landing paths remain reachable. A guest-belt lookup failure (`auth.ErrGuestLookupUnavailable`) is the one place this gate answers 503 (problem+json, since it fronts app-JSON `/api` handlers) rather than passing through; `auth.ErrGuestInactive` (BZ219 at validation — a disabled/expired guest's still-valid access token) gets the exact same 401 shape as any invalid credential, no oracle between "disabled" and "malformed token". See `mailserver/internal/chat/AGENTS.md`'s "Guest doors (G0)" section for the reducer-side BZ215/216/219 contract this allowlist fronts. |
| `handlerutil` | `UpdateBuilder` — generic SQL partial-update helper for admin PATCH handlers |
| `health` | `/health` and `/ready` probe handlers; system metrics collection |
| `imap` | IMAP4rev2 bridge: full protocol server with per-user connection tracking |
| `jmap` | JMAP Core (RFC 8620) + Mail (RFC 8621) + Calendar + Contacts + Tasks dispatcher; Upload/Download; Session endpoint |
| `kql` | KQL (Keyword Query Language) parser + SQL transpiler for eDiscovery Content Search |
| `ldap` | Read-only LDAP GAL server; DN builder; search/filter engine |
| `ldapsync` | LDAP identity sync engine: delta sync, attribute mapping, conflict handling. Legacy sync runs share AD's current-schema `identity_sync_log` projection; the admin read seam is `admin_directory_legacy.go`, which returns the stable console response and fail-closes aggregate log details for domain-scoped admins because the ledger has no domain axis. Same Go-canonical address-write seam as `ad` (create → `identity.ProvisionAccount` with `Email` lifted via the validation-free `.AssumeValidated()` directory escape, `ad_upn` `CanonicalAddress`; UPDATE → `ldaputil.SyncAttrs`). `DryRun`/`Preview` (admin console read paths, zero writes) share the bind+search leg (`searchOnly`) with `sync()` but never call `upsertUser`/`resolveManagerIDs`/the cursor or log writers; `previewRow` classifies a directory user's would-be action via a read-only `users`/`field_policy` lookup. `SetFilter` overrides the default `(objectClass=person)` search filter (`buildSyncFilter` composes it with the incremental `modifyTimestamp` cursor). Connection failures classify as `ConnClassNetwork`/`ConnClassBindAuth` (`ConnError`) for the console's distinct error copy. |
| `ldaputil` | LDAP filter parsing, DN escaping, attribute utilities. `SyncAttrs(email identity.CanonicalAddress, …)` — the UPDATE-path attr map; the `email` column param is `CanonicalAddress` (a raw email fails to compile), so the AD/LDAP UPDATE writer is Go-canonical. |
| `lifecycle` | Dependency-ordered startup/shutdown manager (not yet wired into `main.go`) |
| `linkprotect` | URL rewriting for outbound messages; scan pipeline (Safe Browsing, Spamhaus); click-time redirect handler. **The click verdict (all-errors passthrough; strict_mode unknown→malicious) AND the audit insert now happen in one txn via the `link_protect_check_and_audit` reducer — the audit row is durable BEFORE redirect.** FAIL-CLOSED-TO-WARN: if the decide+audit write fails, the handler shows the WARN interstitial (never a silent redirect without a durable audit row). Go keeps only the HMAC verify + the third-party scanner HTTP calls |
| `mediaevict` | Bounded shared participant-eviction effect (AR-07): `EvictParticipantThrough` kicks `RemoveParticipant` until a reducer-supplied `evict_until` horizon — Twirp `NotFound` (`ParticipantNotFound`) is a successful attempt but never an early exit, any other error surfaces immediately to the domain ledger, waits are exactly `min(5s, remaining)`, and cancellation during a wait stops the timer and returns `ctx.Err()`. Consumer-side `ParticipantRemover` interface only, satisfied by the same `meetRooms` value wired at the composition root; no database access and no domain knowledge — claims, leases, acks and retry ledgers (including the identity session-eviction dead-letter cap and guestlifecycle's schedule) stay with the calling workers. Unexported constructor-injected clock/timer seam; the shared test set asserts fake-time steps, never real durations. Consumers: `mailboxlifecycle`, `identitytransition`. |

error). `decodeMeetBody`/`meetIDBody[T]`/`execMeet`/`queryMeetJSON` are the shared decode/exec/query skeletons (the `chat` `decodeChatBody`/`chatIDBody`/`execChat`/`queryChatJSON` precedent, the reducer FIRST, then `h.rooms.RemoveParticipant`/`DeleteRoom` — a controller error there is 502+logged, never silently swallowed, and never rolls back the DB-side authorization (a retry or the Task 12 reconcile sweep converges). `roomNameForMeeting` is the one small direct `SELECT room_name` read `kick` needs since `meeting_kick`'s Task 4 contract is `RETURNS void` (unlike `meeting_end`, which already returns it). `execIDBoolBodyRoute[T]` (free generic function, not a method — Go methods can't carry their own type params) de-duplicates `lockMeeting`/`setGuestAllowed`'s identical "parse id + one JSON bool field, exec a 3-arg void reducer" shape (the `dupl` linter's own catch). `meetings_test.go`: DB-gated (`DB_DSN`, skip-if-absent, the `chat` `tracer_integration_test.go` precedent) route Test: `mailserver/sql/tests/meet_read.pg`. **`lobby.go` (Task 11)** — two shapes: (1) `registerLobbyRoutes()` (also called from `NewHandler`) wires the authed `POST /meet/{id}/requests/{reqId} {action:"admit"|"deny"}` (`decideJoinRequest` — dispatches to `meeting_admit`/`meeting_deny`, an unrecognized action 400s with NO reducer call) and `POST /meet/{id}/knock` (`knock` — resolves the meeting's slug + caller's own `display_name` in one query, then calls `meeting_join_request_create(slug, name, claims.UserID)` and returns 201 `{requestId, secret}`, the SAME shape the public create route returns — the knock-completion contract: a knocking member rides the public poll route below to completion, never a member-only endpoint of its own). (2) `PublicHandler` — the ONE unauthenticated Meet surface (the `booking.PublicHandler` precedent, `internal/booking/public.go`): `NewPublicHandler(pool, flags, cfg, logger)`, `ServeHTTP` dispatches by method only (`main.go`/Task 13 registers the `{slug}`/`{id}` path patterns onto ONE shared instance, so no path-suffix disambiguation is needed the way `booking` needs it). `handleCreate` (`POST /public/meet/{slug}/join-request {displayName}`) checks the org `meet.guest_access` toggle itself, before any DB call (404 if off — the route-layer half of the anti-oracle; `meeting_join_request_create`'s own BZ185 arm is the reducer-layer half for nonexistent-slug/ended/guestAllowed-false), body-caps at `maxLobbyBodyBytes` (4 KiB, the `booking.maxBookingBodyBytes` precedent), and returns 201 `{requestId, secret}`. `handlePoll` (`GET /public/meet/{slug}/join-request/{id}?secret=…`) has NO guest_access gate (a member-knock row must complete regardless of the org guest toggle; `meeting_join_request_poll`'s own re-check is scoped to guest rows only) — calls `meeting_join_request_poll`, and on `{"state":"admitted","grant":{…}}` mints the token via the SAME `MintToken` Task 9 built and returns `{state:"admitted", token, wsPath:"/rtc", expiresAt}`; every other state passes `{state}` straight through, no token. Neither `PublicHandler` nor the two Task 11 authed routes touch `h.rooms`/`RoomController` — a join-request completion never creates a room (some member already did, by joining). `lobby_test.go`: DB-gated (`DB_DSN`) route tests over the real `meeting_lobby.sql` reducers — the guest happy path (create→admit→poll mints a `g:`-identity, `roomAdmin:false` token), the wrong-secret BZ185 anti-oracle, the guest-kick-then-poll `denied`-with-no-token re-mint-kill, the full member-knock-rides-public-poll round trip (poll returns a `u:`-identity token, never a guest identity), and the `meet.guest_access` OFF split (CREATE 404s, POLL still completes an existing knock). **`webhook.go` (Task 12)**: `(h *Handler) WebhookHandler() http.Handler` — POST /meet/webhook, returned as its OWN `http.Handler` (not an `h.mux` route) since it must run outside `Handler.ServeHTTP`'s claims/feature/configured gate (`main.go`/Task 13 mounts it directly, unauthed by `chain.AuthenticateRequest`). Verifies the LiveKit webhook JWT via `protocol/webhook.ReceiveWebhookEvent` + `auth.NewSimpleKeyProvider(cfg.APIKey, cfg.APISecret)` — a bad signature/checksum is the one case this handler rejects outright (401); everything past that point is "ignore or forward," never reject, per spike §2 finding 2 (webhooks are facts, never authorization). `webhookEvents` is the small set this pillar actually consumes (`participant_joined`/`participant_left`/`room_started`/`room_finished`) — anything else (B2's `egress_*`, `track_*`, unknowns) is a silent 200+debug-log. The UUID defensiveness (eng #10): a non-UUID `event.Room.Name` is 200+debug-log with NO call to `meeting_participant_event` (its `p_room_name` is typed `uuid` — a cast exception there would 500 a retried webhook). A genuine DB fault calling the reducer IS a 500 (the one place this handler 500s on purpose) so LiveKit's own retry redelivers it. **`worker.go` (Task 12)**: `Worker{pool pgxutil.WorkerPool, flags, cfg, rooms, logger}` / `NewWorker(...)` / `(w *Worker) Run(ctx)` — the `drive.Worker`/chat `unfurl.Worker` `Run` precedent (ticker loop, exits on ctx cancel), started unconditionally in `main.go` (Task 13); `tick` itself no-ops unless `feature.meet` is ON AND `cfg.Configured()` (checked BEFORE any pool/RoomController access — `TestWorkerTickNoopWhenDisabledOrUnconfigured` pins this with a nil-wrapped `pgxutil.WorkerPool` that would panic if touched). Every 60s (`workerInterval`) tick: (1) `expireLobby` calls `meeting_requests_expire()` (Task 5's reducer, wired to a caller for the first time here); (2) `deleteEndedButLiveRooms` retries the Task 10 kick/end 502 backstop — any meeting `state='ended'` (bounded to the last 24h, `meeting_list.sql`'s own decluttering window) whose room the SFU's `ListRooms()` still lists gets a `DeleteRoom` retry; (3) `gcLostWebhooks` is the lost-webhook GC (spike §2 finding 2's own backstop): a `state='live'` meeting with zero open participants (`NOT EXISTS … left_at IS NULL`), whose activity high-water mark — `GREATEST(created_at, max(joined_at), max(left_at))` computed via scalar subqueries over ALL participants, not just open ones — is older than `staleWindow` (10 min), AND whose room the SFU no longer lists, gets a synthetic `meeting_participant_event(room_name, 'room_finished')` call. Documented deviation from the plan's literal single-LEFT-JOIN SQL (the plan's own Task 12 section pre-authorizes a rewrite here): a join filtered to `left_at IS NULL` can never see a CLOSED participant's `left_at`, so `max(p.left_at)` inside it is always NULL — the two-scalar-subquery form is the correct implementation of the SAME contract. `fake_rooms_test.go` gained a `rooms []string` field + `setRooms([]string)` setter so `ListRooms()` can report an arbitrary "what the SFU currently lists" set (every prior task's tests default to empty/nil, unaffected). `webhook_test.go`/`worker_test.go`: DB-gated (`DB_DSN`) tests over the real reducers on `mailserver_test2` — webhook tests sign requests exactly the way LiveKit's own `URLNotifier` does (protojson-marshal → sha256+base64 → `AccessToken.SetSha256` → `Authorization` header), proving the real wire contract; `httpCaptionsClient` is a ~40-line loopback client to `asrbot`'s control API, constructed in `main.go` only when `ASR_URL` is set). `worker.go` gains four new arms inside the SAME `feature.meet && Configured()` gated section, never gated on `feature.meet_breakouts`/`feature.meet_captions` themselves (D11 extended): `gcClosedBreakoutRooms` + `reconcileBreakoutPresence` (Task 5 — child-room GC sharing `retryDeleteForRooms` with the parent-room GC leg, and the assignment↔presence healer with a one-tick `absentSince` grace) and `reconcileCaptionsCrashed` + `reconcileCaptionsOrphaned` (Task 14 — bidirectional, F5: a crashed bot's stale `captionsOn` flag flips off past a `captionsSince` grace window, and every orphaned live session gets a retried `Stop`). `TestEveryRevokePathEvicts` (Task 5) grows four cases ("breakout move"/"breakout close"/"end with breakouts open"/"kick while in breakout"). `main.go` (package-external wiring, this row's convention) constructs `meetCaptions` (`httpCaptionsClient`) only when `meetCfg.ASRURL != ""`, registers the `"asr"` health probe the same way `"sfu"`/`"egress"` are registered, and passes it into both `meet.NewHandler` and `meet.NewWorker` beside the existing `rooms`/`egress` construction. Deploy (Task 16, outside this package): `setjoin-asr-bot.service` + `setjoin-funasr.service` + `asrbot.env.template` + `lib-livekit.sh` extensions (`render_asrbot_unit`/`render_funasr_unit`/`render_asrbot_env`/`install_funasr_models`) + `install.sh`'s `MEET_CAPTIONS_ENABLED` leg + `test-livekit-render.sh` growth. Full B3 contract, the F1/D10/D12/D13/E5 breakout-choreography detail, the F5/D4/D6/D8 captions-contract detail, and the B3 Task 18 integration-gate + honesty ledger (the live F14 `MoveParticipant`-unimplemented proof RUN against a real pinned SFU, the live FunASR round-trip + GPU sizing NOT run — owed pre-flip): `mailserver/internal/meet/AGENTS.md`'s "Breakouts (B3a)" + "Captions (B3b)" + "B3 Task 18" sections; the `asrbot/` sidecar module itself: `asrbot/AGENTS.md`. **W4 native-RLS Go leg (completes V134):** every user-request-path reducer/table call in this package now runs GUC-scoped via `dbscope.RunUserScoped(ctx, h.pool, claims.UserID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {...})` — through the `execMeet`/`queryMeetJSON`/`roomNameForMeeting`/`queryStringColumnScoped` choke points (`meetings.go`) or inline where a route needs more than one statement (`createMeeting`/`join`/`kick`/`endMeeting`, `lobby.go`'s `knock`, and every route in `breakouts.go`/`captions.go`/`recording.go`). Exempt (documented in code, allowlisted by name in the guard): `webhook.go`'s `handleWebhook`/`handleEgressEvent` (the LiveKit webhook receiver runs on the APP pool with **no caller GUC** — a webhook has no user — which is WHY V134 converted `meeting_participant_event`/`meeting_recording_event` to SECURITY DEFINER, not because this handler happens to run on `workerPool`); `lobby.go`'s `PublicHandler.handleCreate`/`handlePoll` (the one unauthenticated Meet surface — `meeting_join_request_create`/`_poll` are already SECURITY DEFINER, self-scoped by slug/secret, never by caller identity); `worker.go`/`worker_breakouts.go`/`worker_captions.go`/`worker_recordings.go` run on `workerPool` (BYPASSRLS) like every other maintenance worker. Guarded by `scripts/check-rls-scope-wrap.sh`; live-proven (not just shape-checked) by `rls_scope_live_test.go`'s `TestRLSScopeWrapMeetingCreate`, which runs the real `Handler` against a pool connected AS the constrained `setjoin_app` role. |
| `guestinvite` | Shared invite-credential seam for new admin invites, approved member requests, and explicit existing-guest recovery. `Prepare` mints 32 random bytes, stores only SHA-256 through worker-owned `guest_invite_create` in the caller transaction, captures the reducer-returned exact invite id as `int64`/decimal string, and retains the raw token only in an in-memory `Prepared`; its target must already be invited with sign-in disabled. The requested TTL is capped by the guest-account expiry in Go and re-enforced in the reducer. `Send` is called strictly after commit and is the sole token-bearing email construction path. Sender/auth URL/TTL resolve from authoritative `system_config`. |
| `guestlifecycle` | Guest G5/G6 unconditional expiry/SFU-eviction reconciler. `NewWorker(workerPool, rooms, logger).Run(ctx)` immediately and hourly calls worker-owned `guest_expire_due`; a 5s leg drains exact `guest_live_eviction_intents` room/identity targets with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`. Disable, expiry, and re-invite snapshot every relevant parent/breakout identity while token minting shares the guest lifecycle lock. Successful and Twirp-not-found removals remain retryable through the maximum 4h Meet token lifetime plus 5m; incomplete `reinvite` intents additionally fence recovered Meet token mint. `DrainUser` is the admin handler's post-commit fast path for disable/recovery (and any future handler-driven expire); recovery calls it before mail queueing. The durable worker is authoritative and never feature-toggle-gated. |
| `mdm` | Mobile Device Management: check-in, compliance evaluation, wipe state machine, device policy push, MAM (Mobile App Management) |
| `metrics` | Prometheus metrics exposition (`/metrics`) with API key auth. Its unconditional 30-second identity collector uses the worker pool to publish only bounded aggregates: source/cursor/inbox health, conflicts, critical shadow divergence, transition/effect age and backlog, canary size, and retained auth/grant event totals. It never labels a person, external subject, link, transition, provider display name, or error text. |
| `middleware` | `RequireAuth` (authn-only + DomainScope, the SOLE outer auth gate), `RequirePermAudited` / `RequireAnyPermAudited` (granular perm enforcement, audit-logged via `AuditEvent` callback), `RequireApp` (B8.4 per-app entitlement gate), `PoolEntitlementChecker`, `DeviceTrustGate` (`device_trust_gate.go` — wraps shared `RequireDeviceCompliance` behind `feature.device_trust_enforcement`, **DEFAULT OFF** ⇒ pure pass-through no-op; composed into `requireAuth` in `main.go`). **`DeviceLockdownGate`** (CA-2, `device_trust_gate.go`) — the strict CA-2 gate layered AFTER `DeviceTrustGate` on the authenticated chain; gated `feature.device_lockdown` (DEFAULT OFF). Delegates to `deviceLockdownGate(toggles, resolve, excludePresent, logger)` (testable inner fn). Build-gate (locked decision 9): **refuses to enforce unless the CA-0a break-glass exclude list (`ca_break_glass_user_ids` in `system_config`) is present and non-empty** — a global lockdown can never lock out every admin. Gate logic: toggle OFF → pass-through; toggle ON + no exclude list → pass-through + WARN; toggle ON + exclude list present: `bound && state=="approved"` → 200, else → 403 BZ095. Wraps `DeviceCredentialResolver.Resolve` by reading `userID` from the request context (set by `RequireAuth` upstream). **`DeviceRevocationGate`** (offline #2b, `device_revocation.go`) — P2b revocation denylist: maintains an in-process cache of revoked-device hex-SHA256 fingerprint hashes (keyed to match `user_devices.fingerprint_hash`); cache loaded from the pool on first use and refreshed on `pg_notify` + a short TTL tick. **FAIL-CLOSED on first-load error** (startup error → deny all requests; a denylist that fails to load must not silently permit). Gated by `feature.device_trust_enforcement` (same toggle as `DeviceTrustGate` — DEFAULT OFF → pure pass-through). Wired in `main.go` as `DeviceRevocationGate(pool, featureToggles, logger)`. `RequireReason(perm, pool)` (`reason.go`) — reads `audit_policy.reason_required_areas` per-request; if the permission's area prefix is listed, requires a non-empty `X-Audit-Reason` header (missing → 400 + core `sensitive_action.reason_missing` audit row via `WriteAutonomous`); present reason is stored in context via `ReasonFromContext`. Composed onto sensitive write routes (roles, identity, audit, security) in `main.go`. `RequireAdmin`/`RequireRole`/`RequireSuperAdmin`/`RequireAuditor`/`RequireSplitPermAudited` were removed in the RBAC cut-over — `RequirePermAudited` is now the SOLE authorization gate. Rate limiter, security headers. |
| `migration` | IMAP / EWS / M365 Graph / PST connectors; migration orchestrator; ICS/VCF import; credentials encryption; per-user and admin-initiated flows. **Every `migration_jobs` row mutation flows through the `migration_orchestrate` reducer (one atomic phase transition: status + cursor/delta/progress merge + error-log append + terminal NULLing); `RecoverStuckJobs` calls `migration_recover_stuck`.** Complete/fail/recovery also enroll stable R4 terminal facts in that same transaction. The reducer returns typed user/mailbox subject metadata plus `publication_enrolled`; only flag-OFF results take the post-commit `DispatchForUser|Mailbox` compatibility path. The connector/MinIO fetch loop stays in Go; reads (`loadJob`/`loadCursor`/`getMailboxID`) stay raw. Per-source wiring is behind a `ConnectorFactory` interface (`imapFactory`/`ewsFactory`/`m365Factory`, dispatched by a factory map) that owns each connector's config-merge + decrypt + auth strategy, so the orchestrator no longer special-cases source types — the legacy pipe-delimited IMAP path was folded into the same `createConnector`+`runConnectorPhase`. `resolveConfig` unwraps the `MigrationJobConfig` wrapper for non-global-auth jobs (producers store the wrapper; the factories decode a bare connector config — without the unwrap a job carrying its own credentials decoded to an empty config; legacy bare blobs still pass through). **EWS boundary:** administrator-approved exact public/private profiles share `shared/ssrf` dial-time address enforcement; redirects stay disabled, shared credentials remain bound to their immutable approved origin, and durable reducer leases/quotas bound work. The client rejects non-HTTP(S), enforces connect/TLS/header/total timeouts, caps every SOAP response at 16 MiB, and emits an explicit `Exchange2016` request-version header for GetFolder/GetItem/SyncFolderItems compatibility. `InsecureSkipVerify` remains explicit admin opt-in. **Self-service EWS credential boundary:** `user.StartMigration` requires the caller's OWN complete username/password (400 `self-service EWS migration requires your own username and password`, unconditional — a URL-only request must never inherit the shared service account) and stores the `MigrationJobConfig.SelfService` marker; the `ewsFactory` merge then overwrites the seeded global username/password with the caller's and drops any impersonation principal before connector construction, refusing fail-closed when caller credentials are incomplete. The admin path (`AdminAPI.Start`) is unchanged: global service-account credentials + optional per-job `SourceEmail` impersonation, while the global network policy (immutable origin, TLS floor, private-network exceptions) still layers onto every job — credentials are the forbidden inheritance, not the policy. Pinned at the connector boundary by `TestEWSFactorySelfServiceCredentialsAtConnectorBoundary` (both AuthModes × both `feature.mail_migration_source_identity` states) and at ingress by `TestStartMigration_EWSRequiresCallerCredentials`. |
