- `GET/POST/DELETE /auth/me/smime/certs` — User self-service S/MIME cert upload/delete

**S/MIME recipient certs / Contacts' Certificates** (`admin_smime_recipient_certs.go`, `RecipientCertHandler`):
- `GET /auth/me/smime/recipient-certs` — List the caller's saved recipient certs (`owner_user_id`-scoped); populates `isEncryptCapable` from the parsed cert.
- `POST /auth/me/smime/recipient-certs` — Import a PEM cert (multipart `{cert, label?}`); validates: parseable PEM, not expired, `IsEncryptionCapable`, has SAN email. Upserts by `(owner_user_id, email, fingerprint)` (sha256 of DER), setting `source='imported'`. cert_pem NOT returned in JSON. 2 MB body limit.
- `DELETE /auth/me/smime/recipient-certs/{id}` — Delete; ownership-scoped (`WHERE id=$1 AND owner_user_id=$2 RETURNING id`); 404 if not found/wrong owner.
- Schema: `V025__recipient_smime_certs.sql` + `V030__recipient_smime_certs_label.sql` (adds nullable `label TEXT` column). UI: `settings/security/+page.svelte` Contacts' Certificates card (SP1 Phase 6).

**S/MIME trust store** (`admin_trust_anchors.go`, `TrustAnchorHandler` — Mail console, `domains.read` permission):
- `GET /admin/smime/trust-anchors` — List anchors visible to the caller (domain-scoped: global admin sees all; scoped admin sees domain_id IS NULL ∪ own domain)
- `POST /admin/smime/trust-anchors` — Import a trust anchor (multipart `{label, cert}`); validates PEM + rejects already-expired certs; on conflict (same fingerprint + domain) updates label
- `DELETE /admin/smime/trust-anchors/{id}` — Delete an anchor; scoped admin can only delete their domain's rows
- Per-tenant `smime_trust_anchors` table (`domain_id NULL` = deployment-wide; `domain_id = N` = tenant-scoped). Inbound verify chains against bundled Mozilla roots ∪ deployment-wide anchors ∪ recipient-domain anchors. Hot-reloaded on `smime_trust_changed` pg_notify after any write. Schema: `sql/dbschema/V015__smime_trust_anchors.sql`. Admin console page: `webmail/src/routes/admin/(mail)/smime-trust/`.

**MDM** (`admin_mdm.go`, `admin_mam.go`):
- `GET/PUT /admin/mdm/policies` — Device management policies
- `GET /admin/mdm/devices` — Enrolled device list
- `POST /admin/mdm/devices/{id}/wipe` — Initiate remote wipe (full or selective)
- `GET/PUT /admin/mam/policies` — Mobile App Management policies
- All MDM **and** MAM routes are registered in `main.go` as `requireAuth(RequirePermAudited(mam.read|mam.write,...))` — guarded-by-construction. Read routes require `mam.read`, mutations require `mam.write` (MDM reuses the MAM scopes — one mobile-management console). The handlers carry **no inline auth**; see the enforcement contract below.

**Device Lockdown** (`admin_device_lockdown.go`) — CA-2 admin API for device lifecycle management:
- `GET  /admin/devices/queue?state=&q=&limit=` — Org-wide device approval queue (default `state=enrolled`; `q=` email/name prefix filter; `limit=` capped 100/500). Response is always a JSON array (empty array, not null). `mam.read`.
- `GET  /admin/devices?user_id=` — Per-user device list (all states). `mam.read`.
- `POST /admin/devices/{id}/enroll|approve|revoke` — Lifecycle transition via `device_enroll_transition` reducer; optional `{"reason":"…"}` body. BZ095 (illegal transition) → 409 with reducer HINT; infra error → 500. `mam.write`.
- Perm-gating at the route; no inline auth. Actor `UserID` stamped by `auth.UserFromContext`. Reducer writes `device_enroll_events` audit rows in-transaction.

**Permission enforcement is route-declared, never inline.** Admin handlers in `admin/` assume an authenticated, authorized caller. The permission decision is made once, at route registration in `cmd/mailserverd/main.go`, via `middleware.RequirePermAudited` / `RequireAnyPermAudited` (`internal/middleware/console_guard.go`), which audit every denial as `console.denied`. `RequirePermAudited` is the **sole authorization gate** — the enum-role wrappers (`requireAdmin`/`requireSuperAdmin`/`requireReadOnly`/`requireAuditor`/`requireAdminOrAuditor`) were deleted in the RBAC cut-over; `RequireSplitPermAudited` was also removed with the `users.write`/`mail.write` umbrella perms (V022 dropped them). **`requireSuperAdmin` collapse (deliberate):** routes formerly behind `requireSuperAdmin` are now reachable by any role that holds the granular perm (e.g. `security.write` → Security Admin for IP-rules, `compliance.write` → Compliance Admin for eDiscovery) — this is the approved SoD outcome. Do **not** add `if !claims.IsAdmin` / `AdminRole` checks inside handlers — they bypass the audit trail and the granular RBAC registry (`shared/permissions`). The domain-scope tenancy checks (`DomainScopeFromContext`) and the `admin_templates.go` data-shape branch (admins see all rows vs enabled-only) are not permission decisions and are intentionally left inline. `users.impersonate` is gated at the mailserver edge; `org_chart.go` is gated on `users.read`.

**Spam** (`admin_spam.go`):
- `GET /admin/spam` — ML spam classifier dashboard
- `DELETE/disable/enable /admin/spam/models/{userId}` — Per-user model management

**TLS** (`admin_tls.go`, `admin_tls_policy.go`):
- `GET/PUT /admin/tls/config` — TLS configuration. `GET` redacts secret `tls_*` rows to `[ENCRYPTED]` (was returning every value raw); `PUT` routes through the `ConfigStore` (authoritative `encrypted` column), still `tls_`-prefix-filtered. The admin form persists `tls_autocert_challenge`, `tls_autocert_domains`, `tls_autocert_email`, `tls_dns_provider`, and `tls_autocert_cache_dir`.
- `GET /admin/tls/status` — Certificate status plus the persisted form values even when ACME is disabled, so saving with challenge `none` survives a reload. `getConfig` reads JSONB scalar values with `value #>> '{}'` so strings reach the handler without JSON quote artifacts; `config_store_test.go` pins the exact keys, decoded status, disabled-state round trip, and out-of-prefix refusal.
- `POST /admin/tls/import-cert` — Import a certificate
- `GET/PUT /admin/outbound-tls-policy` — Per-domain outbound TLS policy

**Outbound queue** (`admin_queue.go`):
- `GET /admin/queue/summary` — Queue statistics
- `GET /admin/queue` — Queue listing
- `POST /admin/queue/{id}/retry` — Force retry
- `POST /admin/queue/{id}/bounce` — Force bounce with NDR

**Alerts** (`admin_alerts.go`):
- `GET /admin/alerts` — Active alerts
- `POST /admin/alerts/ack` — Acknowledge alerts
- `POST /admin/alerts/unsuppress` — Remove suppression
- `GET/PUT /admin/alerts/config` — Alert thresholds and notification settings

**Reports** (`admin_reports.go`):
- `GET /admin/reports/summary` — Mail traffic summary report

**Monitor** (`admin_monitor.go`):
- `GET /admin/monitor/live` — Live SMTP/IMAP connection stream (SSE)

**Auth log** (`admin_auth_log.go`):
- `GET /admin/auth-log` — Authentication event log

**Threat read APIs (B6.1)** (`admin_threat.go`):
- `GET /admin/threat/{view}` — Generic threat-flow read handler. `view` is one of:
  - `attempts` → `auth_attempts` (sourceIp, targetUsername, protocol, outcome, failureReason, geoCountry, occurredAt)
  - `lockouts` → `auth_lockouts` (lockoutKey, failedCount, lockedUntil, escalationLevel, updatedAt)
  - `feed-blocks` → `threat_feed_blocks` (cidr, source, addedAt)
  - `rate-counters` → `rate_limit_counters` (key, count, windowStart, blockedUntil)
- Response envelope: `{rows:[...], total, limit, offset}` matching the M1 Table contract.
- Pagination: `limit` (default 100, max 500), `offset` (default 0); bad values clamped, never 500.
- Filters: `q` (text search, per-view), `since` (interval for attempts), `sort` (allow-listed column), `dir` (ASC/DESC).
- Sort column allow-listed per view; arbitrary user input falls back to default order (OWASP A03 injection prevention).
- Auth: `requireAuditor` (super_admin or auditor role) + `adminLimit` rate limiter — same guard as auth-log.
- All queries are parameterised; no user input is string-concatenated into SQL.

**IP allow/block rules (B6.2)** (`admin_ip_rules.go`):
- `GET /admin/threat/ip-rules` — List all IP rules from the `ip_rules` table. Response: `{rows:[{id,cidr,action,description,expiresAt,createdAt,createdBy}], total, limit, offset}`. Auth: `requireAuditor` + `adminLimit`.
- `POST /admin/threat/ip-rules` — Create a new IP rule. Body: `{cidr, action, description, expiresAt?}`. CIDR validated via `net/netip`; action must be `allow` or `block`; `created_by` always taken from auth context (never the request body — R2). Writes `ip_rule_create` row to `admin_audit_log`. Returns `{id}` with 201. Auth: `requireSuperAdmin` + `adminLimit`.
- `DELETE /admin/threat/ip-rules/{id}` — Delete an IP rule by numeric `id`. Returns 204 on success, 404 if not found. Writes `ip_rule_delete` row to `admin_audit_log`. Auth: `requireSuperAdmin` + `adminLimit`.
- Audit log writes are best-effort (errors suppressed); the main operation is not blocked if audit fails.

**Config** (`admin_config.go`, `admin_config_backup.go`):
- `GET/GET/{key}/PUT/{key} /admin/system/config` — System config key management (super-admin)
- `GET /admin/system/config/export` / `POST /admin/system/config/import` — Config snapshot backup/restore

**Welcome email** (`admin_welcome_email.go`):
- `GET/PUT /admin/system/welcome-email` — edits the `welcome_email` `system_config` key (enabled, default_locale, per-locale subject/body/optional HTML). GET gates on `PermSystemConfig`; PUT on super-admin + `PermSystemConfig` (records `updated_by`). `POST .../test` sends the supplied (possibly unsaved) content to the calling admin's own mailbox (preview). Sender logic lives in `internal/notify` (`SendWelcomeEmail`, idempotency-guarded; `SendWelcomeEmailWithConfig`, the test path).

**Webhooks** (`admin_webhooks.go`):
- `GET/POST/DELETE/rotate-secret/deliveries /admin/webhooks` — System-level webhook management
- `GET /admin/webhooks/events` — Webhook event catalog

**Anti-phishing** (`admin_antiphish.go`):
- `GET/PUT /admin/anti-phish` — Anti-phishing configuration

**Branding** (`admin_branding.go`):
- `GET/POST /admin/branding` — Logo and platform name customization

**Departments** (`admin_departments.go`):
- `GET/tree/POST/PUT/{id}/DELETE/{id}/assign-user /admin/departments` — Department hierarchy management

**Updates feed** (`admin_updates.go`):
- `GET /admin/updates/summary` — Device update summary
- `GET /admin/updates/devices` — Per-device update status
- `POST /admin/updates/push` — Push update to devices
- `POST /admin/updates/airgap/upload` — Upload air-gap update bundle (super-admin)

**Setup** (`admin_setup.go`):
- `GET /setup/status` — First-boot initialization status
- Public setup HTTP is read-only (`GET /setup/status`). First-admin creation is local-only through `mailctl bootstrap-admin` and the atomic `ensure_default_admin` reducer.
- The local Unix control socket alone mounts `POST /setup/preflight` for restore preview and `POST /setup/restore` for synchronous DR. Both retain the no-users safety rail; restore uses `StartRestoreJob`, bounded request-independent `FinishRestoreJob`, and SHA-256/canonical-hash validation. A full restore copies blobs first while empty, then `mailctl` performs the owner-side `pg_restore`.

### AI Assist — `assist/` (B4.3)

- `POST /assist/complete` — Full-response AI assist (gated, rate-limited). Requires `Authorization: Bearer` JWT. Checks the registered DEFAULT-OFF `toggle.FeatureAssist` data-egress boundary via `ToggleAssistGate`; returns 403 if off, missing, or invalidated by a failed authoritative toggle reload. Chat translation uses the same constant and failure posture. Decodes `{messages, context?, locale?}` envelope, validates via `shared/assist.Validate`, loads config per-call via `LoadConfig` (503 if `ErrNotConfigured`), calls `shared/assist.OpenAICompatible.Complete`, returns `{content}` or 502 on upstream error. Upstream errors never leak raw provider output.
- `POST /assist/stream` — Reserved SSE streaming path; always returns 501 Not Implemented. See `docs/specs/ASSIST.md` for the full endpoint contract.

### User-Facing REST API — `user/`

- **Rules** (`user/rules.go`) — `GET/POST/GET/{id}/PUT/{id}/DELETE/{id}/test /settings/rules`; rule template instantiation
- **Signatures** (`user/signatures.go`) — `GET/PUT/DELETE /settings/signatures`; List + Put carry `default_for` (A1; new-vs-reply default discriminator ∈ new/reply/both/none, validated on PUT). Put runs the whole mutation in **one transaction** (the `is_default` clear, the upsert `RETURNING id`, then — when `default_for != 'none'` — a `SELECT set_signature_default(user_id, sig_id, default_for)` call): the atomic single-role flip ("at most one signature holds each new/reply/both role per user") is delegated to the `set_signature_default` reducer rather than the plain upsert, so concurrent PUTs can't both win a role or leave a window with no default
- **Sender list** (`user/sender_list.go`) — `GET/PUT /settings/senders`, `POST /settings/senders/never-block` — block/allow lists
- **IMAP settings** (`user/imap_settings.go`) — `GET/PUT /settings/imap` — per-user IMAP folder visibility
- **View preferences** (`user/view_prefs.go`) — `GET/PUT /settings/view` — density, sort order
- **External identity labels** (`user/external_sender.go`) — `GET/PUT /settings/external-sender` — default-on per-user switch for outside sender/recipient labels and warnings; the SPA calls it under `/api/settings/external-sender`
- **Undo-send** (`user/undo_send.go`) — `GET/PUT /settings/undo-send`; `POST /messages/{id}/undo` — cancel queued message
- **Message recall** (`user/recall.go`) — `POST /messages/{id}/recall` — attempt recall of already-delivered message
- **Newsletter unsubscribe** (`user/newsletter.go`) — `POST /messages/{emailId}/unsubscribe` — one-click list-unsubscribe
- **Newsletter bundle prefs** (`user/newsletter_bundles.go`) — `GET /settings/newsletter-bundles` returns `{bundleNewsletters, optedOutLists}`; `PUT /settings/newsletter-bundles` merges `bundleNewsletters` bool and `optedOutLists` string array into `users.view_prefs` (keys `bundleNewsletters` / `newsletterOptouts`). Default: bundleNewsletters=true, optedOutLists=[]. Consumed by the webmail settings Switch (B5.3) and the `newsletter_bundles_list` reducer which reads the same view_prefs key.
- **Delivery-path inspector** (`user/trace.go`, `user/owned_message.go`, `internal/received/`) — `GET /messages/{id}/trace` — ownership-checked via `ResolveOwnedMessage` (mailbox→user JOIN; IDOR-safe 404), `recover()`-guarded (degrades, never 500). **Direction-discriminated by the Sent location tag** (`message_tags`→`tags`, `name='Sent' AND tag_type='system' AND exclusive_group='location'`): **inbound** → the stored raw blob's `Received:` chain parsed (`internal/received`, stdlib-only, resource-bounded ≤1 MB/≤100 hops) into a hop timeline with per-hop latency + a self-identity trust anchor; **outbound** (Sent) → per-recipient delivery status read from `delivery_attempts` (`DISTINCT ON (recipient_email) … ORDER BY attempt_number DESC` — the latest state per recipient; NEVER `COUNT`/`SUM` over the append-only rows) overlaid on `outbound_queue.rcpt_addrs`, with an `outbound_queue`-derived overall status, sorted problems-first, plus retention-skew + undo-send fallbacks. A `user`-local `outboundTrace` type marshals to the same `direction`-tagged JSON the webmail `DeliveryPanel` branches on. Reads run on `workerPool` (ownership by the `mailbox_id` filter). Phase 2b (admin per-recipient drill-down on `delivery_attempts`) is a documented fast-follow, NOT in this surface.
- **Raw email download** (`user/raw_email.go`) — `GET /messages/{emailId}/download` — download RFC 2822 `.eml`
- **Save all attachments** (`user/attachments_zip.go`) — `GET /messages/{id}/attachments.zip` — streams a server-side zip of a message's non-inline attachments. A single bound ownership read (`message_attachments` → `messages` on `(id, received_at)` → `mailboxes` on `mb.user_id`) supplies both the R1/R4 ownership gate and the attachment list; zero rows → 404 (not-owned and nothing-to-zip are indistinguishable). Runs on `workerPool` (BYPASSRLS, cross-mailbox-for-one-user; allowlisted `BYPASS` in `scripts/rls-coverage-allowlist.txt`). Blob bytes are read through a `blobGetter` func seam (production wraps `minio.GetObject`, whose `*minio.Object` is an `io.ReadCloser`; the test injects canned bytes — an interface returning `*minio.Object` can't be faked since `minio.Object` has no public constructor). Zip entry names are `sanitizeFilename`'d (zip-slip hygiene) + deduped on collision. Webmail: a "Save all (N)" native download anchor in `ReadingPane.svelte`, shown only when >1 visible attachment (`inbox.save_all_attachments`).
- **User webhooks** (`user/user_webhooks.go`) — `GET/POST/DELETE /auth/me/webhooks` — per-user outbound webhook management
- **App passwords** (`user/app_passwords.go`) — `GET/POST/DELETE /auth/me/app-passwords` — IMAP/SMTP app-specific passwords
- **Devices** (`user/devices.go`) — `GET /user/devices` — active device listing (the per-device force-logout `DELETE /user/devices/{fingerprint}/sessions` was removed by owner decision A7 — it never worked; session revocation lives in `user/sessions.go`)
- **Offline size estimate** (`user/offline_size.go`, offline #1b) — `GET /offline/size-estimate` — per-cumulative-age-window (`7d`/`30d`/`90d`/`180d`/`365d`/`all`) message count + body-only byte totals (`octet_length(body_text)+octet_length(body_html)`, attachments excluded) for the native Storage-slider "needed on disk" preview. One aggregate scan on `workerPool` (BYPASSRLS) with an explicit R1 `mb.user_id` filter (cross-mailbox-for-one-user); allowlisted `BYPASS` in `scripts/rls-coverage-allowlist.txt`. No migration — the window pref reuses `users.view_prefs`.
- **Mailbox quota self-view** (`user/quota.go`) — `GET /user/quota` — this user's own storage total (`SUM(used_bytes)`/`SUM(quota_bytes)` across their mailboxes), for the Settings Cloud storage bar. Reads ONLY `mailboxes` (no native RLS — only messages/message_tags are FORCE'd), scoped by R1 `WHERE user_id = $1`, so it runs on the regular app `pool` (not `workerPool`); not flagged by `check-rls-coverage.sh`. No migration.
- **Login history** (`user/login_history.go`) — `GET /user/login-history` — sign-in events (client/auth_method/current-session flag/failed_distinct summary); gated by `audit.read`
- **Secure account** (`user/secure_account.go`) — `POST /user/sign-ins/secure-account` — revoke all sessions + set `must_change_password` in one atomic tx via the `secure_account` reducer (BZ084 on missing user); Tier-3 type-to-confirm `SECURE` dialog
- **Active sessions** (`user/sessions.go`) — `GET /user/sessions` (list live oauth families + web sessions; R1), `DELETE /user/sessions/{id}` (per-session revoke; R3 ownership in WHERE), `POST /user/sessions/revoke-all` (sign out everywhere in one tx)
- **Notification preferences** (`user/notification_prefs.go`) — `GET/PUT /user/notification-preferences` — push, email, DND settings, and the Track A `read_receipt_policy` (`ask`/`always`/`never`; validated on PUT; empty preserves the existing value on update, defaults to `ask` on insert)
- **Automatic replies / Out-of-Office (A5)** (`user/auto_reply.go`) — `GET/PUT /user/automatic-replies`. `AutoReplyHandler.Get`/`Set` translate to the `get_vacation`/`set_vacation` reducers. The target mailbox is the caller's **primary mailbox**, resolved from `claims.UserID` (`WHERE user_id=$1 AND is_primary`) — never from the request body (R2). The body (`AutoReply`) carries enabled, subject, separate internal (`body_internal`/`_html`) and external (`body_external`/`_html`) reply bodies, schedule (`start_date`/`end_date` as `*string` cast `::date`), and `external_audience` ∈ all/contacts/none (validated at the handler; BZ046/BZ047 from the reducer map to 400). GET returns privacy-safe defaults (`external_audience:'all'`) when no OOO row exists. vacation_settings is not a synced JMAP object — no state bump.
- **Security score** (`user/security_score.go`) — `GET /user/security-score` — MFA, app-password, device-session posture score
- **Org chart** (`user/org_chart.go`) — `GET /auth/me/org-chart` — reporting hierarchy view
- **Quarantine self-service** (`user/quarantine.go`) — `GET/count/release/reject /quarantine`. **R3 (no TOCTOU):** both `Release` and `Reject` fold ownership (`mailbox_id IN (SELECT id FROM mailboxes WHERE user_id=$caller)`) into the SAME mutating UPDATE and gate on its `RETURNING` (`pgx.ErrNoRows` → 403) — no separate ownership COUNT before the mutation. `Release`'s post-release tag rewrite (drop `Quarantined`, re-add the `original_tag`) runs in a `dbscope.RunScoped(mailboxID)` tenant tx and sources `message_tags.received_at` from the message row (the composite `(message_id, received_at)` FK to the partitioned `messages` table forbids a literal `now()`).
- **Spam report** (`user/report.go`) — `POST /report` — user reports spam/phishing/concern. Log-only stub: validates the type and writes one `admin_audit_log` row (`action='report_<type>'`); nothing consumes that row yet — it does **not** train the Bayesian classifier (the `internal/spam` trainer does not read these audit rows). Wiring the report into spam training is not yet implemented.

### Migration Connectors — `migration/`

- **Source session contract** (`migration/source_session.go`) — every resumable
  IMAP/EWS/M365 connector implements `SourceSession`: source identity,
  provider-owned durable/legacy restore and an ordered stream of closed
  `SourceBatch` pages. The stream has capacity one and blocks the next provider
  read until the orchestrator acknowledges the current page. `Orchestrator`
  remains the only persistence owner: it applies the complete page, commits its
  opaque candidate through `migration_checkpoint_commit_v2`, then acknowledges
  the producer. Cancellation stops provider reads and uses a bounded fresh-context
  join; IMAP additionally terminates its socket because go-imap `UidFetch` has no
  context API. An incomplete page never advances the cursor. IMAP candidates are
  bounded UID ranges with UIDVALIDITY, EWS pages use stable folder IDs while
  dual-writing the old display-name delta projection, and M365 pages retain Graph
  continuation/delta links per category; its existing ordinary calendar and China
  contact pagination replays the final provider page instead of inventing a delta
  token. Per-page dedupe is bounded at 100;
  reducer operation IDs own cross-page replay. Adapters never receive a database
  persistence dependency, and the orchestrator has no concrete connector switch.
- **IMAP connector** (`migration/imap_client.go`) — Username/password auth; folder list; incremental `FetchItemsCursor`; Docker-based integration test
- **EWS connector** (`migration/ews_client.go`) — Exchange Web Services; NTLM + Basic auth; folder enumeration; item streaming; delta link sync. NTLM is connection-bound, so its safe transport deliberately disables HTTP/2 while preserving the shared dial-time address policy, disabled redirects and response limits. GetFolder/GetItem/SyncFolderItems carry an explicit `Exchange2016` request-version header; omitting it can make a modern server return an old-schema success-shaped response with no item instead of the requested MIME. The optional impersonation address is XML-escaped before entering the SOAP header. Mail parsing accepts Exchange's MIME-bearing `Message`, meeting-message/request/response/cancellation and `PostItem` variants; treating every mail folder object as only `Message` drops valid items and makes a mixed `GetItem` page fail its identity/count fence. The real-server E2E config carries explicit `private_hosts`/`private_cidrs` and optional strict-TLS-negative, bounded-concurrency and exact synthetic oversize-item inputs. Its delta lane recreates the connector from the returned cursor to prove restart/resume; private Exchange access never weakens the default public-only policy.
- **Microsoft 365 connector** (`migration/m365_client.go`) — Graph API OAuth2 client-credentials; mailbox enumeration; message/folder streaming; delta links; `M365Connect`/`M365Callback` OAuth2 flow (`GET /admin/migration/m365/connect`, `callback`)
- **PST writer** (`pst/writer.go`) — Export mailbox content to Microsoft PST format
- **ICS import** (`migration/ics_import.go`) — iCalendar bulk import to calendar
- **VCF import** (`migration/vcf_import.go`) — vCard bulk import to contacts
- **Folder i18n** (`migration/folder_i18n.go`) — Localised IMAP folder name normalization (Sent Items → Sent, etc.)
- **Admin action endpoints** (`migration/admin_api.go` — `AdminAPI`, `NewAdminAPI(orch)`) — the admin console's two action buttons: `POST /admin/migration/test-connection` (body `{source_type, host/port/ssl | url/username/password}`; dials the admin-supplied source with a 10 s bound and returns `{ok:true}` or the connector's error as plain text 502; IMAP without credentials = reachability/TLS-only check; M365 reuses the stored `m365.app` global config; EWS URL gets `validateEWSServerURL` — same admin-trusted/internal-allowed SSRF posture as the EWS factory) and `POST /admin/migration/start` (body `{source_type, auth_mode, targets[{source_email,target_email,password}], imap_*|ews_*}`; one `migration_jobs` row per target via the same INSERT + `Orchestrator.StartJob` seam as `user.MigrationHandler.StartMigration`; EWS `per_user` targets carry their own creds, `impersonation` sets `ImpersonateAs`; returns 201 `{ids, status:"pending"}`). Both routes `requireMailPipeline`-gated (test-connection makes the server dial an admin-supplied host — write-class, not read). In-package so it reuses connector constructors + factory config machinery unexported
- **Credentials encryption** (`migration/credentials.go`) — AES-GCM encryption for stored connector credentials
- **Orchestrator** (`migration/orchestrator.go`) — Dispatches on `source_type`; merges global + per-job auth; pause/resume/cancel state machine

### Link Protection — `linkprotect/`

- **URL rewriting** (`linkprotect/linkprotect.go`) — `RewriteMessageBody` rewrites HTTP(S) URLs in inbound message bodies to `/link-protect?token=…` (HMAC token embeds the original URL + recipient mailbox_id/message_id)
- **URL un-rewrite (egress, A2)** (`linkprotect/linkprotect.go:UnwrapMessageBody`) — reverses the rewrite on a forwarded copy: regex-finds each `/link-protect?token=…` redirect, verifies the token signature, and substitutes the original URL back. Foreign-secret/tampered/expired tokens are left untouched (never trusted). Used by `smtp/outbound.go` when `forward_unwrap_links` is set — see the Outbound Worker section.
- **Click-time redirect** (`linkprotect/handler.go`) — `GET /link-protect` — validates JWT token, runs scan pipeline, redirects safe or blocks malicious
- **Google Safe Browsing** (`linkprotect/scanner_sb.go`) — Scanner via Safe Browsing API v5
- **Spamhaus scanner** (`linkprotect/scanner_spamhaus.go`) — DBL/HBL URL lookup
- **Scan pipeline** (`linkprotect/scanner.go`) — Ordered scanner chain; first malicious verdict short-circuits; `allErrors` passthrough mode
- **Admin audit** (`admin_link_protect.go`) — `GET /admin/link-protect/audit` — clicked URL history

### S/MIME — `smime/smime.go`

- **Signing** — CMS/PKCS#7 detached signature; RSA/ECDSA private key; multipart/signed construction
- **Encryption** — CMS EnvelopedData; recipient public-key lookup
- **Verification** — CMS SignedData signature check; signer cert extraction
- **Decryption** — EnvelopedData decryption with user private key
- **Cert management** — Upload, list, delete X.509 certs (`auth/me/smime/certs`); admin cert view per user

### MDM & MAM — `mdm/`

- **Device check-in** (`mdm/checkin.go`) — `POST /mdm/checkin` — processes `DeviceReport`; resolves policy; evaluates compliance; returns `CheckinResponse` with wipe command if needed
- **Wipe state machine** (`mdm/engine.go`) — `none → WipePending → WipeAcknowledged → WipeExecuted`; selective (app data) or full (factory reset) wipe levels
- **Compliance evaluation** (`mdm/engine.go`, `mdm/policy.go`) — Checks OS version, app version, encryption, screen-lock, jailbreak; grace period for first violation
- **Device identity** (`mdm/device_identity.go`) — Certificate-based device identity; enrollment token
- **Policy distribution** (`mdm/policy.go`, `mdm/policy_push.go`) — Push policy updates to enrolled devices
- **MAM (Mobile App Management)** (`mdm/mam_policy.go`) — App-level policies: copy/paste restrictions, screenshot prevention, remote app wipe
- **Events** (`mdm/events.go`) — Device event log for audit
- **Registry secret backfill** (`mdm/registry_secret_backfill.go`) — one-shot startup drain (worker pool; wired in `cmd/mailserverd`) sealing legacy plaintext `device_registry.device_secret` into the V260 `device_secret_enc` envelope and NULLing the plaintext; idempotent, non-fatal, retried next boot. The column has no live reader, so this backfill is its only migration path (D6/DBSCHEMA-005)

### SCIM 2.0 — `scim/`

- **Users CRUD** (`scim/user.go`) — `GET/POST/GET/{id}/PUT/{id}/PATCH/{id}/DELETE/{id} /scim/v2/Users` — RFC 7643/7644 user provisioning
- **Groups CRUD** (`scim/group.go`) — `GET/POST/GET/{id}/PATCH/{id}/DELETE/{id} /scim/v2/Groups` — distribution group provisioning
- **Source isolation** — every Group query and mutation includes the authenticated active SCIM source; duplicate external IDs are valid across sources but never mutually visible or mutable
- **Filter parsing** (`scim/filter.go`) — SCIM filter expression → SQL translation
- **PATCH operations** (`scim/patch.go`) — `add`, `remove`, `replace` operations
- **Schemas** (`scim/schemas.go`) — `/scim/v2/Schemas`, `/scim/v2/ServiceProviderConfig`
- **Bearer token auth** (`scim/server.go`) — bcrypt token comparison only against tokens bound to an active SCIM `identity_sources` row; request context carries exact source ID/generation; constant-time compare and background last-used update
- **Deletion policy** — Configurable hard-delete vs soft-delete per `system_config`

### Webhooks — `webhook/`

- **Dispatcher** (`webhook/dispatcher.go`) — Fan-out to registered endpoints; per-endpoint circuit-breaker; HMAC-SHA256 signing; delivery log; one Dispatcher-wide fixed eight-worker executor and eight-item queue owned by `StartRetryWorker(lifecycleCtx)`. Database lease admission reserves only the 16 running+queued slots, ordered by `next_attempt_at,id`; expired leases consume the subscription retry ceiling, and circuit-open attempts settle to a future retry rather than remaining a hot pending row. Shutdown closes admission and drains only the already-admitted bounded set. Generic events are organization-only because they have no authenticated subject; typed user dispatch matches `organization OR exact self`; best-effort saturation logs an explicit drop.
- **Publication worker** (`webhook/publication_worker.go`) — unconditional WorkerPool drain of V211 publication facts. Claims at most 100, performs reducer-owned current subscription fan-out, marks the fact delivered after logical rows are durable, submits bounded immediate HTTP work, and prunes 30-day terminal residue. HTTP remains at-least-once and consumers deduplicate by stable CloudEvent `id`.
- **SSRF guard** — the dispatcher dials via `shared/ssrf.SafeHTTPClient` (post-DNS `net.Dialer.Control` guard: rejects internal/link-local targets incl. cloud-metadata, defeats DNS rebinding). The guard lives in `shared/ssrf` (also used by newsletter unsubscribe, `/api/discover`, and the authserver SAML metadata fetch); the old `webhook/safeguard.go` was removed when it was extracted

### Telemetry — `telemetry/`

- **Privacy-first telemetry** (`telemetry/handler.go`) — `POST /telemetry` — accepts anonymized client telemetry (app version, platform, error codes); no PII; configurable enable/disable (`telemetry/store.go`)

### ACME Certificate Automation — `acme/`

- **DNS-01 challenge** (`acme/dns01.go`) — Certificates are obtained ONLY by the managed background loop (`LegoManager.Start`, called from `mailserverd`'s TLS setup): renew when no cached cert or NotAfter inside a 30-day window; single-flight gate + post-gate recheck; backoff with jitter; ctx-cancellable. **`GetCertificate` is serving-only** (ACME-001/002): cache read + NotAfter check + SNI allowlist (lowercase, trailing-dot trim, wildcard entry match); unknown SNI / cold cache / expired → `(nil, nil)` so the `mailserverd` fallback chain handles the handshake — no handshake (lego OR autocert) may ever issue. The ACME account key persists (KeyFile or `<CacheDir>/account.key`, generate-once) so restarts do not re-register. Offline lifecycle tests: `acme/dns01_lifecycle_test.go` (injectable `now`/`registerFn`/`issueFn`).
- **Multi-provider DNS** (`acme/dns_providers.go`) — Cloudflare, Route53, Gandi, etc.
- **Cache** (`acme/cache.go`) — Disk-based cert cache (autocert-compatible blob format); `Put` is atomic temp+rename — interrupted writes never clobber the previous blob

### Geo/Network — `geoip/`, `network/`

- **DB-IP Lite auto-download** (`geoip/dbip.go`) — Downloads and updates DB-IP Lite MMDB for geo-lookup
- **GeoDNS detection** (`network/geo_detect.go`) — Detects if request came through China CDN/GeoDNS
- **China mirror router** (`network/mirror.go`) — Redirects to domestic mirror when GeoDNS detected

### Cron / Scheduled Tasks — `cron/`

Each task has the transfer name `cron:<Name>` in `GO_WORKERS_OFF`; the scheduler
checks exact, comma-separated, whitespace-trimmed names before reservation.
Disabled tasks produce no SQL effects; all scheduling, single-flight, timeout and
join semantics are unchanged for other tasks. The gateway separately gates the
whole scheduler as `cron`. The launch golden records all task names.

All 31 tasks live in `cron/tasks.go`; `cron/worker.go` validates the fixed registry
before startup and runs them through a clock/ticker plus Exec-only runner seam.
Reservation happens before goroutine launch, so adjacent ticks cannot overlap the
same task. Cancellation stops new admission and joins already-reserved work for at
most the gateway's 30-second shutdown budget; a runner that ignores cancellation
emits `cron_join_timeout` rather than silently extending shutdown.

| Frequency | Task | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| Every 2s | `drain-outbound-hot` | Flush undo-send window; dispatch immediate sends |
| Every 1m | `dispatch-scheduled-actions` | A6 deferred-action dispatcher — claim due `scheduled_actions` (exactly-once) → route to send/snooze/remind handlers. Replaces the legacy process-snoozed/process-scheduled/process-message-timers trio. |
| Every 1m | `scan-due-reminders` | Enqueue `remind` actions for due event-VALARM / task reminder_at / flag-due sources |
| Every 1m | `retry-outbound-queue` | Re-pend due deferred outbound rows without changing the SMTP worker-owned attempt count/backoff (V151; `next_attempt_at=now()`, never NULL) |
| Every 1m | `drain-journal-queue` | Dispatch compliance journal copies |
| Every 1m | `prune-ws-subscriptions` | Remove stale WebSocket subscription rows |
| Every 5m | `health-check` | Aggregate system health snapshot |
| Every 5m | `refresh-antiphish-contact-graph` | Refresh anti-phishing contact materialized view |
| Hourly | `reset-sending-quotas` | Reset per-user per-hour sending quotas |
| Daily 02:05 | `enforce-retention` | Apply MRM retention policies (delete/archive past expiry) |
| Daily 02:30 | `purge-deleted-messages` | Hard-delete soft-deleted messages past retention |
| Daily 02:40 | `chat-chain-anchor` | Chat A4a: `chat_chain_anchor()` — nightly per-channel audit-chain digest + chain-head notary (must run before the purge below so every purgeable row is anchored before it dies) |
| Daily 02:50 | `chat-purge-expired` | Chat A4a: `chat_purge_run()` — drains `chat_purge_expired` within a 90s wall-clock budget (300s `Timeout`); gxp/held channels never selected |
| Daily 03:00 | `prune-dmarc-reports` — various | Prune DMARC reports, spam models, log tables, greylist, health history, vacation reply cache |
| Daily 04:00 | `purge-expired-tokens` | Purge expired auth tokens |
| Weekly | `purge-saml-assertions` | Purge old SAML assertion replay cache |
| Monthly 25th | `create-monthly-partitions` | `create_monthly_partitions(1)` — pre-create current+next month partitions for EVERY month-partitioned table (catalog-derived; same reducer boot calls) |

**No `mark-orphaned-blobs` SQL cron task** (removed — it called `mark_orphaned_blobs()`, a function that was never implemented, and being pure SQL run through `pool.Exec` it could never delete a SeaweedFS/MinIO object anyway). Draining `orphaned_message_blobs` (the raw-message-body sibling of `blob_cleanup_queue`) instead lives in the Go blob worker — see `blob/` below.

### Backup & Restore — `backup/`, `restore/`, `storage/`

- **Backup engine** (`backup/engine.go`) — a `backup_runs` NOTIFY drives `processRun`: CAS-claim via `claimRun` (single-flight; the worker startup-drains stranded `pending` rows via `pendingRunIDs`) → `SnapshotBlobs` (fatal on a partial-read cursor error — `scanBlobRefs` + `rows.Err()`) → `CopyNewBlobs` (HeadObject **size**-skip + post-write size check) → `writeManifests` (incr/config + the `backup-manifest-<ts>.json` index + the `backup-manifest.json` commit-point latest pointer written last; a copy error FAILS the run — never publishes a manifest referencing absent blobs) → async best-effort `replicateToBackup` → `ApplyRetention` (**real GFS tiered** prune of old runs' index objects via `runsToPrune`/`parseGFS`, never blobs; policy from `system_config` `backup.retention_policy`, default `7d4w12m`). **GFS counts are tier depths, not a flat sum:** retain the newest run within each of the most-recent `daily` distinct calendar-days, `weekly` distinct ISO-weeks, and `monthly` distinct calendar-months (parsed from the manifest timestamp); prune only runs in NO retained bucket — so a months-old monthly snapshot survives behind newer runs (the old `applyGFS` flat sum `runs[daily+weekly+monthly:]` kept newest-N-total and silently dropped old monthlies). Design: `docs/design/archive/storage-ha-backup-restore.md` + `docs/design/mail/archive/mailctl-restore-lock-replication.md`
- **Restore engine** (`restore/engine.go`) — verifies the embedded Ed25519 backup manifest and bound config digest; legacy or embedded-only recovery can restore data but `RestoreConfig` delegates every key to V229 `restore_config_apply`. Unknown, encrypted, identity-provider, outbound-endpoint and trust-boundary settings go to `restore_config_quarantine`; only catalogued safe settings auto-apply, while independently verified sources may additionally apply `operator_approve` entries. Blob digest/path protection and cancellation-safe single-flight lifecycle remain unchanged.
- **Storage drivers** (`storage/driver.go`) — `Driver` interface: S3 (`storage/s3_driver.go`), S3-Hot tier (`storage/s3_hot_driver.go`), Aliyun OSS (`storage/oss_driver.go`), POSIX local (`storage/posix_driver.go`)
- **WORM enforcement** (`storage/worm.go`, `startup/worm.go`) — Write-Once Read-Many: object lock, legal hold integration; `WORM_LEGAL_HOLD_ENABLED` env
- **Blob lifecycle retention** (`storage/retention.go`) — Policy-based object expiry on storage driver
- **Storage migration** (`storage/migration.go`) — provider migration (S3→OSS, etc.) with status tracking. Backfill + manual cutover only — the dual-write phase is a **no-op** (no dual-write path in any caller), so it is NOT zero-downtime: writes during the run are not mirrored to the target. Treat as a quiescent-writes / maintenance-window migration
  - Legacy and process-owned status reads project NULL progress counters as zero and omit percentage until total is positive. The admin list uses the same counter semantics and refuses the whole response on row decoding or terminal cursor failure; it never returns a partial successful list.
- **Consumer-defined storage interfaces (ISP)** — the data-mover consumers no longer accept the fat `storage.Driver`; each declares its own narrow, consumer-defined interface holding only the methods it calls, satisfied structurally (zero driver edits) by the consolidated concrete drivers (`s3HotDriver`/`backupS3Driver`/`posixDriver`/`ossDriver`):
  - `backup.BackupStore` (`backup/store.go`) — `HeadObject`/`GetObject`/`PutObject`/`ProviderName` only (the `retentionOps` sub-interface was dropped when WORM was segregated out of `storage.Driver`: WORM is enforced on the primary bucket by `storage.WORMRetentionPolicy` and on the backup bucket by the backup-S3 driver's bucket-level Object Lock + PutObject-time retention, so the backup engine never sets per-object retention itself). `backup.Engine` fields/params are `BackupStore`.
  - `restore.RestoreStore` (`restore/store.go`) — `HeadObject`/`GetObject`/`PutObject`. `restore.Engine` fields/params are `RestoreStore`.
  - `storage.blobMover` (`storage/migration.go`) — `ProviderName`/`HeadObject`/`GetObject`/`PutObject`; the in-package `Migration` orchestrator's `source`/`target` fields + `NewMigration` params.
  - `migration.MigrationStore` (`migration/store.go`) — **declared-contract-only**: the canonical migration storage contract with a compile-time subset assertion, but **not yet consumed** (no migration body holds a driver). Ready target for a future migration. (`blob.BlobStore` — the analogous contract for the blob path — was deleted as dead: the live blob handler + JMAP blob path use `*minio.Client` directly and nothing ever consumed it.)
  - `NewDriver(...)` results are assignable to every consumer interface (each is a subset of `Driver`), so **no caller was edited**. The `storage/isp_assert_test.go` mirror interfaces prove the concrete drivers carry the method sets (storage can't import the consumer packages — cycle — so it mirrors). This is **complementary to** storage-driver-consolidation (which unified the implementations): consolidation narrowed *who implements* storage to one driver set; ISP narrows *who consumes* it. Orthogonal, both correct.

### Deploy & HA — `deploy/`

- **SeaweedFS systemd units** (`deploy/seaweedfs.go`) — Generates volume/master/filer units for systemd
- **HA planner** (`deploy/seaweedfs_ha.go`) — Scale-up/scale-down wizard; replica placement
- **PgBouncer config** (`deploy/pgpool.go`) — Generates PgBouncer config from DB DSN

### Notification / WebSocket — `notify/`, `ws/`

- **LISTEN/NOTIFY dispatcher** (`notify/dispatcher.go`) — Single direct DB connection; routes `pg_notify` channels to registered Go channels
- **WebSocket handler** (`ws/handler.go`) — `GET /jmap/ws`; upgrades HTTP; the Request branch is now transport-only: decode `wsEnvelope` → `JMAPHandler.ExecuteBatch` → encode RFC 8887 `Response` or `RequestError` (including `requestId`). It owns no `using`, limit, reference, `createdIds`, gate, or dispatch loop. The unchanged 10 MiB frame bound remains in the reader. Push resolves the caller's **primary mailbox id** and subscribes to `tag:{mailbox_id}` + `calendar:{mailbox_id}` — never `tag:{user_id}` (ADR-003: `users.id` ≠ `mailboxes.id`) — then emits mailbox-keyed RFC 8887 StateChange objects. The producer↔consumer channel contract lives in `sql/AGENTS.md` → "NOTIFY channel contract"; `resultref_test.go` owns the shared HTTP/WS execution corpus and exact RequestError projection.
- **Welcome email** (`notify/welcome.go`) — Sends localized welcome email to new users via `deliver()` reducer; templates for en/zh/de

### Well-Known & Discovery — `wellknown/`, `discovery/`

- **`/.well-known/setjoin`** — Platform discovery: protocol URLs, capabilities, version
- **`/api/discover`** — Client auto-discovery: SRV → MX → `.well-known` resolution for JMAP, IMAP, SMTP, CalDAV, CardDAV endpoints

### Air-Gap Updates — `updates/`

- **Upload bundle** (`updates/airgap.go`) — `POST /admin/updates/airgap/upload` — stores signed update bundle
- **Appcast** — `GET /updates/appcast` — serves Sparkle/WinSparkle appcast XML for desktop auto-update
- **Asset serving** — `GET /updates/{slug}/{filename}` — serves update binaries from local storage

### Transactional updater — `updater/`, `cmd/setjoin-updater`

The updater is a separate root-owned process, never an admin HTTP handler. The
gateway may authorize and observe V178 intents but cannot execute updater
reducers or write `/var/lib/setjoin-updater`. U1 accepts fixed state/store/
credential paths and a stable executor ID; it has no TCP listener, caller-owned
release path, shell command, or production fault flag. Its systemd unit reads
the NOBYPASSRLS `setjoin_updater` DSN and the distinct owner/recovery secrets
from fixed root-owned `0600` files below `/etc/setjoin-updater` (`0700`), keeps
their values out of argv, environment, and gateway/auth units, and grants an
empty capability set with only the journal writable. The direct-file form is
also deterministic under supported nested-systemd rehearsals where
`LoadCredential` can be silently unavailable. `docs/guides/UPGRADE_RECOVERY.md` is mandatory before any
`recovery_required` result. U2's adapters use only fixed artifact targets,
core units and loopback readiness probes. Signed cohorts carry
`installed.evidence.json`, whose digest covers the exact expanded directory and
file inventory; activation and rollback recompute it. Adopted cohorts recompute
their recorded artifact digests (four before #264, five with the Rust gateway)
and reject extra entries. The adapters are
compiled, fixture-tested, and conditionally installed into the daemon's fixed
step catalog only behind the root-owned execution config, database execution gate,
and verified recovery provider. `internal/deploycatalog` is the compiled
authority for Setjoin-owned unit identity, role, requiredness, start/stop
order, template, destination, renderer, and recovery-observed membership.
`cmd/unit-catalog-gen` produces the checked-in, read-only
`deploy/generated/unit-catalog.sh` projection consumed by the installer,
`setjoinctl`, and deployment-document guard. Never make this catalog
manifest-, request-, or config-selectable; per-unit render functions remain
the typed adapters for their distinct token contracts. Run
`bash scripts/check-deploy-unit-catalog.sh --self-test` after changing a unit.
The #264 fixed core target `core_setjoin_gateway` stages `setjoin-gateway`
beside Go; the catalog starts its capability-empty service after `setjoin`.
Older adopted cohorts without the binary remain valid rollback targets.
Installer/socket contracts and tests: [mailserver DOX](../DOX.md).
`setjoin-recovery`
accepts only `status|evidence|activate-previous`; the mutating verb requires an
authored recovery state or local-TTY `--force --reason`, stops the updater,
acquires the shared local execution lock, writes exclusive root-only evidence,
and never exposes a data-restore or arbitrary-command path. A local rolled-back
event left ahead of PostgreSQL after response loss is explicitly reconciled by
exact reducer replay before the journal becomes reconciled.

Schema 183+ target and rollback manifests carry a signed feature-registry
contract (`catalog_digest`, sorted compatible modes, compatible rollback
digests). The updater reads live mode/digest/unknown count from PostgreSQL and
checks compatibility before any host action, after migration/data-contract
checks, and after rollback. Initial V182→V183 expansion is the only absent-state
preflight. Observe may cross an explicitly signed digest change; enforce may
not. A pre-V183 executable rollback is allowed only after returning the live DB
to observe. Focused proof lives in `feature_registry_contract_test.go` and the
Architecture R2 registry DB lane.
Preflight and post-commit both route through one compatibility loader; only the
reviewed rollback slot (`current` before switching, `previous` after commit)
and phase differ.

### Connectivity-aware sync E1 gateway seams

- `admin/admin_offline.go` adds strict 16 KiB policy bodies and
  `POST /admin/offline/policy-preview`. Old policy bodies call the legacy reducer
  and preserve V209 fields; complete QoS bodies call v2. Preview uses decimal-
  string mailbox/device IDs and authorizes `mail.offline_policy` before resolution.
- `jmap/sync_preferences.go` owns strict 4 KiB
  `PUT /user/offline/sync-preferences`. It resolves the authenticated JWT device
  fingerprint against the caller's user row; JSON cannot select a device. The
  route 404s while the feature or winning policy mode is OFF, calls the canonical
  scoped reducer, and returns refreshed effective capabilities.

### Xinchuang (国产) DB Adapter — `xinchuang/db/`

- **Adapter interface** (`xinchuang/db/adapter.go`) — Common `Adapter` interface: `Open`, `Ping`, `DriverName`, `SupportsNotify`, `ErrorMapper`
- **PostgreSQL adapter** (default) — Standard pgx driver; native `LISTEN` support
- **Domestic DB adapters** — Built with `-tags xinchuang`; maps domestic DB errors to standard ERRCODE equivalents (`errormap.go`)
- **Polling LISTEN fallback** (`xinchuang/db/notify.go`) — Poll-based LISTEN emulation for DBs that lack native NOTIFY

### Supporting Infrastructure

- **`auth/`** — JWT **dual-verify** (ADR-005): HS256 (shared `JWT_SECRET`) AND ES256 first-party tokens against the authserver JWKS — `jwt.go` `Validate` dispatches on header `alg` (ES256 → `jwtutil.ParseES256` + JWKS-resolved key, same iss/aud/exp), `jwks.go` `JWKSClient` (fetch+cache `AUTHSERVER_JWKS_URL` only, refresh-on-miss rate-limited, serve-stale, fail-closed). Plus **SM2** (`smx`/`hybrid` profile, kid `authserver-sm2-1`) verified with the shared `SM2_PRIVATE_KEY_FILE` (symmetric-trust, NOT JWKS-distributed; `chain.go` loads it via `cryptokey.LoadSM2PrivateKey` and passes it to `NewJWTProvider`, fatal-on-error so an SMx gateway never silently rejects every token). Also bcrypt password check, Kerberos/SPNEGO (RFC 4559), Basic auth; priority-chain dispatch (JWT=10, Kerberos=20, Basic=30)
- **`authproxy/`** — Reverse-proxy `/auth/*` to `authserver`; copies request headers; streams response
- **`blob/`** — Content-addressed blob PUT/GET handler; computes SHA-256; stores in SeaweedFS via S3 API. `StartCleanup`'s ticker drains both `blob_cleanup_queue` (attachments) and `orphaned_message_blobs` (raw message bodies)
- **`config/`** — `Config` struct; koanf YAML + env loading; `crypto_reload.go` for DKIM/TLS key re-read on reload
- **`db/`** — `pool.go` pgxpool wrapper; `listen.go` direct NOTIFY connection with reconnect loop
- **`handlerutil/`** — `UpdateBuilder` builds `SET col=$n` clauses for partial update handlers
- **`health/`** — public `/health` preserves only the aggregate `status` and HTTP verdict; detailed check names, values, thresholds, timestamps, and dependency errors are available only through authenticated admin health routes. `/ready` remains the lightweight DB readiness probe.
- **`kql/`** — KQL parser (`parser.go`) + SQL transpiler (`sql.go`) for eDiscovery search queries
- **`ldaputil/`** — RFC 4515 filter parsing, RFC 4514 DN escaping, attribute type normalization
- **`ldapsync/`** — LDAP identity sync: delta pull, attribute mapping, upsert into `users` table
- **`lifecycle/`** — Ordered `Add(name, deps, start, stop)` manager; topological startup/shutdown (not yet wired into `main.go`)
- **`metrics/`** — Prometheus `Handler` with `MetricsAuth` middleware (shared API key). Identity safety collection runs on the worker pool so FORCE-RLS tables remain readable without weakening isolation, stops on context cancellation, marks stale samples with `gateway_identity_collector_success=0`, and emits the canonical `setjoin_identity_*` operator metrics plus compatibility `gateway_identity_*` gauges.
- **`middleware/`** — Auth chain (`RequireAuth` enforces `TypeAccess` JWT + MFA), admin guards, role/perm guards, `RequireApp` / `RequireAppFeatureFirst` (user-id per-app entitlement; Chat/Drive/Meet preserve feature-first 404; fail-closed 403 on DB error), `PoolEntitlementChecker`, per-route rate limiter, security headers (HSTS, CSP, X-Frame-Options)
- **`mimeutil/`** — RFC 2822/2045 parsing: `ParseHeaders`, `ParseAddress`, `DecodeBody`, charset normalization
- **`protocoltest/`** — `Cassette`: record/replay of TCP protocol sessions for regression testing
- **`pst/writer.go`** — Export to Outlook PST; used by mailbox export flow
- **`spam/`** — Per-user FTRL-Proximal online learning (`ftrl.go`); feature extraction (`features.go`); model store (`store.go`); async trainer (`trainer.go`); admin dashboard integration. **`smtp/ml_detectors.go`** (arch review #A5) additively wires 4 of `shared/spam`'s deterministic detectors — `DetectSenderMismatch`/`DetectForgedRecipients`/`DetectMixedCharset`/`DetectFakeHTMLScore` — into `deliver()`'s spam score behind `toggle.FeatureSpamMLDetectors` (DEFAULT OFF, V137); see "Spam scoring" below and `shared/AGENTS.md`'s `spam` row for which of `shared/spam`'s detectors stay unwired and why.
- **`startup/`** — `minio.go` SeaweedFS/S3 bootstrap; `partitions.go` `EnsureMonthlyPartitions` = thin `SELECT create_monthly_partitions(1)` on the owner pool (current+next month for every month-partitioned table, catalog-derived); `report.go` startup recovery report; `secrets.go` auto-generates `JWT_SECRET`/`S3_ACCESS_KEY` if absent; `worm.go` WORM object-lock init
- **`telemetry/`** — `POST /telemetry` ingest + store; configurable per-deployment opt-in
- **`testutil/`** — `docker.go` Docker-based test DB setup; `SkipIfNoEnv` for e2e skipping; `routeinventory.go` validates real `ServeMux` patterns/conflicts and atomically emits package-owner route evidence without serving requests
- **`tlsutil/`** — `BuildTLSConfig` (TLS 1.2+ min, cipher selection); `LimitListener` wraps net.Listener with semaphore; `CertMonitor` checks the selected in-memory certificate chain and records expiry health. TLS worker oracle recorders: [worker oracle](../../server-rust/DOX.md#worker-oracle).
- **`webdav/`** — WebDAV `PROPFIND`, `MKCOL`, `COPY`, `MOVE`, `LOCK`, `UNLOCK` base handler shared by CalDAV and CardDAV
- **`wellknown/`** — Serves `/.well-known/setjoin` discovery JSON

## Work Guidance

All project-wide rules from root `AGENTS.md` apply. Additional constraints for this directory:

- **Route additions**: register in `main.go` only; no side-effect init in package `init()`. Every new route needs auth middleware (`requireAuth`, `requireAdmin`, etc.).
- **Guestgate path normalization**: normalize only a real `/api/` mount segment; malformed textual prefixes such as `/apiauth/` remain untouched and denied. `root-prefix` rules deliberately match the original bare-root path instead.
- **New packages**: update this document's Package index; update parent/child rails only for changed ownership or routing.
- **Feature toggles**: new toggleable features need a `feature_toggles` DB row seeded in a migration and a `feature.X` key passed via `setIfDisabled` or `toggles.IsEnabled("feature.X")`. Toggle reads are cheap; no caching needed.
- **Federation dynamic listener**: when static Federation certificate/domain
  config is present, `Service.Run` starts at boot even if
  `feature.federation` is OFF. Its accept loop must remain dormant and
  cancellation-responsive while OFF, then enter `Listen` after a live toggle
  enable. `ErrFederationDisabled` is a retry state, never permission for the
  goroutine to exit permanently.
- **Cron tasks**: add to `cron/tasks.go` `AllTasks()`. Do not use pg_cron; the in-process worker manages all scheduling.
- **Admin handlers**: one file per domain area in `admin/`, following the `admin_<domain>.go` naming convention. Use `handlerutil.UpdateBuilder` for PATCH-style partial updates.
- **Protocol compliance**: before modifying SMTP/IMAP/CalDAV/CardDAV/LDAP handlers, read `docs/ref/protocols/<protocol>.md` and the relevant RFC.
- **DB security rules R1–R4** (see root AGENTS.md and `docs/standards/DATABASE_SECURITY.md`) apply to every query in every handler. No exceptions.
- **Error responses**: use `shared/httputil.WriteProblem` or the constructors (`NewBadRequest`, etc.) — never `err.Error()` in responses, never swallow errors silently.
- **S/MIME outbound signing**: Phase D moved signing from the outbound worker into the `EmailSubmission/set` Go handler (`jmap/mail_smime.go` — `signSubmissions`/`signOneSubmission`). The handler signs before the reducer call (before `send()` splits local vs external delivery) so both audiences receive the same signed blob. The outbound worker (`smtp/outbound.go`) no longer signs. **S/MIME encryption** (`encryptSMIME`, recipient-keyed) was likewise re-homed to the `EmailSubmission/set` handler by SP1 Phase 3 (`jmap/mail_encrypt.go`) — it no longer lives in the worker. It is **fail-closed**: encrypt-requested mail with any missing recipient/sender/journal cert is dropped into per-creation `notCreated` (never sent, never plaintext); SP1 Phase 4 names every missing recipient so the compose UX can list them. See "S/MIME encryption (SP1 Phase 3 …)" above.
- **JMAP vendor extensions**: all nonstandard properties/capabilities must be declared under `urn:setjoin:params:jmap:mail` (`jmap/handler.go:VendorCapability`). Document in `docs/specs/JMAP.md`.

## Verification

```bash
# Full gateway build
cd mailserver && go build -o mailserverd ./cmd/mailserverd

# Gateway unit tests
cd mailserver && go test ./internal/...

# Linter (must be clean before commit)
cd mailserver && golangci-lint run ./...

# E2E protocol tests (requires running stack)
bash scripts/e2e.sh imap
bash scripts/e2e.sh smtp
bash scripts/e2e.sh ldap

# Specific protocol suites
cd mailserver && go test ./internal/smtp/... -v
cd mailserver && go test ./internal/imap/... -v
cd mailserver && go test ./internal/jmap/... -v
cd mailserver && go test ./internal/caldav/... -v
cd mailserver && go test ./internal/scim/... -v
cd mailserver && go test ./internal/admin/... -v

# Reducer-affecting changes: use affected pgTAP cases on the verified disposable
# test DB plus required scope guards; full-suite execution follows the lane contract.
# Full-suite example, only after confirming mailserver_test is the owned test DB:
pg_prove -d mailserver_test mailserver/sql/tests/*.pg

# EWS e2e (requires TEST_EWS_CONFIG)
cd mailserver && TEST_EWS_CONFIG=/path/to/ews.json \
  go test -tags e2e -run TestEWSRealServer -v -count=1 -timeout 300s ./internal/migration/...

# M365 e2e (requires TEST_M365_CONFIG)
cd mailserver && TEST_M365_CONFIG=/path/to/m365.json \
  go test -tags e2e -run TestM365RealServer -v -count=1 -timeout 300s ./internal/migration/...
```

- `meet/AGENTS.md` — Meet Pillar B1 (`internal/meet/`): the three spike laws (eviction-not-TTL, webhooks-are-facts, one-secret-one-generation-step), the org-open member-join posture + kick-as-ban-until-re-admit (both gate-ratified), the grant-spec contract (reducers compose, Go only translates), the BZ185–190 map, the `RoomController`/`fakeRooms` SFU test seam, what the Task 18 gate did/did not exercise (incl. the live-SFU-smoke Docker substitution, the real `/rtc` `apiPinnedPrefixes` bug found+fixed, and the fresh-VM leg 3 that is NOT run — an open merge precondition), and the B1→B2/B3/guest-accounts deferral registry.

contract](../../server-rust/crates/protocols/ldap/DOX.md) owns replay instructions.
